**[00:04:19.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000052)  
Honorable members, distinguished colleagues and esteemed guests, good afternoon.

**[00:04:25.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000053)  
We welcome our guests from the Department of Justice,

**[00:04:28.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000054)  
led by Secretary Fredderick A. Vida,

**[00:04:30.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000055)  
together with the heads of its attached agencies.

**[00:04:33.480] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000056)  
Today, we deliberate on the proposed

**[00:04:35.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000057)  
budget of the Department of Justice

**[00:04:37.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000058)  
and its attached agencies for fiscal year 2027

**[00:04:41.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000059)  
with a total obligation budget of 46.435 billion pesos,

**[00:04:47.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000060)  
inclusive of automatic appropriations.

**[00:04:50.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000061)  
This represents only 0.68% of the 7.2 trillion peso national budget

**[00:04:56.560] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000062)  
and is about 2% higher than the previous budget.

**[00:05:01.840] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000063)  
In 2025, the DOJ recorded important operational gains.

**[00:05:05.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000064)  
The National Prosecution Service handled 428,779 cases and disposed or resolved 395,780, a 99.12% disposition rate, while the prosecution success rate improved to 90.84%. The NBI also conducted 56,212 investigations

**[00:05:31.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000065)  
and processed 6.62 million clearance applications.

**[00:05:36.320] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000066)  
While the PCGG reported about 1.57 billion pesos in recoveries

**[00:05:41.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000067)  
and income from surrendered or sequestered assets.

**[00:05:46.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000068)  
These gains, however, must be sustained amid significant resource pressures.

**[00:05:51.840] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000069)  
Under the fiscal year 2027 NEP,

**[00:05:54.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000070)  
the Bureau of Immigration budget is reduced by 35.8%,

**[00:05:58.840] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000071)  
the NBI by 22.3%,

**[00:06:01.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000072)  
and the Office for Alternative Dispute Resolution,

**[00:06:05.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000073)  
by 16.3%.

**[00:06:07.220] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000074)  
In DOJ OSEC,

**[00:06:10.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000075)  
funding for the

**[00:06:11.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000076)  
Special Enforcement and Protection Subprogram

**[00:06:14.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000077)  
is reduced by almost half,

**[00:06:17.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000078)  
including lower allocations for anti-trafficking,

**[00:06:19.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000079)  
anti-cybercrime,

**[00:06:21.420] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000080)  
and OSAEC-CSAEM.

**[00:06:23.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000081)  
The corrections program also faces a 21.05% reduction,

**[00:06:28.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000082)  
including a cut in victims' compensation services

**[00:06:31.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000083)  
from 19 million to 8 million pesos.

**[00:06:35.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000084)  
At the same time,

**[00:06:36.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000085)  
68.32% of the Department's

**[00:06:40.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000086)  
fiscal year 2027 budget is for

**[00:06:42.780] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000087)  
personal services,

**[00:06:44.500] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000088)  
while leaving 24.42% for MOOE

**[00:06:48.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000089)  
and only 7.26% for capital outlay.

**[00:06:53.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000090)  
The Committee must therefore determine

**[00:06:54.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000091)  
whether the proposed budget can preserve

**[00:06:56.920] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000092)  
the DOJ's 2025 gains

**[00:06:58.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000093)  
while addressing heavy caseloads,

**[00:07:02.920] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000094)  
prison decongestion,

**[00:07:04.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000095)  
border control,

**[00:07:05.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000096)  
investigative capacity,

**[00:07:06.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000097)  
cybercrime and trafficking,

**[00:07:08.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000098)  
victim compensation,

**[00:07:10.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000099)  
and access to justice.

**[00:07:11.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000100)  
We look forward to the Department's

**[00:07:12.760] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000101)  
clarification of these challenges,

**[00:07:14.240] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000102)  
its priorities for fiscal year 2027,

**[00:07:17.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000103)  
and the measurable results

**[00:07:18.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000104)  
it expects to deliver

**[00:07:20.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000105)  
with the resources proposed.

**[00:07:23.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000106)  
Thank you po.

Secretary Vida,

**[00:09:16.990] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000157)  
The Department's overarching framework

**[00:09:19.510] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000158)  
is defined by its core mandate

**[00:09:21.850] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000159)  
to uphold the rule of law

**[00:09:23.630] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000160)  
and serve as the Department's principal law agency.

**[00:09:27.330] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000161)  
In pursuit of its vision

**[00:09:28.870] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000162)  
to foster a just and peaceful society

**[00:09:31.810] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000163)  
rooted in transparency,

**[00:09:33.970] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000164)  
accountability,

**[00:09:35.210] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000165)  
fairness, and truth,

**[00:09:36.190] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000166)  
the Department executes its mission,

**[00:09:38.790] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000167)  
by delivering an effective,

**[00:09:41.150] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000168)  
efficient,

**[00:09:42.150] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000169)  
and equitable administration of justice nationwide.

**[00:09:45.790] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000170)  
The Department

**[00:09:46.590] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000171)  
administers the criminal justice system

**[00:09:49.270] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000172)  
through the investigation of crimes,

**[00:09:51.870] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000173)  
the prosecution of offenders,

**[00:09:53.290] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000174)  
and the administration of the correctional system.

**[00:09:57.130] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000175)  
Beyond criminal justice,

**[00:09:59.030] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000176)  
the Department also maintains oversight

**[00:10:01.850] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000177)  
over immigration,

**[00:10:03.710] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000178)  
citizenship,

**[00:10:04.710] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000179)  
and land titling system.

**[00:10:06.870] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000180)  
Above all,

**[00:10:08.070] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000181)  
the Department,

**[00:10:08.790] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000182)  
guarantees social justice

**[00:10:10.390] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000183)  
by extending free legal assistance

**[00:10:12.690] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000184)  
to our most vulnerable

**[00:10:14.150] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000185)  
indigent citizens.

**[00:10:16.350] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000186)  
In the next few slides, we will highlight

**[00:10:18.570] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000187)  
some of the key operational accomplishments

**[00:10:20.550] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000188)  
of the Department.

**[00:10:22.350] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000189)  
The Department, through the National Prosecution

**[00:10:24.630] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000190)  
Service, or NPS,

**[00:10:26.770] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000191)  
consistently demonstrated

**[00:10:28.730] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000192)  
enhanced prosecutorial output

**[00:10:30.870] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000193)  
and case management efficiency

**[00:10:33.010] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000194)  
over the past five years.

**[00:10:35.670] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000195)  
More significantly,

68.32 percent is for personal services, including salaries and other compensation of employees.

**[00:20:32.440] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000380)  
Only 24.42 percent is programmed for maintenance and other operating expenses, while 7.26 percent

**[00:20:41.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000381)  
accounts for the capital outlay of select agencies of the department. The slight increase in the

**[00:20:48.120] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000382)  
overall NEP budget of the DOJ is attributed mainly to the increased funding requirements

**[00:20:54.140] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000383)  
for personal salaries and other compensation. For 2027, the department-wide budget proposal

**[00:21:03.180] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000384)  
amounts to 121.63 billion, but the total budget recommended in the 2027 NEP is only 46.35 billion,

**[00:21:16.660] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000385)  
or only 38.18 percent of the submitted proposed budget.

**[00:21:24.140] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000386)  
This slide shows our comparative budget for 2026 and 2027 by allotment class in millions pesos.

**[00:21:33.900] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000387)  
Details of these figures will be shown later. With the permission of the honorable members

**[00:21:39.120] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000388)  
of the committee, please allow me to present the DOJ OSSEC budget in the next several slides.

**[00:21:47.590] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000389)  
For 2027, the Office of the Secretary is provided with a total budget of 13.93 billion,

**[00:21:55.710] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000392)  
wherein 70.67% is for PS, 12.47% for MOOE,

**[00:22:01.430] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000393)  
and 16.86% for CO.

**[00:22:04.770] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000394)  
For our law enforcement program, the budget of the National Prosecution Service

**[00:22:09.350] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000395)  
increased by 7.64%, on the contrary, with substantial reductions in the budgets for

**[00:22:16.710] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000396)  
anti-trafficking in persons, anti-cyber crime, and OSEC-CISCM. The budget for special enforcement and

**[00:22:24.670] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000397)  
sub-program is reduced by almost half in 2027 at 204 million compared to the

**[00:22:31.690] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000398)  
current appropriations of 402 million. For 2027, the target disposition rate of

**[00:22:37.990] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000399)  
criminal complaints handled by the NPS is set at 97 percent. The

**[00:22:43.390] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000400)  
Department, through the Witness Protection Program, aims to act on all

**[00:22:47.050] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000401)  
applications for witness coverage and that 95.34 percent of

**[00:22:52.210] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000402)  
investigations undertaken by special enforcement units must be completed. The

**[00:22:58.450] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000403)  
total recommended appropriations for our corrections program in 2027 is reduced

**[00:23:04.090] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000404)  
by 21.05 percent. Compared to our current year's approved funding, the

**[00:23:10.270] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000405)  
recommended budget for our pardons and parole services is slightly reduced by

**[00:23:15.610] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000406)  
2.63 percent at 37 million. On the other hand, the recommended budget for victim

**[00:23:22.190] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000407)  
compensation service is cut by more than half from 19 million in 2026 to 8

**[00:23:29.090] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000408)  
million in 2027. With significantly reduced budgets for next year, the Board

**[00:23:35.310] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000409)  
of Pardons and Parole will ensure that 99.72 percent of appropriations for

**[00:23:41.030] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000410)  
parole and executive clemency are acted upon and that 100 percent of board

**[00:23:46.070] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000411)  
resolutions on the grant of parole and executive clemency are issued within the

**[00:23:50.930] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000412)  
prescribed period.

**[00:23:52.190] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000413)  
Moreover, the Board of Claims will have to act upon all applications for victim

**[00:23:56.570] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000414)  
compensation next year. For 2027, the legal staff shall ensure that 97.07

**[00:24:03.950] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000415)  
percent of requests for legal services are acted upon with a working budget of

**[00:24:09.590] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000416)  
205 million, which is 4.59 percent higher than the 2026 budget. For attached and

**[00:24:17.090] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000417)  
constituent agencies, we start with the Bureau of Corrections.

**[00:24:21.650] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000418)

**[00:24:29.510] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000419)  
percent higher than the appropriations of the current year. On average, the

**[00:24:34.790] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000420)  
Bucor is committed to maintaining safekeep around 56,454 PDLs daily and to ensure that 98.13 percent of PDLs participate in their rehabilitation programs. For the Bureau of Immigration, their 2027 budget is

**[00:24:51.650] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000421)  
recommended budget is 2.98

**[00:24:54.210] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000422)  
billion, which is 35.8

**[00:24:57.450] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000423)  
percent lower than the 2026 budget. Given this, the BI is set to clear the

**[00:25:03.870] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000424)  
arrival or departure of 99.99 percent of foreign passengers.

**[00:25:09.210] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000425)  
Further, the BI needs to dispose, within 60 days, 95.91 percent of their

**[00:25:16.490] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000426)  
intelligence cases.

**[00:25:19.510] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000427)  
For the Land Registration Authority,

**[00:25:20.930] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000428)  
Authority, the 2027 recommended budget amounts to 3.28 billion, which is 13.70 percent higher

**[00:25:30.450] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000429)  
than the 2.88 billion budget in 2026. Given this, the LRA will be able to provide land

**[00:25:37.570] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000430)

**[00:25:45.130] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000431)  
will be issued and 95.64 percent of deeds are annotated within 20 days after the submission

**[00:25:52.370] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000432)  
of complete documents. For the National Bureau of Investigation, their 2027 recommended budget

**[00:25:58.610] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000433)  
is 22.34 percent lower at 3.03 billion from 3.9 billion in 2026. For that said 2027 budget,

**[00:26:10.390] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000434)  
the NBI should be able to process more than 6.6 million clearance

**[00:26:15.110] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000435)  
applications, of which at least 98 percent should be issued within 10 minutes from receipt of

**[00:26:20.450] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000436)  
requests. The NBI committed to act upon the conduct and conduct at least 50,000 investigations

**[00:26:29.370] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000437)  
next year. On behalf of the Government Corporate Council, the 2027 recommended budget amounts

**[00:26:36.830] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000438)  
to 310 million, which is 2.21 percent lower than the 316 million budget in 2026. For the

**[00:26:45.110] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000439)

**[00:26:51.870] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000440)  
within the prescribed period and that 82 percent of all contracts will be reviewed and legal

**[00:26:58.110] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000441)  
opinions will be rendered within the prescribed period. For the Office of the Solicitor General,

**[00:27:04.290] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000442)  
the 2027 recommended budget is increased to 2.14 billion from 1.9 billion this year, with

**[00:27:11.270] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000443)  
a positive variance of 13.15 percent in their budget.

**[00:27:15.110] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000444)  
The OSG aims to act upon at least 98 percent of referred cases in 2027, out of which 99

**[00:27:22.450] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000445)  
percent are acted upon within 30 days. For resolution of petitions for naturalization,

**[00:27:28.710] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000446)  
the OSG intends to resolve 100 percent by the end of the year. For the PPA, the 2027

**[00:27:37.190] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000447)

**[00:27:43.590] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000448)

**[00:27:46.360] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000450)  
With their 2027 NEP-based budget, PPA will ensure that 98.96 percent of clients will

**[00:27:53.970] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000451)  
be compliant with the terms of their probation and or parole conditions, and that 98.69 percent

**[00:28:03.010] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000452)

**[00:28:06.810] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000453)  
For the Presidential Commission on Good Government, their recommended budget under the 2027 NEP

**[00:28:13.190] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000454)

**[00:28:15.110] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000455)  
which is 9.35 percent higher than their current-year appropriations of 310 million. For 2027, the PCGG targets at least 846 million of surrendered or sequestered assets to be submitted to the Bureau of the Treasury. For the Public Attorney's Office, its 2027 recommended budget amounts to 7.101 billion, which is

**[00:28:40.730] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000456)  
6.10 percent higher

**[00:28:43.320] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000457)  
than its 6.693

**[00:28:46.160] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000458)

**[00:28:48.880] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000459)  
With this recommended budget, the PAO will be able to achieve 83.05 percent

**[00:28:54.480] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000460)

**[00:29:02.120] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000461)  
The office will also ensure that all requests for nonjudicial services in 2027 will be acted upon within two hours.

**[00:29:10.560] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000462)  
Lastly, for the Office for Alternative Dispute Resolution, the

**[00:29:15.880] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000463)  
2027 recommended budget amounts to 139 million, which is 16.27 percent lower than its current year's appropriation.

**[00:29:26.550] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000464)  
Despite the reduced funding, the OADR will ensure that 1,880 ADR practitioners and implementers will be trained

**[00:29:36.510] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000465)  
and 100% of applications for accreditation will be acted upon in 2027.

**[00:29:42.350] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000466)  
This concludes our presentation of the 2027 department-wide budget.

**[00:29:48.630] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000467)

**[00:29:57.090] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000468)

**[00:30:00.000] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000469)

**[00:30:06.620] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000470)

**[00:30:13.120] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000471)

**[00:30:18.120] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000472)

**[00:30:24.460] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000473)

**[00:36:22.880] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-000566)  
uh on Wednesday a group from the Philippines will go also to

**[00:36:39.920] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-000569)

**[00:36:45.680] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-000570)

**[00:36:51.000] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-000571)

**[00:37:02.440] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-000573)

**[00:37:08.000] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-000574)

**[00:38:57.674] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000598)

**[00:38:59.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000599)  
Chair, at gusto ko lamang pong ma-manifest dito na kaming

**[00:39:04.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000600)

**[00:39:07.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000601)

**[00:39:13.599] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000603)

**[00:39:16.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000604)

**[00:39:20.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000605)

**[00:39:24.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000606)

**[00:39:26.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000607)

**[00:39:30.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000608)

**[00:39:42.839] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000612)

**[00:39:46.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000613)  
Nagkaroon po kami ng consultations patungkol sa

**[00:39:49.359] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000614)

**[00:39:57.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000616)  
Um, may mga reklamo ang ating mga

**[00:39:58.839] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000617)  
IPs at ICCs na nagkaroon ng

**[00:40:02.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000618)

**[00:40:05.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000619)

**[00:40:10.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000620)  
Um, kahit sa mga

**[00:40:11.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000621)

**[00:40:15.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000622)

**[00:40:18.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000623)

**[00:40:20.839] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000624)

**[00:40:23.839] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000625)  
Um ano ang konkretong hakbang o programa ng

**[00:40:27.839] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000626)

**[00:40:33.524] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000627)  
With your permission, may I ask Administrator Jerry Sirios to respond?

**[00:40:39.363] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000628)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat, Mr. Chair.

**[00:40:43.359] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000629)  
Kami po sa

**[00:40:47.119] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000630)  
LRA ay kamakailan ay nakipag-ugnayan po

**[00:40:51.240] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000631)  
sa National Commission on Indigenous Peoples

**[00:40:55.000] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000632)  
sa pangunguna ni Chairperson Catamco, and

**[00:40:59.359] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000633)  
uh kasama rin nilang nagpunta sa amin nung nakaraang

**[00:41:02.920] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000634)  
linggo ay ang counterpart nila sa BARMM po.

**[00:41:07.800] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000635)

**[00:41:12.839] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000636)  
So kami po ay nakikipag-ugnayan ngayon

**[00:41:15.040] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000637)  
at kami po ay bumabalangkas ng isang memorandum

**[00:41:18.760] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000638)

**[00:41:22.680] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000639)  
ng mga ini-issue na certificate of ancestral domain

**[00:41:26.480] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000640)  
title and certificate of ancestral land

**[00:41:30.720] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000641)  
title sa nasasakupang lugar po nila.

**[00:41:36.417] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000642)  
Paano naman po kapag hindi sa BARMM? Other

**[00:41:40.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000643)

**[00:41:46.053] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000644)  
Opo. Kami rin po ay nakikipag-ugnayan

**[00:41:50.640] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000645)  
sa, tulad ng nabanggit ko po,

**[00:41:54.319] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000646)  
NCIP and also sa DENR and

**[00:41:58.720] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000647)  
sa LMB po upang maiwasan ang overlapping.

**[00:42:03.640] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000648)  
Ang LRA through the Registry of Deeds ay

**[00:42:07.480] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000649)  
ang huli po sa proseso ng ah issuance ng title.

**[00:42:12.240] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000650)  
Sa amin lang po inirerehistro.

**[00:42:14.480] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000651)  
But ang issuance po ng title ay nanggagaling po sa tatlong tanggapan po.

**[00:42:21.119] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000652)  
Una, ang DENR sa pamamagitan ng LMB.

**[00:42:24.240] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000653)  
Nag-i-issue po ng patents. Pangalawa, sa DAR

**[00:42:28.720] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000654)

**[00:42:34.400] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000655)  
And pangatlo po yung NCIP tulad ng

**[00:42:37.240] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000656)  
nabanggit ko kanina po at ang ikaapat ang judicial titling process.

**[00:42:41.680] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000657)  
kung saan dumadaan sa ating mga korte at kami ay dina-direct upang makipag

**[00:42:46.079] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000658)  
ah makapag-issue ng isang decree at ng titulo.

**[00:42:49.760] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000659)  
Pero sa lahat ng proseso po ng pagtitulo, ang LRA po sa

**[00:42:54.480] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000660)  
pamamagitan ng RD ang huling ahensya po na humahawak sa titulo.

**[00:42:59.408] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000661)  
Sige follow up question po Mr. Chair.

**[00:43:02.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000662)

**[00:43:08.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000663)

**[00:43:17.127] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000664)  
Sa kasalukuyan po, kami po ay nakikipag-ugnayan tulad ng nabanggit ko. Pero ang solusyon po nito ay ang

**[00:43:23.760] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000665)  
pagkakaroon po ng mga ah applications, programs na makakatulong upang maiwasan ang overlapping.

**[00:43:30.720] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000666)  
Sa kasalukuyan, mayroon po tayong ine-evaluate na public-private partnership, subalit

**[00:43:37.920] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000667)

**[00:43:44.680] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000668)

**[00:43:51.760] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000669)

**[00:43:59.348] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000670)  
Sige po, Mr. Chair.

**[00:44:00.599] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000671)

**[00:44:03.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000672)

**[00:44:04.715] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000673)

**[00:44:10.640] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000676)  
Ito po ay interagency issuance po.

**[00:44:14.200] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000677)  
Patungkol po ito, kung hindi po ako nagkakamali, dito po

**[00:44:18.920] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000678)  
sa DAR sa DAR kung saan

**[00:44:22.520] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000679)  
nagkaroon ng ah

**[00:44:25.960] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000680)  
ugnayan para makapag-issue po ng mga

**[00:44:29.800] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000681)

**[00:44:36.085] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000682)

**[00:44:45.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000683)

**[00:44:55.319] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000684)  
Opo.

**[00:44:55.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000685)  
Okay. Now, follow-up question po. Ano po iyong ginagawa ninyo kasalukuyan diyan bilang parte po kayo ng JAO para masiguro na hindi na nagkakaroon ng overlapping dahil itong tatlong ahensya ay hindi nagkakasundo?

**[00:45:10.848] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000686)

**[00:45:25.359] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-000687)

**[00:45:43.298] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000688)

Next in line from the majority, Representative J.P. Padernos.

**[00:48:00.430] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000709)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat sa ating Department of Justice,

**[00:48:05.900] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000710)  
headed by our Secretary Vida, and all attached agencies.

**[00:48:11.370] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000711)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[00:48:15.910] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000712)  
Mr. Chair, magtatanong lang po ako per agency para mas concrete.

**[00:48:22.070] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000713)  
I'll start with the Bureau of Corrections.

**[00:48:24.280] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000714)  
Is DG Katapang here?

**[00:48:27.060] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000715)  
Ayan.

**[00:48:27.220] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000716)  
I want to discuss yung facility decongestion and regionalization program ng DOJ.

**[00:48:37.630] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000717)  
Pangangailangan po kasi ng BUCOR ang humigit kumulang 55 billion para sa regional facilities,

**[00:48:44.520] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000718)  
pero 676 million lang po ang capital outlay nito habang nasa 387% ang overcrowding sa NBP.

**[00:48:54.610] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000719)  
Sa ganitong pondo, paano po?

**[00:48:56.840] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000720)  
Maabot ang target na full closure ng NBP by 2028?

**[00:49:01.900] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000721)  
Mr. Chair, Congressman Padernos,

**[00:49:07.740] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000722)  
nabigyan naman po kami ng pondo ng ating gobyerno.

**[00:49:12.020] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000723)  
Pag pinagsama-sama, simula ng 2022 hanggang ngayon,

**[00:49:16.330] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000724)  
sama pa rin yung hopefully maipasa po yung 472 capital outlay for the...

**[00:49:26.910] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000725)  
maximum security sa [unclear].

**[00:49:30.090] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000726)  
Pag sinama-sama po yun, more than 5 billion po yun.

**[00:49:34.000] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000727)  
At ang capacity po na mangyayari na madadagdag is 11,390 additional PDL capacity.

**[00:49:43.800] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000728)  
So itong 2025, nadagdagan tayo ng capacity na 1,000.

**[00:49:52.120] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000729)  
2026, 2005.

**[00:49:55.070] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000730)  
2027, 6,000.

**[00:49:56.890] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000731)  
210.

**[00:49:58.070] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000732)  
And 2028, 1,600.

**[00:50:01.910] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000733)  
Alam po namin na hindi po kayang ibigay lahat ang kailangan namin para lang ma-address yung decongestion.

**[00:50:10.670] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000734)  
Pero ang maganda pong nangyayari, nire-repair po namin yung mga sira naming kulungan at nakakatulong po.

**[00:50:20.100] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000735)  
Ang report po na sinasite nyo is yung last year pa po yan.

**[00:50:23.760] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000736)  
Pero ngayon, sa NBP po,

**[00:50:28.860] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000737)  
from 350%, ngayon po, 180% na lang ang congestion.

**[00:50:34.740] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000738)  
So, kaya nga po,

**[00:50:36.990] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000739)  
iniimbitahan namin kayo para makita nyo kung saan na pupunta yung sinusuporta nyo sa amin.

**[00:50:42.990] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000740)  
And surely, we will also look for other ways.

**[00:50:47.410] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000741)  
Yung aming property po dito sa New Bilibid Prison,

**[00:50:50.670] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000742)  
sa Muntinlupa, humihingi po kami ng permission na ipa-lease na.

**[00:50:58.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000744)  
Kasi po, yung Muntinlupa po, it should be repurposed.

**[00:51:06.520] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000745)  
Napaikutan na po kami ng mga first-class subdivision.

**[00:51:10.440] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000746)  
So, ang inisip namin ay gamitin ito for better use para tumakbo ang economy.

**[00:51:17.710] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000747)  
And hopefully, may mga targets kaming ine-expect na kung mapapa-lease namin ito for the next five years, makaka-generate kami ng 8 billion.

**[00:51:28.830] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000749)  
So, yung po, ang ginagawa namin.

**[00:51:32.790] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000750)  
Alam po namin na hindi kaya ng ating gobyerno ibigay lahat ang kailangan ng Bucor.

**[00:51:38.200] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000751)  
Pero, I think we are doing our best.

**[00:51:41.580] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000752)  
For the past four years, we have already turned around the congestion problem in Bucor.

**[00:51:49.590] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000753)  
Mr. Chair, napakagandang balita po niyan.

**[00:51:52.580] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000754)  
So, can you repeat lang, DG Katapang?

**[00:51:56.350] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000755)  
Yung from...

**[00:51:58.330] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000756)  
Ilan po?

**[00:51:58.800] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000757)  
From 387% overcrowding, nasa 187 na lang po?

**[00:52:04.620] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000758)  
Opo, sa NBP po.

**[00:52:06.280] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000759)  
Kasi, kailangan po talagang dahan-dahanin ko na ang paglipat.

**[00:52:11.050] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000760)  
So, yung mga napagawa naman natin na outside Metro Manila, doon po sila nililipat.

**[00:52:18.190] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000761)  
So, but overall, sa buong pagsamasamahin lahat yung ating mga kulungan, 204 na lang po ang congestion rate.

**[00:52:27.400] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000762)  
Sana lang po, Mr.

**[00:52:28.520] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000763)  
Mr. Chair, tulad po nang nabanggit ko last year sa ating budget deliberation,

**[00:52:33.810] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000764)  
let's keep in mind lang po sana yung mga pamilya na siyempre karamihan naman po ng mga nade-detain is from NCR.

**[00:52:45.120] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000765)  
So, sana po hindi sila ma-displace in other parts of the country, especially yung sa Palawan po.

**[00:52:51.790] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000766)  
Diba, papunta na po doon yung main facility.

**[00:52:54.990] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000767)  
Kamakailan po, binigyan po kami ni Governor Umali

**[00:52:58.560] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000768)  
dito po sa Nueva Ecija

**[00:53:02.440] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000769)  
ng 60 hectares. Doon po namin ililipat yung reception and diagnostic center.

**[00:53:13.030] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000770)  
At yung po rin magiging mga tiga Metro Manila, north of Metro Manila, yung po ang aming target na magiging kulungan nila.

**[00:53:22.460] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000771)  
And then south of Metro Manila, meron po kami tinitignan na property.

**[00:53:26.340] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000772)  
Magsaswapping po kami ng isang institusyon.

**[00:53:30.610] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000773)  
May lupa raw sila sa Laguna ng mga 8,000 hectares na hindi naman nila masyadong ginagamit.

**[00:53:37.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000774)  
And in exchange, bibigyan po namin sila ng 500 hectares sa Palawan para itayo yung kanilang eskwelahan sa Palawan.

**[00:53:45.460] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000775)  
So, pag nangyari po yun, lahat ng tiga Luzon ay hindi na maililipat sa outside Luzon.

**[00:53:53.330] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000776)  
Yung mga tiga Luzon, sa Luzon ang makakukulong.

**[00:53:55.570] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000777)  
And then lately rin po, yung nakita rin namin na mas marami pa lang tiga Cebu ang mga nakukulong sa Bisaya.

**[00:54:04.680] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000778)  
So, we are also coordinating with the governor, the good governor, na bigyan din po kami ng kulungan doon sa lugar

**[00:54:12.590] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000779)  
para yung tuloy-tuloy po natin magiging regionalization yung ating mga PDL.

**[00:54:20.340] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-000780)  
Salamat po.

**[00:54:21.090] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000781)  
Maraming salamat po, DG Katapang.

**[00:54:22.990] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000782)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:54:23.590] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000783)  
Napansin ko po doon sa report kanina ng ating department, mostly yung kanilang performance indicators,

**[00:54:33.390] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000784)  
but wala po dito yung utilization po ng budget from last year.

**[00:54:40.100] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000785)  
So, maybe we can request our department or the DOJ to provide committee the utilization po ng budget.

**[00:54:53.650] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000786)  
As of today po.

**[00:54:56.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000787)  
If it is available, please furnish Representative Padernos and a copy to the committee.

**[00:55:02.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000788)  
Yes, we will furnish, Your Honor.

**[00:55:04.880] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000789)  
Your Honor, if I may add lang, doon sa decongestion.

**[00:55:08.470] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000790)  
Dalwa ho to, lutong bibingka.

**[00:55:10.950] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000791)  
Ang isa ho yung nagdadagdag tayo ng facilities, no?

**[00:55:14.850] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000792)  
Basically, ang isa pa rin ho, pinabibilis din natin yung release natin.

**[00:55:18.870] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000793)  
Na nagawa na po natin ng medyo maganda na po ang proseso natin.

**[00:55:23.480] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000794)  
Lalo na po yung mga may GCTA nating mga...

**[00:55:27.420] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000795)  
Alam naman po natin yung mga kababayan na natin na naligaw ng landas.

**[00:55:31.370] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000796)  
Pero lahat naman po may opportunity ng second chance.

**[00:55:34.590] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000797)  
Pag naka-comply na ho sila doon sa requirements ng batas, mag-comply sila sa GCTA,

**[00:55:40.990] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000798)  
buong yabang din ho namin pinagmamalaki na ang BUCOR po,

**[00:55:46.340] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000799)  
kakibat ang PAO, kakibat po yung ating PPA,

**[00:55:51.280] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000800)  
nakapagpa-release na ho tayo ng...

**[00:55:52.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000801)  
From 2022, 32,932 PDLs.

**[00:56:00.730] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000802)  
So kasama po nito, mas mabilis na po natin na pro-proseso.

**[00:56:05.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000803)  
Gumamit na rin po tayo dito ng teknolohiya, no?

**[00:56:08.380] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000804)  
So ngayon yung mga karpeta, wala na ho yung pagala-gala o nawawalang karpeta.

**[00:56:13.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000805)  
Ngayon ho sa BUCOR, lahat po, bago pa lang sila pumasok,

**[00:56:17.250] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000806)  
naka-digitize na ho. May redundancy plan na ho tayo

**[00:56:20.950] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000807)  
na hindi mawawala yung karpeta.

**[00:56:22.950] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000808)  
Kaya po, mas na-anticipate na natin.

**[00:56:26.990] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000809)  
At tumataas din yung pag-asa ng ating mga kapatid na nakakulong

**[00:56:31.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000810)  
na pag ako'y nagbait dito sa loob, makakalaya na ako sa ganitong panahon.

**[00:56:36.500] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000811)  
So, ano ho, dalawang inisyatibo ho,

**[00:56:41.570] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000812)  
padamihin yung regional prisons facility natin

**[00:56:44.450] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000813)  
at ang isa, pabilisin din yung releases natin dun sa qualified na po.

**[00:56:49.570] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000814)  
Kasi po, ang ayaw po natin ay yung nakakulong,

**[00:56:52.980] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000815)  
nakakakuha lang ng abogado ang nare-release.

**[00:56:55.940] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000816)  
Dahil sila lang yung nakakapag-file ng writ of habeas corpus.

**[00:57:00.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000817)  
Kaya po, sa ngayon ho, updated po yung ating releases.

**[00:57:04.150] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000818)  
Ang target po ng DOJ diyan ay not a day longer.

**[00:57:09.230] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000819)  
Not a day longer if, of course, it's not a perfect system.

**[00:57:13.450] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000820)  
We're working on it, we're striving hard para po,

**[00:57:16.500] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000821)  
not a day longer yung ating pong mga kapatid na PDLs.

**[00:57:19.660] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000822)  
Ito po ang dahilan kaya 180% po.

**[00:57:23.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000823)  
As of this time po, pero we're working hard para po

**[00:57:26.490] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000824)  
makamit natin yung ideal number po para sa ating mga nakakulong.

**[00:57:31.690] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000825)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:34.270] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000826)  
For my next question is on, with regard to the ano po,

**[00:57:38.760] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000827)  
I'd also like to request from the DOJ yung ating vacancies in every department,

**[00:57:45.770] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000828)  
every agencies, attached agencies,

**[00:57:47.830] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000829)  
para po malaman natin saan po ba kailangan na lagyan ng,

**[00:57:53.300] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000830)  
ano po yung mga open for applications.

**[00:57:55.690] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000831)  
Kasi may plantilya na po yung mga yan,

**[00:57:57.690] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000832)  
sayang naman po kung hindi ma-fill up yung ating vacancies.

**[00:58:01.590] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000833)  
Mayroon po ba tayong brief report on our vacancies?

**[00:58:13.060] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000834)  
We will submit a comprehensive report,

**[00:58:15.220] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000835)  
but as a summary, Your Honor,

**[00:58:17.660] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000836)  
out of the 7,651 authorized plantilya positions

**[00:58:23.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000837)  
for the Office of the Secretary, that's the Department of Justice,

**[00:58:27.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000838)  
we have 6,145 filled up.

**[00:58:30.480] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000839)  
And 1,506 unfilled positions.

**[00:58:35.860] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000840)  
So, and we would also be happy to report to you na,

**[00:58:43.450] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000841)  
nung nag-start po ng administration na to,

**[00:58:45.590] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000842)  
especially po yung mga presidential appointees,

**[00:58:48.370] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000843)  
we started with a timeline na almost 2 years, yung mga piskal po natin.

**[00:58:53.190] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000844)  
Now, with cooperation with DBM and the Office of the President,

**[00:58:56.810] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000845)  
na-shorten na po natin to 4 to 5 months po,

**[00:59:00.490] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000846)  
yung mga piskal po natin, yung mga prosecutor positions.

**[00:59:04.050] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000847)  
And we're also happy to report to this committee

**[00:59:06.630] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000848)  
that since the start of this administration,

**[00:59:10.530] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000849)  
from 2,966 prosecutorial position,

**[00:59:15.210] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000850)  
the DBM has created additional 1,002.

**[00:59:19.430] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000851)  
So, we're now 3,968 prosecutorial positions.

**[00:59:25.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000852)  
And ang take-up po nito sa public, maganda po.

**[00:59:27.660] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000853)  
Meron po kaming mga posisyon, mga Your Honors,

**[00:59:30.500] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000854)  
isang posisyon, 103 applicants.

**[00:59:33.260] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000855)  
So, we're able to recruit, hopefully, the best and the brightest

**[00:59:38.700] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000856)  
from those in the legal sector so that they join the workforce po

**[00:59:44.300] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000857)  
ng ating National Prosecution Service.

**[00:59:47.560] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000858)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:49.720] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000859)  
Gusto ko lang din pong i-discuss yung ating mga petition for review under the DOJ

**[00:59:55.030] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000860)  
kasi based on my experience,

**[00:59:57.540] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000861)  
my experience even before when I was practicing, dun po talaga nagkakaroon ng parang patong-patong

**[01:00:07.360] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000862)  
na kaso. Ano na po bang status doon and paano po na-address ngayon ng ating bagong

**[01:00:14.480] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000863)  
kalihim itong problema na to po?

**[01:00:18.660] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000864)  
Of course, when I assumed as acting secretary November 11, sometime in this year po, early

**[01:00:30.590] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000865)  
this year, I issued a department circular. Pag nakita ninyo po yung docket namin, total po iyan ng

**[01:00:37.620] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000866)  
27,000 sa Office of the Secretary, petitions for review, starting as old as panahon pa po

**[01:00:44.530] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000867)  
ni Secretary Raul Gonzalez.

**[01:00:47.130] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000868)  
So what happens?

**[01:00:48.450] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000869)  
What I did, I issued the department circular. So commencing po November 12, 2025, all petitions

**[01:00:56.310] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000870)  
for review filed starting November 12, I have delegated, I've given authority to all the

**[01:01:02.030] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000871)  
undersecretaries to review them and to ensure na 100% ang clearance rate. Ang nakikita po

**[01:01:09.120] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000872)  
namin, Your Honors, around 200 per week po yung nagpe-petition for review. So around 800

**[01:01:15.490] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000873)  
per month. With eight undersecretaries.

**[01:01:18.230] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000874)  
Ang mandate ko po sa kanila is hopefully we clear everything that goes in starting November

**[01:01:25.800] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000875)  
11. Naka-delegate na po sa undersecretaries. Ano naman po ang gagawin ni Secretary ng department

**[01:01:31.570] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000876)  
for those pending November 11 and prior years. So nag-cut off po kami ng January 1, 2022-November 11, 2025.

**[01:01:40.350] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000877)  
November 11, 2025. Yan po yung nasa opisina ko and I have certain task forces helping me

**[01:01:47.200] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000878)  
out.

**[01:01:48.020] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000879)  
On that, para po ma-resolve siya, ma-resolve siya, different reviewing legal teams po.

**[01:01:54.510] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000880)  
And so far po, from those pending from 2020 to 2025, that's around 16,000. That's around

**[01:02:04.990] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000881)  
16,000 po cases. My office has resolved almost 500 na po thus far. But we continue the work.

**[01:02:13.070] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000882)  
We continue the work.

**[01:02:15.340] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000883)  
For the older ones, yung prior to 2020.

**[01:02:17.800] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000884)  
We also have a... We also issued publications to litigants who are parties to those petitions

**[01:02:26.800] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000885)  
for review and we've asked them, basically, to confirm or not to confirm yung interest

**[01:02:34.100] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000886)  
sila on those cases yet. And hopefully, by end of this quarter, Your Honors, no? And

**[01:02:41.910] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000887)  
we will gladly share with the committee how we are moving forward with all these cases.

**[01:02:47.050] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000889)  
This is basically po, ang tawag nga po sa mga practitioner dyan, pag pumasok daw sa DOJ, parang black hole.

**[01:02:55.130] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000890)  
Hindi na alam kung ano yung nangyari ng petition for review.

**[01:02:58.110] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000891)  
But we're trying to address this because this is a check and balance mechanism sa mga field offices po natin.

**[01:03:04.860] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000892)  
For everyone's information, we have 250 offices nationwide.

**[01:03:09.330] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000893)  
Ito po yung city prosecutor's office, provincial prosecutor's office, regional prosecutor's office.

**[01:03:15.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000894)  
And also the office of the Secretary of Justice Prosecutorial Service, which is in Padre Faura.

**[01:03:21.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000895)  
Of all this, the litigant has a remedy going to the Secretary of Justice.

**[01:03:26.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000896)  
And we're trying to put up a system here so that we resolve them in time.

**[01:03:33.100] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000897)  
The problem po is, based on time and motion, talaga pong may challenge ang Department of Justice.

**[01:03:38.740] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000898)  
But we cannot naman prohibit filing of these petitions for review.

**[01:03:43.900] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000899)  
So it's basically...

**[01:03:45.430] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000900)  
It's basically driven din po from the litigants po na nagpa-file ng petitions sa amin.

**[01:03:52.870] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000901)  
But I think, Your Honor, we're in the right direction.

**[01:03:57.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000902)  
Hopefully, we improve as the months, as the days go by.

**[01:04:02.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000903)  
Your Honor.

**[01:04:03.750] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000904)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:06.820] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000905)  
Pasadaan ko lang po sa ating Bureau of Immigration naman on the status of our e-gates and modernization.

**[01:04:13.270] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000906)  
Ilang e-gates na po bang...

**[01:04:15.180] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000907)  
Ang fully operational ngayon sa Naia, Clark, Mactan, Cebu, at Davao.

**[01:04:21.310] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000908)  
Anong procurement status po nung natitirang units for Phase 1, 1.976 billion,

**[01:04:27.510] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000909)  
at Phase 2, 1.347 billion?

**[01:04:31.490] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000910)  
May service level agreement po standards ba tayo sa IT providers natin pag nagka-downtime?

**[01:04:38.000] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000911)  
At nakasingil na po ba tayo ng penalties laban sa mga kontraktor?

**[01:04:42.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000912)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:04:43.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000913)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[01:04:44.570] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000914)  
Mr. Chairman and Congressman Padernos and Honorable Members of the House of Representatives.

**[01:04:50.150] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000915)  
Pagdating po sa e-gates po natin, sa Terminal 1 po,

**[01:04:54.190] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000916)  
ang total po currently is 18, 12 para sa arrival, at for departure, 6 na e-gates po.

**[01:05:01.930] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000917)  
Para naman po sa T3, ang arrival po, meron pong tatlumpu, or 30.

**[01:05:07.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000918)  
Sa departure po, tatlumpu rin po, or 30.

**[01:05:10.390] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000919)  
So ang total po sa Terminals 1 and 3 is 78.

**[01:05:14.710] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000920)  
Pagdating naman po sa Cebu International—Clark, I'm sorry po—Clark International Airport, meron pong tatlo.

**[01:05:24.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000921)  
Dito naman po sa Mactan Cebu, tatlong rin po.

**[01:05:27.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000922)  
Sa Davao International Airport, dalawa po.

**[01:05:31.490] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000923)  
So yun po ang total number po ng e-gates po natin, not only sa T1 and T3, but sa ibang international airports po, sa labas po ng Metro Manila.

**[01:05:42.390] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000924)  
Ngayon, pagdating naman po sa...

**[01:05:44.860] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000925)  
status po nung ating e-gates project,

**[01:05:51.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000926)  
yun pong sa Phase 1, na awarded po nung 2025, 1.5 billion po yun.

**[01:05:58.330] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000927)  
Continuing po ang aming procurement, Your Honor.

**[01:06:01.570] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000928)  
Nakatipid po tayo doon sa intended budget na 1.9 billion, ng 400 million.

**[01:06:06.210] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000929)  
Pagdating naman po sa Phase 2 po ng proyekto,

**[01:06:09.890] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000930)  
ang terms of reference po para dito sa Phase 2 ng proyekto, worth 1.3 billion,

**[01:06:15.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000931)  
underway na po sa Departamento.

**[01:06:16.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000932)  
The Department of Justice, yung pong listahan po ng mga terms of reference.

**[01:06:22.130] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000933)  
Pagdating naman po sa Phase 3, 565 million po yun.

**[01:06:26.090] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000934)  
Ang commitment po namin, at we're on track, na on budget po for 2025, meron po kaming catch-up plan,

**[01:06:33.100] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000935)  
magiging fully obligated na po by November 2026.

**[01:06:36.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000936)  
Kasama na po dyan yung sa e-gates Phase 1 project.

**[01:06:40.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000937)  
At ang Phase 2 po ng e-gates project, worth 1.3 billion, magiging fully obligated na rin po by November.

**[01:06:46.950] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000938)  
As a matter of fact, Your Honor, we have been conducting weekly meetings

**[01:06:52.210] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000939)  
para po malaman ng ahensya kung ano na po ang status ng ating proyekto.

**[01:06:59.850] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000940)  
Pagdating naman po, Your Honor, sa service agreements, meron po tayong service agreements,

**[01:07:03.690] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000941)  
but those are constantly under review.

**[01:07:05.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000942)  
Kasama po sa service agreements, hinihingi po namin ng commitment sa mapuprovide po dito ng mga kumpanya, Your Honors.

**[01:07:24.900] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000943)  
Follow-up question lang po.

**[01:07:28.900] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000944)  
So okay na po lahat. So kailan po matatapos yung Phase 2?

**[01:07:32.340] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-000945)  
Ang Phase 2 po, matatapos po by November 2026. Fully obligated na po yan by that time.

**[01:07:38.760] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000946)  
So I'd just like to commend, Mr. Chair, yung efforts po ng ating Bureau of Immigration.

**[01:07:43.840] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000947)  
If you notice, ating mga kababayan, mas mabilis na ngayon yung proseso pag nasa airport.

**[01:07:50.060] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000948)  
Of course, with their partnership also with...

**[01:07:54.470] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000949)  
Airport operator.

**[01:07:56.300] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000950)  
Sa ano naman po ako, sa PCGG po. Saan po ba ang PCGG?

**[01:08:03.280] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000951)  
Nandiyan po ba ang PCGG?

**[01:08:05.010] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000953)  
Ayan.

**[01:08:05.230] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000954)  
Justice.

**[01:08:06.090] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000955)  
Justice po.

**[01:08:06.670] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000956)  
Mr. Chair, gusto ko lang po sanang itanong. What is the current budget vis-a-vis total remittance of PCGG?

**[01:08:13.790] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000957)  
And with that contribution, they have a specific...

**[01:08:17.800] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000958)  
Does PCGG have a specific proposal which will not entail additional funding from Congress?

**[01:08:28.060] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000959)  
Mr. Chair, members of the committee, thank you for this opportunity.

**[01:08:36.800] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000960)  
Just for historical context, your honors, in the past 40 years, the PCGG has recovered a total amount of 183 billion in cash,

**[01:08:54.290] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000961)  
125 billion in assets.

**[01:08:57.270] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000962)  
And as of now, during the current administration, the PCGG was able to recover

**[01:09:09.080] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000963)  
8.3 billion worth of assets. As far as budget is concerned, historically, the budget of

**[01:09:20.770] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000964)  
the PCGG has always been below 200 million. So, given that we have already, that it has

**[01:09:28.520] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000965)  
remitted 8.3 billion during the current administration, I would say that the budget of PCGG was really

**[01:09:38.410] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000966)  
given us good returns.

**[01:09:48.200] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000967)  
Maraming salamat po. Meron pa po kayong maidadagdag?

**[01:09:52.740] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000968)  
The additional funding from Congress.

**[01:09:59.110] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000969)  
Ano po iyon?

**[01:10:00.320] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000970)  
We have a bigger budget now, Your Honors, because PCGG will start paying rent for office

**[01:10:13.630] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000971)  
space. Because before, our office space was free. As one of the conditions, when we sold

**[01:10:21.680] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000972)  
an asset, which is in front of Mega Mall along EDSA, we sold that for 800 million pesos.

**[01:10:28.270] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000973)  
And the buyer was San Miguel. And they have a building, which we are now occupying, and

**[01:10:35.670] PCGG Chairperson Melchor Quirino C. Sadang** (asr-000974)  
they gave us a part of the building for occupation.

**[01:10:40.160] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000976)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Quick manifestation na lang, Mr. Chair. So, base naman po sa report

**[01:10:47.160] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000977)  
ng ating DOJ, res ipsa loquitur. The numbers speak for themselves. Case disposition rate

**[01:10:54.280] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000978)  
po, napakataas, 99%.

**[01:10:56.490] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000979)  
Ang success rate nila ng prosecution, also 90 plus percent. And lahat po ng ating mga

**[01:11:05.210] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000980)  
performance indicators, it would appear na the DOJ is really doing their job and exceeding

**[01:11:14.480] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000981)  
our expectation. So, with that said, I would like to manifest my full support for the proposed

**[01:11:22.710] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000982)  
budget. Actually, edit po natin yan.

**[01:11:27.270] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000983)  
I manifest that not just my full support for the proposed budget of the Department of

**[01:11:33.650] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000984)  
Justice, but also the increase of its budget, especially for the NBI and BI, kasi nakita ko

**[01:11:40.520] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000985)  
po ang NBI at BI, 33 percent and 27 percent decrease. Meron po ba tayong brief lang na

**[01:11:48.480] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000986)  
explanation kung bakit po nabawasan ng sobrang taas yung dalawang agencies na yan? But with

**[01:11:56.050] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000987)  
that said, yun lang po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:11:59.660] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000988)  
I believe this would be relative to the absorptive capacity ng agency. This was, of course, this

**[01:12:08.910] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000989)  
is the DBM's call, no? But it was assessed by the DBM yung absorptive capacity of the

**[01:12:13.570] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000990)  
two agencies. That's why we have catch-up plans for 2026, so that they are able to cope

**[01:12:19.320] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000991)  
with the budget releases for the two respective agencies. But thank you very much, Your Honor,

**[01:12:24.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-000992)  
for the support.

**[01:12:25.530] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-000993)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:12:26.510] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000994)

**[01:12:33.550] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000995)  
members who are present?
