Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:13:00.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001001)  
Thank you. Next in line to interpellate from the minority, Representative Chel Diokno.

**[01:13:08.380] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001002)  
Maraming salamat po, Chair Mike, at magandang hapon po sa DOJ family.

**[01:13:14.200] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001003)  
Sisimulan ko po sa isang letter C. Hindi po ito Chel, kundi corruption ang dapat nating

**[01:13:20.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001004)  
pag-usapan.

**[01:13:22.240] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001005)  
I noticed that there was a recent article attributed to the DOJ.

**[01:13:27.400] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001006)  
which stated that the over 841 million pesos

**[01:13:32.800] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001007)  
ay naibalik na sa ating treasury from flood control projects.

**[01:13:39.160] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001008)  
Mula sa apat na nasangkot sa flood control cases na naging state witness,

**[01:13:45.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001009)  
I think this would refer to Henry Alcantara, former USEC Bernardo, 500 million,

**[01:13:52.440] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001010)  
Henry Alcantara, 181 million, Engineer Opulencia, 140 million, and Sally Santos, 20 million.

**[01:14:02.400] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001011)  
Una ko pong tanong kay Secretary Vida, good morning po.

**[01:14:06.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001012)  
Voluntary po itong pagbabalik nila?

**[01:14:09.310] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001013)  
Opo, voluntary po ito.

**[01:14:11.100] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001014)  
Meron bang na-file na mga forfeiture cases against any of these four individuals

**[01:14:16.920] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001015)  
to ensure na baka naman meron pa silang tinatago na hindi pa nila binibigay

**[01:14:21.880] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001016)  
o binabalik sa gobyerno?

**[01:14:24.570] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001017)  
Actually, kaakibat po ito sa kondisyon nung kanilang pagpasok bilang state witness, Your Honor.

**[01:14:30.880] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001018)  
At sa ngayon nga po, nagagamit sila ngayon ng Office of the Ombudsman

**[01:14:35.120] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001019)  
sa patuloy na paglilitis ng mga kasong nakasalang ngayon sa Sandiganbayan at sa RTC.

**[01:14:42.280] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001020)  
Kaya po ang patuloy nilang pagganap dun sa terms and conditions

**[01:14:47.680] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001021)  
ng kanilang pagiging state witness, ito po ang kabahagi nun.

**[01:14:51.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001022)  
Your Honor.

**[01:14:52.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001023)  
Did you rely just on their word, or did you also

**[01:14:55.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001024)  
coordinate with AMLA, get bank secrecy waivers, and do other checking

**[01:15:02.560] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001025)  
para masiguro natin na lahat ng sinasabi nilang ibinalik ay yun na po yun?

**[01:15:08.240] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001026)  
Ang naging estratehiya po niyan para makita po yung mga commitment nilang amounts,

**[01:15:14.840] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001027)  
tandaan po natin, paalala lang po pala, yung pagiging state witness nila ay case-specific lang ho.

**[01:15:22.680] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001028)  
Kaya yung kanilang immunity, dun sa kasong yun lang ho.

**[01:15:25.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001029)  
Kaya yung may disclosure sila na sila kesyo ay may kinita dun sa project na yun,

**[01:15:33.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001030)  
yun lamang po sila absuwelto.

**[01:15:35.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001031)  
Kaya po pag may nadiskubre ang Office of the Ombudsman, ang DOJ, o kung ano mang ahensya,

**[01:15:41.600] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001032)  
na ibang bagay na sila'y sangkot pa rin, hindi ho yun saklaw.

**[01:15:45.760] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001033)  
Kaya po ang atin po, tinakda po natin yung pagtatalaga nung amount na to,

**[01:15:50.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001034)  
nagsimula pa po ito panahon ni Secretary, now Ombudsman, Boying Remulla,

**[01:15:55.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001035)  
na project-specific lang.

**[01:15:58.160] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001036)  
Yung sinabi nyo lang na kayo ay sangkot at kayo ay involved,

**[01:16:03.480] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001037)  
pero yung immunity mo, dun lang sa particular na project na yun.

**[01:16:07.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001038)  
Kaya pag may nakita po tayo, tingin ko ho, protektado ang bayan dito.

**[01:16:11.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001039)  
Pag may nakita ho tayo sa apat na to, sa kanilang apat na individual,

**[01:16:15.280] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001040)  
na walang disclosure o may tinago sa atin, pwede pa ho natin silang habulin dun.

**[01:16:20.120] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001041)  
Of course, iba po yung sa pananaw ng Office of the Ombudsman.

**[01:16:23.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001042)  
Iba pong usapin.

**[01:16:25.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001043)  
Sa amin po, yung pagiging state witness sila under the Witness Protection Program.

**[01:16:29.660] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001044)  
Thank you, Secretary Vida.

**[01:16:31.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001045)  
Doon sa very impressive yung inyong report, lalong-lalo na yung tinatawag ninyong

**[01:16:38.160] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001046)  
prosecutorial success rate, or PSR,

**[01:16:40.720] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001047)  
na ngayon na taon na ito, o 2025, ay nasa 90.8%.

**[01:16:46.120] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001048)  
Do you have a figure for your PSR for graft and corruption cases?

**[01:16:54.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001049)  
Kasi yun na yun po

**[01:16:55.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001050)  
ang tinututukan ng taong bayan ngayon.

**[01:16:57.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001051)  
Gusto naming malaman kung yung mga graft and corruption cases ba

**[01:17:01.880] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001052)  
nabibigyan ng top priority ng ating pamahalaan.

**[01:17:06.240] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001053)  
Karamihan ba ng mga nasasangkot ay nakakasuhan at nako-convict?

**[01:17:11.460] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001054)  
Opo, opo, Your Honor. We need to disaggregate our data, yung available po sa amin.

**[01:17:18.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001055)  
And we will submit to the committee, Your Honors, if you may.

**[01:17:23.400] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001056)  
We need to disaggregate yung data namin.

**[01:17:25.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001057)  
And of course, this data doesn't involve cases handled by the Office of the Ombudsman.

**[01:17:30.920] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001058)  
Yes.

**[01:17:31.800] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001059)  
In fact, I'll go back to that point later.

**[01:17:34.600] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001060)  
But I'm just curious, because in 2022, when Secretary, now Ombudsman, Remulla

**[01:17:41.160] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001061)  
occupied the Office of Secretary of Justice,

**[01:17:44.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001062)  
nabanggit niya na ang conviction rate, hindi po ito PSR, kundi conviction rate,

**[01:17:49.440] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001063)  
ay nasa 25% lang.

**[01:17:51.400] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001064)  
In fact, he expressed dismay over it.

**[01:17:54.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001065)  
Now,

**[01:17:55.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001066)  
your figure of 90% is very impressive,

**[01:17:58.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001067)  
but it may give the wrong impression to the public

**[01:18:04.240] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001068)  
that we have already overcome our issues with convictions.

**[01:18:08.560] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001069)  
So, may I know, does the DOJ keep a separate tab on conviction rate aside from PSR?

**[01:18:15.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001070)  
O na-abandonan po ninyo yan?

**[01:18:20.600] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001071)  
Actually, hindi po namin.

**[01:18:22.880] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001072)  
The prosecution success rate, mix po ito.

**[01:18:26.760] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001073)  
Yung conviction ng kaso, kasama na rin po dito yung tinatawag po na nagkaroon po ng compromise agreement.

**[01:18:35.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001074)  
And based on that compromise, nagkaroon pa rin ng hatol yung husgado sa isang particular na kaso.

**[01:18:42.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001075)  
Ito po yung pumapasok.

**[01:18:44.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001076)  
Pero batid po namin, yung 20%, if I recall it right, that would be more particular po.

**[01:18:51.280] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001077)  
Yung frustration po ni Secretary Boying at the time was more in the drug cases.

**[01:18:57.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001078)  
Yung after po, yung after nung 2022, na lumabas na po yung mga tunay na proseso na kahit fiscal ka o judge ka,

**[01:19:11.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001079)  
na nag-dismiss ka ng kaso base sa ebidensya, hindi ka li-labelan na protector ng droga.

**[01:19:19.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001080)  
Tapos, ang isa pa rin ho, ito po yung pagtataas namin nung antas, yung from probable cause,

**[01:19:24.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001081)  
tinaas po ng DOJ, yung antas po ng ebidensya na kailangan natin ngayon,

**[01:19:29.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001082)  
yung prima facie evidence with reasonable certainty of conviction.

**[01:19:35.160] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001083)  
Ang isa pong nakikita naming ehemplo dito ay yung sa, pag nakita po natin yung mga sa United States po,

**[01:19:45.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001084)  
ang taas po ng kanilang plea bargaining.

**[01:19:47.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001085)  
Dahil pag plea bargaining, mas mabilis po ang proseso, mas maliit ang resources ng gobyerno.

**[01:19:54.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001086)  
So, dahil matitibay yung kasong fina-file.

**[01:19:57.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001087)  
And we see ho the benefit now of our case buildup, Your Honors, with the data we're getting.

**[01:20:05.140] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001088)  
Tama po ba, Secretary Vida, na pagdating sa prosecutorial success rate,

**[01:20:12.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001089)  
hindi po kasama doon sa computation yung mga nadi-dismiss na kaso

**[01:20:16.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001090)  
dahil let's say, hindi dumating yung mga testigo, let's say drug case,

**[01:20:21.240] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001091)  
yung mga pulis na dapat humarap ay hindi humarap,

**[01:20:24.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001092)  
kaya nadismiss na agad ang kaso.

**[01:20:27.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001093)  
Is that captured by the PSR?

**[01:20:45.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001094)  
Is that captured?

**[01:20:48.220] DOJ representative** (asr-001095)  
Not captured in the data.

**[01:20:52.240] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001096)  
We need to check. We will submit, Your Honor.

**[01:20:55.270] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001097)  
Well, yeah, yeah.

**[01:20:57.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001098)  
Kasi the data would seem to be parang...

**[01:21:01.200] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001099)  
Kaya ako po tinatanong yung, Secretary,

**[01:21:03.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001100)  
ay dahil sa experience ko, hindi lang sa drug cases, pati na rin sa corruption cases.

**[01:21:09.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001101)  
Yung isang lusot na ginagamit ng mga corrupt at ng

**[01:21:12.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001102)  
mga criminal, ay they will influence, intimidate, or purchase the witnesses for the prosecution,

**[01:21:19.960] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001103)  
hindi na sila haharap, at kung hindi naka-capture yan ng PSR,

**[01:21:24.200] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001104)  
then malaking bagay po iyon na hindi natin namo-monitor,

**[01:21:28.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001105)  
which I think is very important for the DOJ to monitor.

**[01:21:33.730] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001106)  
Completely agree with that, Your Honor.

**[01:21:35.680] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001107)  
We will do that, Your Honor.

**[01:21:37.260] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001108)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:21:39.080] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001109)  
I think I have a minute and 18 seconds left.

**[01:21:42.760] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001110)  
Nabanggit niyo kanina na hindi lang naman ninyo trabaho yung anti-corruption,

**[01:21:46.440] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001111)  
and tama-tama po kayo doon.

**[01:21:48.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001112)  
It's also the job of the Office of the Ombudsman, as well as the Anti-Money Laundering Council, among others.

**[01:21:54.560] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001113)  
Kayo po ba ay nag-uusap?

**[01:21:56.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001114)  
Do you have a coordination committee or anything like that that discusses, for example, budget that all of you need,

**[01:22:05.600] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001115)  
that discusses things that are needed by the government,

**[01:22:10.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001116)  
beyond yung particular...

**[01:22:11.720] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001117)  
department po ninyo, DOJ?

**[01:22:14.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001118)  
What we have now, our engagement with the Office of the Ombudsman,

**[01:22:18.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001119)  
is an existing memorandum of agreement

**[01:22:21.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001120)  
relative to investigation of cases and filing of cases

**[01:22:25.480] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001121)  
for those salary grade 27 and above

**[01:22:29.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001122)  
for the Office of the Ombudsman and those below

**[01:22:32.400] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001123)  
to be handled by the Department of Justice.

**[01:22:35.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001124)  
Ang isa pa ho naming engagement with the Office of the Ombudsman now is

**[01:22:39.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001125)  
the possibility of coming up with a code on anti-corruption.

**[01:22:44.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001126)  
This would basically be seeing the learnings that we saw in the recent past,

**[01:22:52.320] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001127)  
kung paano natin talaga mas mapapatibay yung mga batas natin on anti-corruption.

**[01:22:58.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001128)  
We have an ongoing engagement on that.

**[01:23:01.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001129)  
We have a technical working group, Your Honors,

**[01:23:03.480] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001130)  
on possibly doing work on possible legislation

**[01:23:08.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001131)  
on coming up with a code on anti-corruption, Your Honors.

**[01:23:11.720] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001132)  
So bale, sa kasalukuyan ay ad hoc lang po

**[01:23:15.040] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001133)  
ang pakikipag-usap ninyo ng AMLA at ng OMB.

**[01:23:19.800] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001134)  
Kaya ako tinatanong yan, because way back in, I think it was 2006,

**[01:23:25.880] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001135)  
I was informed, I learned that there was a study made of the corruption cases

**[01:23:32.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001136)  
that went through the Sandiganbayan, at ang nalaman po nila noon

**[01:23:37.400] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001137)  
ay out of 110 persons convicted of graft and corruption cases,

**[01:23:43.320] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001138)  
sa sampu lang, only 10 were found to have been actually inside the jail serving their sentence.

**[01:23:51.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001139)  
Ito po yung mga convicted of graft and corruption by final judgment.

**[01:23:56.400] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001140)  
Now, is there, to your knowledge, may I just finish my question, Mr. Chair?

**[01:24:01.880] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001141)  
To your knowledge, is there any centralized system or database

**[01:24:06.560] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001142)  
of the government that is there to keep track na kung yung isang tao ay na-convict ng graft and corruption?

**[01:24:14.120] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001143)  
Kahit sabihin natin, ibang mga criminal cases, at in-appeal niya yung kanyang conviction,

**[01:24:23.560] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001144)  
later na-affirm yung kanyang conviction, namu-monitor ba natin kung talagang nakukulong yung nakonvict?

**[01:24:29.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001145)  
Kaya ako po, let me just finish. The reason I ask is because not one agency can keep track of this.

**[01:24:35.680] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001146)  
Hindi kaya ng DOJ yan, hindi kaya ng Ombudsman, hindi kaya ng NBP or ng ating nasa DILG.

**[01:24:43.680] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001147)  
So, shouldn't there be a consolidated effort by our government?

**[01:24:49.800] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001148)  
Because sayang naman yung milyong-milyones na ginagastos niyo pang-prosecute ng isang tao kung hindi naman pala nakukulong?

**[01:24:56.800] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001149)  
Hindi po ba?

**[01:24:57.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001150)  
Yes, I completely agree po. Sa particular directive or legal framework should be brought out

**[01:25:04.400] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001152)  
so that those... I completely agree, Your Honor. Ang isa po namin, from our end, which I've initiated po,

**[01:25:12.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001153)  
we just had a meeting with my regional prosecutors and si PG Dong, is basically to monitor yung mga sinampahan namin ng kaso,

**[01:25:23.200] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001154)  
alin yung mga naka-archive doon, dahil at large yung mga akusado. That's something we can look within the DOJ.

**[01:25:32.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001155)  
Kaya po, nasa poder ko po yun. Pero I completely agree with you, Your Honor.

**[01:25:37.600] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001156)  
We need to look at yung mga convictions, kung sino talaga yung nahatulan ng sentensya at nakakulong.

**[01:25:45.440] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001157)  
Thank you so much, Secretary Vida. Sana kayo po ang maging pambato nitong talagang kinakailangan natin pagdating sa corruption.

**[01:25:52.600] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-001158)  
Thank you so much, Chair Mike.

**[01:25:55.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001159)  
Thank you po, Representative Chel Diokno. Next in line from the majority, Representative Brian Poe.

**[01:26:03.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001160)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Mr. Chair, I'd like to start with some questions for the NBI.

**[01:26:11.270] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001161)  
Mr. Chair, I want to ask the NBI, if the NBI has any funding for representatives or attaches in embassies abroad.

**[01:26:26.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001162)  
Based on RA 10867, Section 7F, the NBI is allowed to have an attaché or representative.

**[01:26:35.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001164)  
However, the funding, according to the law, must come from the NBI.

**[01:26:41.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001165)  
I want to ask if in this year's budget, is there an allocation for the NBI?

**[01:26:47.400] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001166)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Wala po. And I'm very thankful that you raised that issue because that is one of the future plans that the NBI is looking at,

**[01:26:57.740] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001167)  
that we put NBI attachés in at least yung selected po muna na mga areas.

**[01:27:02.430] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001168)  
Because dito po sa atin, sa Pilipinas.

**[01:27:04.310] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001169)  
Yung mga embassies from Europe and the U.S. Embassy itself, meron po silang mga counterparts.

**[01:27:11.830] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001170)  
It's been helping us to address transnational crimes.

**[01:27:14.650] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001171)  
So thank you for raising that issue.

**[01:27:17.470] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001172)  
And kasama po sana sa wish list namin iyan na mapalagay po.

**[01:27:22.170] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001173)  
And I'll take advantage also to request because we want to increase the number of agents.

**[01:27:27.810] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001174)  
Because if we will comply with that, we need additional agents.

**[01:27:31.560] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001175)  
Right now, for the benefit of this committee po and everybody,

**[01:27:36.260] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-001176)  
I'd like to tell you that ang NBI agents po namin sa buong Pilipinas is only about 300 persons lang po.

**[01:27:45.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001177)  
Mr. Chair, this is quite alarming, no?

**[01:27:50.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001178)  
Na our NBI doesn't have funding for any international agents or representatives to be attached to any of our embassies.

**[01:27:59.430] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001179)  
Mr. Chair, if I could remind the committee.

**[01:28:01.390] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001180)  
If I could remind the committee of all the responsibilities of the NBI going after

**[01:28:05.330] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001181)  
POGOs, scam networks, cyber fraud, online gambling, Interpol cases, human trafficking,

**[01:28:12.210] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001182)  
online sexual exploitation of children, the list goes on.

**[01:28:16.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001183)  
And yet we haven't given a single centavo for a single attaché representative abroad.

**[01:28:22.050] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001184)  
That's absolutely ridiculous, Mr. Chair.

**[01:28:25.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001185)  
And I feel that it's something that we should look into as we continue on this.

**[01:28:30.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001186)  
This deliberation on the appropriations for the Department of Justice.

**[01:28:34.830] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001187)  
Because, as our colleague here Representative Chel Diokno was saying about accountability and investigation,

**[01:28:42.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001188)  
how are we supposed to go after international fugitives if we don't even have funding for NBI representatives abroad?

**[01:28:50.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001189)  
Mr. Chair, I feel that this is one thing that we should take into consideration for this budget season.

**[01:28:56.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001190)  
Thank you, NBI Director po.

**[01:29:00.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001191)  
Next is I'd like to ask about what we're doing for our kababayans who don't have access to legal aid.

**[01:29:09.250] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001192)  
I believe meron tayong, and we were able to fund it last year, I believe, around 8 million pesos more or less

**[01:29:16.130] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001193)  
for the DOJ Action Center or yung tinatawag natin Katarungan Caravan.

**[01:29:22.030] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001194)  
Based on my numbers, and correct me if I'm wrong, Mr. Secretary, you're currently at around 15,000 people who have been

**[01:29:29.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001195)  
served so far in 2026.

**[01:29:31.870] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001196)  
This is well on its way to surpassing the numbers from last year of 25,000 served.

**[01:29:37.770] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001197)  
Is this correct?

**[01:29:39.730] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001198)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:29:41.700] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001199)  
Basically, this is the access to legal services.

**[01:29:44.660] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001200)  
But of course, the primary agency is the Public Attorney's Office, na nagpapasalamat kami.

**[01:29:52.550] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001201)  
Pero nakita po natin kasi yung puwang sa serbisyo.

**[01:29:56.900] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001202)  
At siguro, ang lagi ko hong binibigay niyan—I was interviewed—bakit naging verb yung Tulfo?

**[01:30:06.900] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001203)  
'Ipapa-Tulfo kita.' And I give credit to the Tulfo siblings kasi meron pong kulang na serbisyo.

**[01:30:14.740] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001204)  
At ito po yung pilit na pinupunuan. Kabahagi po dito yung DOJ Katarungan Caravan.

**[01:30:21.470] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001205)  
Sa atin pong pag-ikot sa buong Pilipinas para ilapit yung serbisyo on legal services sa ating mga kababayan,

**[01:30:30.860] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001206)  
nakita natin na meron talagang puwang na pwedeng punuan.

**[01:30:35.290] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001207)  
Pero para ho maging resilient at saka maging permanent po yung sistema ng serbisyo,

**[01:30:43.300] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001208)  
ang plano po natin ay, maliban na lang siya na parang ad hoc o task force, yung DOJ Katarungan Caravan

**[01:30:49.850] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001209)  
maging permanente ito sa bawat opisina ng DOJ.

**[01:30:53.930] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001210)  
Ito po yung sa ating 250 prosecutorial offices nationwide na na-establish.

**[01:31:00.500] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001211)  
ang pinipilit po namin ngayon ay mag-set up kami ng protocols on this legal service

**[01:31:06.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001212)  
para po yung bawat opisina sa bawat probinsya, sa bawat city at sa bawat region

**[01:31:12.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001213)  
ay may madudulugan na yung ating mga kababayan. Yung serbisyo,

**[01:31:19.430] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001214)  
yung puwang na iyon na free legal service.

**[01:31:21.340] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001215)  
Kaya nga po sa pagdedesign namin ng mga opisina ngayon, Your Honor,

**[01:31:25.230] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001216)  
pinipilit namin na kasama natin sa design yung DOJ Action Center

**[01:31:31.790] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001217)  
or ang gusto nga ho namin sanang matawag ito ay para ho may ownership yung mga kababayan natin

**[01:31:37.140] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001218)  
ay DOJ Katarungan Office o DOJKO.

**[01:31:40.900] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001219)  
Kasi sa dulo po itong serbisyong ito sa kababayan naman natin ito

**[01:31:44.910] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001220)  
para ho mas mapalapit natin yung serbisyo, Your Honor.

**[01:31:49.670] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001221)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:31:52.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001222)  
I was asking about this program kasi para sa mga kababayan natin,

**[01:31:55.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001223)  
malaking gastos po to go to their lawyers,

**[01:31:59.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001224)  
to go to centers of commerce where they can access legal services.

**[01:32:04.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001225)  
Kaya maganda yung programa ng DOJ and the fact that the DOJ, I believe,

**[01:32:07.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001226)  
has opened around nine new Katarungan regional centers.

**[01:32:13.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001227)  
Are we going to be opening more in the years to come?

**[01:32:16.390] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001228)  
Do we have funding for this, Secretary?

**[01:32:18.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001229)  
Yes.

**[01:32:19.400] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001230)  
This is part.

**[01:32:20.350] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001231)  
Actually, yung ating optimization plan for the Department of Justice

**[01:32:25.100] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001232)  
so that it's rather than a secretary-initiated program,

**[01:32:31.150] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001233)  
inilatag na po namin ito sa DBM

**[01:32:34.460] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001234)  
for the optimization plan for the Department of Justice

**[01:32:40.540] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001235)  
para may kaukulang plantilya, may kaukulang resources

**[01:32:45.970] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001236)  
para nga po yung 250 field offices natin

**[01:32:49.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001237)  
magkaroon po ng ganitong serbisyo.

**[01:32:52.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001238)  
Mr. Chair, the reason why I'm bringing this up also is

**[01:32:55.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001239)  
because with such a limited budget of only around 8 million pesos

**[01:32:58.450] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001240)  
under the office of the Secretary

**[01:33:00.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001241)  
for the Katarungan Caravan and the DOJ Action Center,

**[01:33:04.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001242)  
they've been able to help more than 15,000 people as of date now.

**[01:33:08.550] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001243)  
They're on track to help more than 25,000 people by the end of the year.

**[01:33:12.490] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001244)  
Mr. Chair, you realize that per person,

**[01:33:14.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001245)  
that means that the government is only,

**[01:33:16.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001246)  
spending about 250 to 300 pesos per person for legal assistance.

**[01:33:20.390] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001247)  
The average lawyer would charge you more than 1,000 to 3,000 pesos an hour.

**[01:33:25.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001248)  
The government is doing good for the people with such a limited fund.

**[01:33:29.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001249)  
I'm hoping that in this year's budget,

**[01:33:31.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001250)  
we will be able to increase the Secretary's capability

**[01:33:33.930] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001251)  
to touch base with the poorest of the poor

**[01:33:35.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001252)  
and help them with legal services.

**[01:33:37.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001253)  
And with that, Mr. Chair,

**[01:33:38.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001254)  
I only have one last set of questions.

**[01:33:42.790] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001255)  
I have a question for the Public Attorney's Office.

**[01:33:44.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001256)  
So, for PAO.

**[01:33:46.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001257)  
For PAO, this is really the bedrock of our public service.

**[01:33:50.810] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001258)  
I believe currently our goal is one attorney, one court.

**[01:33:55.270] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001259)  
Are we able to uphold that with our current budget?

**[01:34:16.570] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001260)  
Thank you for your question, Mr. Chair.

**[01:34:19.560] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001261)  
We have now some district offices

**[01:34:23.560] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001262)  
which I can deploy one-to-one per court,

**[01:34:27.550] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001263)  
but we're still lacking,

**[01:34:29.550] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001264)  
because PAO doesn't handle only criminal cases.

**[01:34:32.540] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001265)  
We have labor cases.

**[01:34:33.540] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001266)  
We have counterparts also served by the Office of the Solicitor General.

**[01:34:38.530] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001267)  
We have preliminary investigation cases.

**[01:34:41.520] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001268)  
And we are asking for additional 559 items.

**[01:34:46.510] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001269)  
And we hope the son of my friend, Senator Grace Poe,

**[01:34:51.500] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001270)  
will request the august halls of Congress

**[01:34:56.490] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001271)  
to fulfill this deficiency.

**[01:34:58.490] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001272)  
And we are very supportive of the Katarungan Caravan

**[01:35:01.480] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001273)  
because we're sending lawyers to assist

**[01:35:03.480] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001274)  
our honorable Undersecretary Margarita Gutierrez,

**[01:35:06.480] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001275)  
who happened to be my student at Ateneo Law School

**[01:35:09.470] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001276)  
and one of my best students.

**[01:35:12.460] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001277)  
So, we are requesting that this hall will insert this deficiency

**[01:35:19.450] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001278)  
because we have not only two agencies which are our counterparts,

**[01:35:24.440] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001279)  
OSG, NPS, but also the private practitioners

**[01:35:28.430] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001280)  
who are worthy opponents of PAO.

**[01:35:30.430] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001281)  
And I'm very happy that the question was propounded today.

**[01:35:34.420] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001282)  
Thank you.

**[01:35:35.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001283)  
Thank you.

**[01:35:36.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001284)  
Mr. Chair, the reason why I asked about that

**[01:35:38.730] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001285)  
is because PAO's budget for fiscal year 2026

**[01:35:42.670] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001286)  
was 6.6 billion, more or less.

**[01:35:45.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001287)  
This year, in the NEP, it's 7.1 billion.

**[01:35:48.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001288)  
There's an increase of about 400 million pesos.

**[01:35:51.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001289)  
Is this for the items that you requested?

**[01:35:55.440] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001290)  
I'm sorry, sir.

**[01:35:57.340] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001291)  
That's not the purpose of those increases.

**[01:36:00.200] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001292)  
But the mandatory increases of SSL VI,

**[01:36:03.050] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001293)  
also, I think there are...

**[01:36:07.810] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001294)  
for the lawyers in addition to the 87 lawyers that were included last year.

**[01:36:13.470] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001295)  
So, these are not new creations but past creations by the DBM.

**[01:36:18.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001296)  
So, it's just funding for your past creations?

**[01:36:21.220] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001297)  
Yes, deficiencies, Your Honor.

**[01:36:22.330] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001298)  
Ah, okay.

**[01:36:23.450] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001299)  
So, that's why there's an increase of 400 million?

**[01:36:25.680] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001300)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:36:26.650] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001301)  
Because the salary now of our lawyers being hired

**[01:36:30.510] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001302)  
is at par with the new prosecutors,

**[01:36:33.400] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001303)  
around six digits per month

**[01:36:35.330] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001304)  
because of the number of cases we're handling.

**[01:36:38.230] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001305)  
In fact, we're happy to tell you that we have served

**[01:36:40.160] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001306)  
16 million Filipino people last year.

**[01:36:42.090] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001307)  
And now, about, I think, 6 million Filipino people as of June.

**[01:36:49.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001308)  
Mr. Chair, the reason why I'm asking

**[01:36:51.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001309)  
whether or not there's sufficient funding,

**[01:36:53.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001310)  
if we're asking for additional items pa,

**[01:36:55.550] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001311)  
kulang pa itong budget na nakita natin sa NEP, no?

**[01:36:59.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001312)  
We actually need to increase it.

**[01:37:01.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001313)  
Hopefully, and I do hope that the PAO is able to assign more

**[01:37:06.470] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001314)  
to the Katarungan Caravan and to our outreach services through the DOJ.

**[01:37:11.430] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001315)  
Hopefully, yung mga additional items po natin

**[01:37:13.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001316)  
can be assigned to grassroots work na kailangan na kailangan na mga kababayan natin.

**[01:37:19.960] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001317)  
Yes, Your Honor, we commit to that.

**[01:37:21.960] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001318)  
And we are also serving the DMW.

**[01:37:24.960] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001319)  
And the OWWA.

**[01:37:26.970] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001320)  
Because we are called upon by these two departments and agencies

**[01:37:29.970] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-001321)  
to assist our OFWs abroad, personally by our PAO lawyers.

**[01:37:34.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001322)  
Nako, Mr. Chair, I'm so happy to hear that PAO is going to be working closely

**[01:37:39.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001323)  
with the DOJAC to be able to provide more for kababayans with legal assistance.

**[01:37:44.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001324)  
Mr. Chair, I just want to reiterate my support

**[01:37:47.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001325)  
for the budget of the Department of Justice.

**[01:37:50.010] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001326)  
I feel that this is one of the ways that we can really make our presence felt.

**[01:37:53.910] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001327)  
Para sa mga kababayan natin.

**[01:37:55.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001328)  
Through their legal aid, legal services,

**[01:37:58.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001329)  
and through the protection of our mga kababayan

**[01:38:01.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001330)  
who are affected by scams, fraud, sexual violence, etc.

**[01:38:06.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001331)  
Mr. Chair, with that being said, I also want to point out that

**[01:38:10.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001332)  
I'm glad that I'm able to work with PAO in this capacity

**[01:38:14.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001333)  
because once upon a time, I was a reporter in her office back in 2013.

**[01:38:19.250] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001334)  
So it's good to see that she hasn't given up her drive for public service.

**[01:38:23.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001335)  
Thank you.

**[01:38:26.770] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001336)  
Thank you po, Representative Brian Poe.

**[01:38:30.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001337)  
Next in line, from the minority, Representative Renee Louise Co.

**[01:38:36.946] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001338)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:38:40.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001339)  
here today. At gusto ko lang din pong mag-take off sa

**[01:38:44.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001340)  
line of questioning that was already established no iyung

**[01:38:48.239] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001341)  
essentially paano natin na-insensure naba-balance nabibilang

**[01:38:52.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001342)  
yung convictions given iyung mga cases that we've filed.

**[01:38:56.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001343)  
No, we note that may bagong evidentiary standard for

**[01:38:59.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001344)  
prosecutors since 2024. dati probable cause

**[01:39:03.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001345)  
dapat yung um evidentially threshold bago

**[01:39:07.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001346)  
dumulog sa korte yung kaso pero ngayon it's reasonable certainty

**[01:39:11.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001347)  
of conviction and tama po ba ah kasi this was in your press release

**[01:39:15.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001348)  
as well this was changed because of longstanding

**[01:39:19.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001349)  
challenges in the justice system iyung weak cases that overburden

**[01:39:22.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001350)  
trials and erode public trust. Is this correct?

**[01:39:28.398] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001351)  
Opo. Maraming salamat po sa katanungan. your honor. Ah, tama po kayo na ang

**[01:39:33.119] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001352)  
layunin po nito ay para bago magsampa ng kaso ang sinumang

**[01:39:37.719] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001353)  
fiscal sa Pilipinas, ibig sabihin handa na ang prosecution.

**[01:39:42.440] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001354)  
handa ng prosecution na manalo. At may isa pa hong kadahilanan dito.

**[01:39:46.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001355)  
Ang napansin po kasi natin sa nakaraang kasaysayan natin about

**[01:39:51.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001356)  
filing cases sa mga may kakayanang kasing kumuha ng

**[01:39:55.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001357)  
abogado dahil probable cause yung ano pwede kang maging biktima

**[01:40:00.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001358)  
lang ng pagfa-file ng maraming kaso. Pero dahil dito po sa

**[01:40:05.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001359)  
mas may wider latitude ang ating mga fiskal, ang ating mga prosecutors

**[01:40:10.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001360)  
to fully assess na ang isasampalang kaso ay yung kayang

**[01:40:14.360] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001361)  
ipanalo or malamang mananalo sa korte.

**[01:40:18.280] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-001362)

**[02:16:13.606] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002243)  
Mr. Secretary, can you go over your budget, proposed budget,

**[02:16:17.435] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002244)  
under MOOE for fiscal year 2027?

**[02:16:20.686] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002245)  
There is a sub-expense labeled as other maintenance and operating expenses.

**[02:16:29.756] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002246)

**[02:16:38.644] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002247)  
Under object of expenditure.

**[02:16:40.569] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002248)  
Mr. Secretary.

**[02:17:09.158] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002249)  
For the proposed budget for fiscal year 2027,

**[02:17:16.721] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002250)  
Other maintenance and operating expenses would amount to 114,309,000.

**[02:17:22.523] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002251)  
Mr. Secretary, I'm not asking about the amount.

**[02:17:24.964] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002252)  
I'm asking about the expenses covered by other MOOE.

**[02:17:59.598] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002253)  
This would cover advertising expenses,

**[02:18:02.943] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002254)  
Printing and publication expenses, representation expenses,

**[02:18:07.489] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002255)  
transportation and delivery expenses, rent for buildings and structures and rents for equipment,

**[02:18:14.720] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002256)  
then ICT software subscription and other subscription expenses, Your Honor.

**[02:18:19.747] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002257)  
There is a sub-label similar to other maintenance and operating expenses.

**[02:18:26.453] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002258)  
Can you confirm?

**[02:18:30.665] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002259)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:18:32.605] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002260)  
What possible expenses are covered by this similar?

Similar label, aside from what you have already mentioned.

**[02:18:54.330] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002262)  
Other similar expenses would be [unclear].

**[02:19:12.751] DOJ budget representative** (asr-002263)  
This would basically cover possible increases of those similar objects of expenditure, Your Honor, I would assume.

**[02:19:23.281] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002264)

**[02:19:24.812] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002265)  
To Commissioner Viado.

**[02:19:27.731] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002266)  
Commissioner.

**[02:19:28.445] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002267)  
Good afternoon, sir.

**[02:19:28.760] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002268)  
Good afternoon.

**[02:19:29.322] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002269)  
Commissioner, could you explain what the border,

**[02:19:32.188] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002270)  
border management information system, or BMIS, is?

**[02:19:39.278] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002271)  
Sir, good afternoon.

**[02:19:40.810] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002272)  
Thank you very much, Congressman Ty, for giving us the opportunity to explain and to build trust with the Filipino people concerning this matter.

**[02:19:50.808] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002273)  
The large-scale border security system is needed at this point

**[02:19:58.670] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002274)  
because this pertains to a technology-driven, AI-driven,

**[02:20:04.697] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002275)  
biometric-driven kind of border control, Your Honor.

**[02:20:08.930] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002276)  
The concept really is to extend our borders beyond the actual physical border of the Philippines.

**[02:20:15.381] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002277)  
We plan to institute a system wherein even if a passenger is in another country and is not qualified to enter the Philippines,

**[02:20:26.468] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002278)  
he will be prevented from doing so using this technology-driven system, Your Honor.

**[02:20:33.160] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002279)  
What is the total project?

**[02:20:34.741] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002280)  
What is the total project cost of BMIS?

**[02:20:37.345] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002281)  
The total project cost—this is a PPP project, Your Honor.

**[02:20:41.678] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002282)  
The total project cost for the first five years will be shouldered by the private proponent, amounting to about 10.7 billion, Your Honor.

**[02:20:51.423] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002283)  
But in the next 20 years, they will put in additional capital and maintenance expenses, amounting to about 52.5 billion, Your Honor.

**[02:21:03.873] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002284)  
How much is that?

**[02:21:04.665] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002285)  
How much is the national government share?

**[02:21:06.218] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002286)  
The government share would amount to, for passengers, the cost would be about $4, Your Honor.

**[02:21:14.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002287)  
$3.80 will go to the private proponent and $0.20 will go to the Philippine government, Your Honor.

**[02:21:19.482] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002288)  
Is there no allocations made to BMIS by the national government?

**[02:21:26.114] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002289)  
At this point, Your Honor, the intention really is to adopt a stepladder approach.

**[02:21:34.281] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002290)  
Any budget that has been allocated by government at this point, sad to say, in a way, is lacking.

**[02:21:45.512] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002291)  
So this PVP project will make up for any differences or any shortfalls that we might have as far as border control is concerned, Your Honor.

**[02:21:56.742] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002292)  
Based on your 2024 GAA, there's an allocation of four-point-something billion for BMIS.

**[02:22:04.734] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002293)  
Can you kindly confirm?

**[02:22:06.732] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002294)  
For the year 2024, I think it was 1.9 billion, Your Honor, for the e-Gates project.

**[02:22:17.667] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002295)  
So there is a government fund used for the BMIS project?

**[02:22:23.118] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002296)  
That's correct, Your Honor, for the E-Gates project.

**[02:22:26.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002297)  
Initially, it was funded at around 1.9 billion, but eventually it was reduced.

**[02:22:32.185] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002298)  
The winning bidder submitted a proposal.

**[02:22:35.208] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002299)  
Which was eventually accepted, which amounted to 1.5 billion.

**[02:22:39.238] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002300)  
But, Your Honor, this only pertains to the E-Gates project.

**[02:22:43.268] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002301)  
It does not pertain to any total border control system, which the private proponent is proposing, Your Honor.

**[02:22:50.320] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002302)  
So the total appropriation release of 1.5 billion, as you mentioned, how much has been obligated?

**[02:22:57.526] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002303)  
And how much has been disbursed?

**[02:22:59.585] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002304)  
I will confer with my colleague, Your Honor.

**[02:23:01.019] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002305)  
The allotment originally, Your Honor, was 1.9 billion.

**[02:23:09.119] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002306)  
But what has been obligated is 1.5 billion, Your Honor.

**[02:23:13.428] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002307)  
But DBM released the SARO corresponding to the 1.9 billion.

**[02:23:22.045] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002308)  
So how much still remains unobligated?

**[02:23:29.774] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002309)  
Your Honor, at this point, what is unobligated is the 1.3, which is the second phase of the project.

**[02:23:36.625] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002310)  
Mr. Commissioner, I'm asking you first, the first phase.

**[02:23:39.638] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002311)  
Don't jump on the second phase. I haven't asked you yet on the second phase.

**[02:23:42.650] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002312)  
It has been fully obligated, Your Honor.

**[02:23:45.687] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002315)  
How much has been released?

**[02:23:46.753] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002316)  
Your Honor, as stated, the original amount was 1.9, but we have a savings of 400.

**[02:24:02.160] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002317)  
So what has been released is 1.5 billion, Your Honor.

**[02:24:04.115] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002318)  
How about for fiscal year 2025?

**[02:24:05.687] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002319)  
How much of the Bureau's appropriations remain unused for this particular project?

**[02:24:12.940] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002320)  
1.3, Your Honor.

**[02:24:15.785] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002321)  
How much has been obligated?

**[02:24:27.976] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002322)  
Your Honor, it has not yet been obligated because the terms of reference

**[02:24:32.532] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002323)  
for the phase of this project will still be transmitted to the Department of Justice

**[02:24:37.088] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002324)  
for further review, Your Honor.

**[02:24:38.911] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002325)  
But it has been released?

**[02:24:40.818] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002326)  
The SARO has been released by the DBM?

**[02:24:42.725] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002327)  
Yes, Your Honor. It has been released.

**[02:24:44.632] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002328)  
So can you go over your 2025 budget?

**[02:24:49.062] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002329)  
Based on the expenditure program appropriation by obligation,

**[02:24:53.492] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002330)  
you have an unobligated allotment of 4 billion.

**[02:24:57.922] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002331)  
Can you please state what these unobligated

**[02:25:02.352] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002332)  
allotments are?

**[02:25:05.675] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002333)  
Primarily, Your Honor, it pertains to the E-Gates project.

**[02:25:09.774] Bureau of Immigration budget representative** (asr-002334)  
It's unobligated.

**[02:25:11.142] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002335)  
You mentioned a while ago that you have obligated 1.5 billion.

**[02:25:16.241] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002336)  
The only unobligated amount is only 400 million.

**[02:25:19.300] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002337)  
How come you have 4.071 billion unobligated in 2025?

**[02:25:25.418] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002338)  
Your Honor, if I may read the summary, Your Honor.

**[02:25:29.437] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002339)  
The implementation schedule for

**[02:25:32.450] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002340)  
the E-Gates projects Phase 1, 2, and 3

**[02:25:35.464] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002341)  
was affected by circumstances beyond the Bureau's control.

**[02:25:38.478] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002342)  
So Phase 1, which was funded under the Fiscal Year 2024 GAA

**[02:25:42.497] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002343)  
in the amount of 1.5 billion,

**[02:25:45.511] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002344)  
was timely subjected to competitive bidding

**[02:25:47.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002345)  
but was declared a failure of bidding.

**[02:25:49.529] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002346)  
It necessitated, Your Honor, a rebidding process.

**[02:25:52.543] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002347)  
And during the rebidding process,

**[02:25:54.552] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002348)  
the Bureau received the donation of E-Gates

**[02:25:56.562] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002349)  
from the new NAIA Infrastructure Corporation

**[02:25:59.576] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002350)  
for NAIA Terminals 1 and 3,

**[02:26:01.585] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002351)  
which required the revision

**[02:26:03.594] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002352)  
of the approved technical specifications and deployment plan

**[02:26:06.608] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002353)  
to avoid any duplication of procurement

**[02:26:09.622] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002354)  
and maximize the utilization of government resources.

**[02:26:12.636] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002355)  
Consequently, the contract was awarded in December 2025.

**[02:26:16.654] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002356)  
Now, as the project is being implemented in phases,

**[02:26:19.668] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002357)  
the delay in Phase 1 necessarily affected

**[02:26:21.677] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002358)  
the procurement schedule of Phase 2.

**[02:26:24.691] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002359)  
For Fiscal Year 2025, amounting to 1.3 billion.

**[02:26:28.710] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002360)  
And Phase 3, Fiscal Year 2026,

**[02:26:30.719] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002361)  
amounting to 565 million.

**[02:26:33.733] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002362)  
The Bureau has since completed the required revisions

**[02:26:37.752] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002363)  
revisions and is expediting, Your Honor,

**[02:26:39.761] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002364)  
the remaining procurement activities.

**[02:26:42.775] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002365)  
And the Bureau assures that it will be able to award Phase 2

**[02:26:45.789] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002366)  
and other Fiscal Year 2025 and 2026 funded projects

**[02:26:49.807] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002367)  
within the current year

**[02:26:50.812] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002368)  
and commits to fully implementing the modernization program

**[02:26:53.826] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-002369)  
as scheduled, Your Honor.

**[02:26:55.835] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002370)  
Last question, Cong. Allan.

**[02:26:58.163] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002371)  
I have...

**[02:27:00.205] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002372)  
I'll do my second round, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:27:02.247] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002373)  
Okay.

**[02:27:03.085] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-002374)  
Yes, sir. Thank you.

**[02:27:08.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002375)  
Next in line, from the majority,

**[02:27:17.453] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002378)

**[02:27:21.482] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002379)

**[02:27:23.496] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002380)

**[02:27:25.510] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002381)  
Hypothetical.

**[02:27:27.525] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002382)  
It pertains to the right of the government

**[02:27:31.553] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002383)  
to expropriate private property

**[02:27:33.568] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002384)

**[02:27:35.582] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002385)  
Now, if an agency exercises

**[02:27:38.603] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002386)  
the power of expropriation,

**[02:27:42.632] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002387)  
acquires private property

**[02:27:44.646] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002388)  
for public use,

**[02:27:46.661] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002389)  
but it's a private corporation

**[02:27:48.675] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002390)  
pays the value,

**[02:27:51.696] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002391)  
is there a proper exercise

**[02:27:53.711] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002392)  
of the power of expropriation,

**[02:27:55.725] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002393)  
Secretary?

**[02:28:03.359] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002394)  
Elements for the proper exercise

**[02:28:06.512] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002395)  
of expropriate...

**[02:28:08.614] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002396)  
the right of eminent domain.

**[02:28:10.716] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002397)  
One, public purpose.

**[02:28:12.818] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002398)  
Second, it's a...

**[02:28:14.919] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002399)  
it's a...

**[02:28:17.021] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002400)  
public purpose of the ano, no?

**[02:28:19.123] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002401)  
And it would be utilizing public funds.

**[02:28:22.276] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002402)  
But, Your Honor,

**[02:28:24.378] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002403)  
as in prior projects, no?

**[02:28:26.480] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002404)  
There have been projects that

**[02:28:28.582] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002405)  
there are engagements by the government

**[02:28:30.684] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002406)  
entering into

**[02:28:32.786] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002407)  
various contractual agreements

**[02:28:35.939] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002408)  
with the private sector

**[02:28:38.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002409)  
and it would be possible, no,

**[02:28:40.142] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002410)  
it would be possible

**[02:28:41.193] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002411)  
that private funds might be used

**[02:28:44.346] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002412)  
to procure portions

**[02:28:46.448] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002413)  
of the right-of-way, Your Honor.

**[02:28:48.560] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002414)  
So, it's possible that you can use

**[02:28:53.288] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002415)  
private funds to pay

**[02:28:55.160] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002416)  
just compensation?

**[02:28:57.969] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002417)  
It's...

**[02:28:59.841] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002418)  
I'll be direct with the Secretary.

**[02:29:05.702] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002419)  
I raised this in the 18th Congress.

**[02:29:08.511] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002420)  
Kasi sa amin,

**[02:29:09.447] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002421)  
NIA exercises expropriation.

**[02:29:12.256] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002422)  
They acquired property

**[02:29:14.128] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002423)  
of farmers around

**[02:29:16.000] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002424)  
the Magat Dam

**[02:29:17.873] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002425)  
for public use.

**[02:29:19.745] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002426)  
But the one who paid it

**[02:29:20.681] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002427)  
is a private corporation.

**[02:29:22.554] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002428)  
Pwede ba 'yon?

**[02:29:29.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002429)  
I don't think that's within the realm

**[02:29:32.118] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002430)  
of our legal system, Your Honor.

**[02:29:35.354] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002431)  
Private funds cannot be used

**[02:29:37.512] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002432)  
in that manner, Your Honor.

**[02:29:39.680] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002433)  
That, in that case,

**[02:29:42.085] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002434)  
is the private...

**[02:29:44.491] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002435)  
is the public...

**[02:29:46.896] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002436)  
public corporation

**[02:29:48.099] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002437)  
or the public entity

**[02:29:50.505] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002438)  
liable for anything?

**[02:29:52.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002439)  
Yes. We have to check the circumstances, Your Honor. Yes, possible po, Your Honor.

**[02:29:59.439] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002440)  
What would be the liability, any administrative or criminal liability?

**[02:30:09.399] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002441)  
Your Honor, if you may, we need additional factual circumstances to make a determination on that matter, Your Honor.

**[02:30:20.800] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002442)  
Well, I'm laying the facts. The facts are simple.

**[02:30:27.412] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002443)  
They raised the level of the dam to the level that it acquired private properties, supposedly for irrigation.

**[02:30:43.698] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002444)  
But when they were supposed to pay for the just compensation, it was a private corporation who wanted to pay and paid them, pero hindi patas.

**[02:30:59.773] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002445)  
Is there anything wrong in that case?

**[02:31:10.403] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002446)  
Secretary? Ano 'yung right—anong ROW 'yun?

**[02:31:11.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002447)  
Under the existing ROW Act, Your Honor, if I get the briefing right, what will be used for expropriation should be public funds.

**[02:31:22.586] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002448)  
So any violation of that law would make the person liable if it violates that particular law, Your Honor.

**[02:31:32.160] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002449)  
The agency, the agency—

**[02:31:34.566] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002450)  
NIA violated the law.

**[02:31:38.926] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002451)  
What is the crime? What is the liability of NIA, administratively or criminally?

**[02:31:48.488] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002452)  
That's what I'm asking.

**[02:31:50.924] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002453)  
Kasi hindi inayos 'to, eh. Oo, hindi kasi...

**[02:31:53.279] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002454)  
Your Honor, we, my apologies, but if you will allow the department, we can give you the proper briefing on that.

**[02:32:03.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002455)  
We can give you the report on that.

**[02:32:06.200] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002456)  
But for any action of an individual, it can arise...

**[02:32:10.040] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002459)  
It can arise to criminal or administrative liability.

**[02:32:13.619] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002460)  
That's possible.

**[02:32:14.607] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002461)  
And also from a criminal act, a civil liability will also ensue.

**[02:32:18.957] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002462)  
So based on the particular act, Your Honor, we need to see with more particularity the circumstances, Your Honor.

**[02:32:27.023] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002463)  
My apologies, Your Honor.

**[02:32:28.200] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002464)  
Okay, I will just ask perhaps your office for your legal opinion.

**[02:32:32.520] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002465)  
Because the farmers are complaining.

**[02:32:35.560] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002466)  
I brought this out in the 18th Congress.

**[02:32:38.240] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002467)  
I told them.

**[02:32:38.800] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002468)  
Ayusin ninyo 'yan. Pero hindi inayos hanggang ngayon. So the farmers are coming back. Ang ginagawa nila kasi, binabarat 'yung iba, binabayaran 'yung iba. Hindi naman puwede 'yun, 'di ba?

**[02:32:54.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002469)  
Yes, Your Honor. The ultimate basis for everything is fairness and justice.

**[02:32:58.770] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002470)  
So flowing from that, if there's something unfair, probably there's a violation of the law.

**[02:33:04.481] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002471)  
So if it's an unfair act, then there's a law violated.

**[02:33:12.381] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002472)  
So we'll just coordinate with your office, Your Honor.

**[02:33:15.439] Rep. Solomon R. Chungalao** (asr-002473)

**[02:42:46.660] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002734)

**[02:42:52.973] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002736)

**[02:42:54.741] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002737)

**[02:43:18.062] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002749)

**[02:43:22.706] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002751)

**[02:43:24.713] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002752)

**[02:43:28.767] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002754)

**[02:43:30.006] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002755)

**[02:43:31.836] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002756)

**[02:43:33.962] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002757)

**[02:43:40.455] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002760)

**[02:43:42.167] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002761)  
At pangarap ko sana, bawat embahada,

**[02:43:44.863] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002762)  
hindi lang may NBI agent doon,

**[02:43:46.771] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002763)  
may PAO lawyer din, tutulong

**[02:43:48.405] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002764)  
sa ating OFWs, makipag-coordinate

**[02:43:50.313] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002765)  
sa counterparts, para

**[02:43:51.927] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002766)  
mabawasan ang nakukulong

**[02:43:53.836] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002767)  
ng mga OFWs po.

**[02:43:55.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002768)  
Additional 500 million po ang hinahanap ninyo?

**[02:43:58.499] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002769)  
Hindi naman ho, pero

**[02:43:59.719] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002770)  
kung ano lang pong kaya sana ng gobyerno,

**[02:44:02.238] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002771)  
kung napupondohan yung iba,

**[02:44:03.910] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002772)  
tapos napupunta lamang sa scandal,

**[02:44:05.937] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002773)  
sana yun talagang nagwo-work po

**[02:44:08.338] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002774)
