**[02:44:10.502] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002775)  
Subok po ang NBI.

**[02:44:11.939] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002776)  
Tsaka mga kasama ko sa department, mga piskal,

**[02:44:14.576] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002777)  
mahal ko po sila dahil mga dati

**[02:44:16.110] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002778)  
PAO ko po karamihan. 80% ng mga

**[02:44:17.842] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002779)  
prosecutors galing po sa akin.

**[02:44:19.849] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002780)  
Sa PAO, naging mentor po nila ako.

**[02:44:22.683] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002781)  
Kaya nalulungkot po ako

**[02:44:23.981] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002782)  
pag nakikita ko may bawas po ang budget

**[02:44:25.870] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002783)  
ng mga ahensya ng DOJ.

**[02:44:27.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002784)  
Sige po. Hayaan ninyo, susuportahan namin

**[02:44:30.023] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002785)  
yung pagdagdag ng

**[02:44:31.175] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002786)  
budget ng PAO.

**[02:44:33.760] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-002787)  
Opo.

**[02:44:37.990] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002788)  
Sa LRA, andyan po ba

**[02:44:40.409] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002789)  
ang LRA?

**[02:44:42.597] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002790)  
Sir, napansin ko yung support for operations nyo,

**[02:44:44.995] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002791)  
lumobo ng 86.15%.

**[02:44:47.414] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002792)  
Ano po dahilan para dyan?

**[02:44:58.720] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002793)  
Sir, iyong pong support for services na nasa ICTD po namin, sa improvement po ng aming services on technology ho.

**[02:45:07.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002794)  
With the improvement in services, gaganda ho ba at bibilis iyong pag-i-issue ng mga titulo na kinakailangan?

**[02:45:13.439] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002795)  
Inaasahan ho namin sa pagkakaroon ng makabagong teknolohiya, maaari ho natin mapabilis na ho talaga.

**[02:45:19.437] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002796)  
At maiwasan na rin ho ang pagkakaroon ng mga pekeng titulo rin ho.

**[02:45:23.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002797)  
Kasi isang hinaing doon po ng marami, lalo na ng mga magsasaka na naghihintay ng CLOA, na mabagal po iyong pag-i-issue ng mga titulo.

**[02:45:33.571] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002798)  
Kasi may mga anomalya ring nangyayari. Ano hong ginagawa ng ahensya para imbestigahan ang mga ito at mapabilis ang paglabas ng titulo?

**[02:45:41.399] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002799)  
Sa usapin po ng mga CLOA, kami po ay mahigpit na nakikipag-ugnayan ho sa Department of Agrarian Reform.

**[02:45:51.751] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002800)  
Sa opisina ni Secretary Conrado Estrella po.

**[02:45:53.684] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002801)  
So unti-unti ho namin na natatapos at naaayos ang mga issue sa usapin sa mga CLOWA po.

**[02:46:03.472] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002802)  
At kami po ngayon ay nagsasagawa ho ng programa para mapabilis na ho ang distribution ng ating CLOA.

**[02:46:12.373] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002803)  
Meron pong ina-undertake na programa si Secretary Conrado na amin pong tinutulungan, ang DAR SPLIT Program po.

**[02:46:21.113] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002804)  
So sa programang iyon ay iyong mga collective CLOA, nababasag ho natin into individual CLOAs and napagdi-distribute po sa ating mga agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[02:46:33.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002805)  
Siguro naman ho, susukatin ninyo iyong bilis kung iyan po ang target ninyo—susukatin ninyo kung naging matagumpay ang inyong inisyatibo. Tama po?

**[02:46:41.960] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002806)  
Opo, hindi lang pong bilis pati po yung bilang po ng ating mga titulo na naipamimigay po.

**[02:46:48.217] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-002807)  
Ang target ho natin ay isang milyong titulo ang maibibigay natin sa ating mga kababayan, at sa aking tantiya nasa 200,000 na ho ang naibibigay po.

**[02:46:59.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002808)  
Next question po. Kaya para sa last set of questions, patapos na ho.

**[02:47:05.006] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002809)  
Ay tapos na, last question.

**[02:47:07.399] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002811)  
So, to the OSG, to Solicitor General Berberabe, I note that lumaki po iyong inyong budget for General Administration Support Services.

**[02:47:17.597] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002812)  
I know previously...

**[02:47:19.523] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002813)  
Nagkaroon kayo ng problema sa caseload, tama? Pero naremedyuhan ninyo. To what extent po iyong dagdag na budget ninyo ay bilang pagtugon sa mga problemang babanggitin ninyo sa amin ngayon?

**[02:47:32.640] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002814)  
Okay. Salamat po sa attention po sa OSG. With regard po sa increase ng budget, substantial portion po nito ay mapupunta sa building po ng OSG.

**[02:47:48.520] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002815)  
So sa ngayon po, yung OSG main building, 49 years old na po ito. Ilang dekada na po na pinag-uusapan. In fact, kakalahati na lang po ang occupants ng OSG sapagkat medyo kahit po dumadaan lang ay mga sasakyan para pong may lindol sa loob ng OSG.

**[02:48:05.160] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002816)  
So iilan na lang po kaming opisina na natitira sa OSG main.

**[02:48:15.200] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002818)  
Ang unang taon ng installment. Ang sinuggest po sa amin ng DBM Secretary at saka ng DOF Secretary, imbes po na bumili o magpagawa from scratch, ay humanap po ng building na ready-made at lease-to-own po.

**[02:48:32.840] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002819)  
So dadaan po kami sa GSIS, Government Financial Institution, sila po ang bibili at kami naman po ay magbabayad ng sampung taon.

**[02:48:44.120] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002820)  
So iyon po sana ang aming pakiusap na kung ma-approve po sa GAA, iyong kasama na rin sa proposal ng NEP, ay matutuloy po, mabibigyan kami ng MYCA at makakalipat po, sapagkat 2022 pa po ang report na ang structural integrity po ng building ay talagang tagilid na po ang estado.

**[02:49:05.800] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-002821)  
Pero yun naman po ay ibebenta ang building na iyon at mapupunta po ang pagbebentahan sa Bureau of Treasury.

**[02:49:14.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002822)  
Thank you, Cong. Tony.

**[02:49:18.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002823)  
Next in line from the minority, Representative Sarah Elago.

**[02:49:24.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002824)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[02:49:26.903] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002825)  
We in Gabriela Women's Party likewise express grave concern over the proposed massive budget cuts in the budget of the Special Enforcement and Protection Subprogram,

**[02:49:38.908] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002826)  
particularly in anti-trafficking in persons enforcement, anti-cybercrime enforcement, as well as the NCC-OSAEC-CSAEM.

**[02:49:45.415] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002828)  
Now, Mr. Chair, may we know the 2027 proposed funding for these particular programs?

**[02:49:52.379] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002829)  
Natalakay po kanina kung magkano yung gusto nating ma-restore na cuts, pero gusto ko lang din ho malaman kung magkano ba yung kinakailangan para maabot yung mga kinakailangan nilang magawa para sa susunod na taon.

**[02:50:08.967] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002830)  
Lalong-lalo na hindi naman po bumababa ang mga kaso.

**[02:50:13.641] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002831)  
Sa katunayan, Mr. Chair,

**[02:50:16.023] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002832)  
Marami ring mga kaso na hindi na talaga nagtutuloy doon sa mismong pagfa-file ng complaint dahil sa laganap pa rin na victim blaming, shaming, o yung takot na wala rin namang mananagot kapag sila ay nagsumbong.

**[02:50:33.069] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002833)  
May we hear, Mr. Chair, from the DOJ or those in charge in the particular programs?

**[02:50:40.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002834)  
If I may ask, Asec. Michelle, can you elucidate?

**[02:50:45.880] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002835)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:50:48.365] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002836)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:50:51.495] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002837)  
Ma'am, for 2026, if I may just clarify, the budget of the NCC-OSAEC-CSAEM Secretariat for 2026 is a total of 111.5 million.

**[02:51:06.236] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002838)  
In the 2027 NEP, it's only 35.6 million.

**[02:51:11.946] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002839)  
So it is our proposal or request that

**[02:51:17.657] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002840)  
we increase this by 75.9 million to restore to the 2026 levels.

**[02:51:25.947] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002841)  
For the Inter-Agency Council Against Trafficking Secretariat, in 2026, the total budget of the IACAT Secretariat is 119 million.

**[02:51:38.753] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002842)  
For the 2027 NEP, it's down to 70 million.

**[02:51:45.108] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002843)  
So we're requesting...

**[02:51:47.594] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002844)  
for more or less 50 million, or 49 million to be exact.

**[02:51:55.884] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002845)  
And for the Office of Cybercrime, in 2026, it had a total budget of 102.2 million.

**[02:52:06.755] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002846)  
For the 2027 NEP, it's reduced to 12.273 million.

**[02:52:13.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002847)  
12.273

**[02:52:14.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002848)  
million. So yung 90% po yung na-reduce from 2026.

**[02:52:18.118] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002849)  
Mr. Chair, mas mababa pa

**[02:52:20.617] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002850)  
pala, no? Mas malalim pa pala yung mga binawas pagdating sa mga

**[02:52:25.844] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002851)  
programa na ito. Ibig sabihin, yung original proposed budget ninyo ay pareho lang ng 2026 GAA o mas mataas pa?

**[02:52:34.200] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002852)  
As to your ask, Mr. Chair, for the original proposed budget for

**[02:52:41.619] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002853)  
2027: for the original proposed budget po, we were constrained to limit our proposals, Madam

**[02:52:50.754] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002854)  
Chair. However, we want to take this opportunity to impress that the real operational need of these units

**[02:53:01.608] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002855)  
are at the level of the same 2026 budget.

**[02:53:07.319] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002856)  
Mr. Chair, we know why

**[02:53:10.290] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002857)  
the

**[02:53:11.324] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002858)  
bodies

**[02:53:12.358] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002859)  
in charge are limited to impose a particular ceiling based on the 2026 GAA on their

**[02:53:20.655] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002860)  
proposals. Hindi ba dapat na, kumbaga, kung ano yung mga kailangan at yung mga dapat para magawa

**[02:53:29.436] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002861)  
yung trabaho ninyo, iyon

**[02:53:31.922] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002862)  
yung ipo-propose natin? Anyhow, meron pa namang pagdadaanan na

**[02:53:36.830] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002863)  
DBM, no? Para, kumbaga, kung ano lang yung batay sa utilization, kung

**[02:53:43.190] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002864)  
ano lang yung batay sa absorptive capacity, iyon lang yung maaaring maibigay.

**[02:53:49.560] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002865)  
Thank you, ma'am, for that question and for echoing our concern as well. Given din po

**[02:53:57.130] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002866)  
yung limitation on the absorptive capacity, as was raised a while ago, na-mention na

**[02:54:03.822] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002867)  
po namin na kailangan ding dagdagan yung ability to conduct timely and reliable procurement processes, as well as

**[02:54:12.269] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002868)  
yung need po for additional plantilla

**[02:54:15.451] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002869)  
positions. Because the

**[02:54:17.318] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002870)  
NCC-OSAEC-CSAEM, IACAT, and OOC budgets are part of a bigger budget po under

**[02:54:23.571] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002871)  
the Office of the Secretary. So kaya

**[02:54:27.192] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002872)  
po constrained din po kami in relation doon sa kabuuang

**[02:54:32.129] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002873)  
budget na hinihingi po under the Office of the

**[02:54:36.628] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002874)  
Secretary. But we

**[02:54:38.494] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002875)  
appreciate and take this opportunity to underscore the real

**[02:54:42.993] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002876)  
operational need

**[02:54:44.420] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002877)  
ng mga divisions

**[02:54:46.286] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-002878)  
and units po na ito.

**[02:54:49.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002879)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[02:54:50.849] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002880)  
Hingin na lang ho natin yung original

**[02:54:54.336] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002881)  
proposal ng mga nabanggit

**[02:54:56.565] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002882)  
na subprograms, mga particular bodies na nangangailangan

**[02:55:00.053] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002883)  
at may trabaho pagdating sa special enforcement and protection. Ngayon,

**[02:55:04.799] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002884)  
Mr. Chair, nais ko

**[02:55:07.028] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002885)  
hong magtanong tungkol sa pag-decongest ng NBP. Nabigyan tayo kanina ng update that

**[02:55:13.033] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002886)  
BuCor is still on track for

**[02:55:16.101] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002887)  
its 2028 target for the decongestion of the New Bilibid Prison.

**[02:55:21.267] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002888)  
May we know, Mr. Chair, if there are any

**[02:55:25.594] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002889)  
complaints or concerns with regard

**[02:55:28.242] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002890)  
to the transfer raised before the Bureau? For instance, Mr. Chair, we've

**[02:55:33.828] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002891)  
received reports na, kumbaga,

**[02:55:36.056] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002892)  
ang request po sana ay mas malapit doon

**[02:55:39.964] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002893)  
sa mga lugar

**[02:55:41.772] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002894)  
kung saan ang kanilang mga minamahal at

**[02:55:45.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002895)  
kanilang mga pamilya, doon ho dapat mailipat yung mga

**[02:55:49.587] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002896)  
PDL. May natanggap ho ba na

**[02:55:52.655] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002897)  
mga ganitong tipo ng concern, at ano

**[02:55:56.142] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002898)  
ang ginawa ng BuCor sa pagtugon sa mga ito?

**[02:56:00.479] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002899)  
With the permission of Secretary...

**[02:56:04.200] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-002900)  
Yes, you may.

**[02:56:04.810] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002901)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:56:07.040] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002902)  
Ang ginagawa po ngayon sa BuCor,

**[02:56:10.100] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002903)  
for as long as we can hold on doon sa may nagre-request

**[02:56:15.670] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002904)  
na huwag muna silang ilipat, they are

**[02:56:19.149] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002905)  
given, they are allowed. Opo. And

**[02:56:22.209] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002906)  
then,

**[02:56:23.177] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002907)  
of course, ang time frame po natin is 2028,

**[02:56:27.493] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002908)  
where masasara na po natin itong New

**[02:56:30.971] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002909)  
Bilibid. That's why nabigyan po tayo ng

**[02:56:34.450] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002910)  
ating gobyerno ng about

**[02:56:36.673] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002911)  
5 billion to spread

**[02:56:38.897] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002912)  
the regionalization. Meron na po

**[02:56:41.538] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002913)  
tayo sa

**[02:56:42.925] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002914)  
Davao,

**[02:56:43.893] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002915)  
sa Zamboanga, Leyte,

**[02:56:45.698] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002916)  
sa Mindoro, and

**[02:56:47.504] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002917)  
then Palawan. And then,

**[02:56:49.727] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002918)  
of course, ang plano po kasi namin sa mga PDL ay bigyan talaga

**[02:56:55.716] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002919)  
sila ng bagong

**[02:56:57.521] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002920)  
buhay, bagong pag-asa.

**[02:56:59.326] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002921)  
And itong

**[02:57:00.712] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002922)  
Palawan po, 32,000 hectares. Ang balak po natin diyan

**[02:57:05.028] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002923)  
gawing PEZA zone para lahat

**[02:57:07.669] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-002924)  
ng mga PDL na

**[03:14:56.279] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003400)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[03:14:57.581] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003401)  
Good afternoon, Secretary Vida and to the DOJ family.

**[03:15:01.558] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003402)  
I'd just like to follow through, if I may, Mr. Secretary,

**[03:15:04.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003403)  
on the issues that were raised, particularly on online sexual abuse and exploitation of children,

**[03:15:10.467] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003404)  
kasama po iyong CSEAEM at iyong ating massive budget cuts.

**[03:15:13.439] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003405)  
May I direct the questions po? Diretso na po ba kay Asec. Lapuz?

**[03:15:17.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003406)  
Yes, of course, Your Honor.

**[03:15:19.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003407)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[03:15:22.233] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003408)  
Secretary Vida, given the massive cuts in your programs and budget items,

**[03:15:31.199] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003409)  
what does this mean in terms of you doing your job?

**[03:15:35.390] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003410)  
Kasi nakita ko po ang range po ng cuts ay from 71%, 58%,

**[03:15:42.696] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003411)  
14%. So ano po ang magiging epekto nito sa ating trabaho?

**[03:15:46.960] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003412)  
Thank you, Your Honor, for the question.

**[03:15:50.202] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003413)  
First of all,

**[03:15:51.080] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003414)  
mapapabagal iyong national response po natin against OSAEC and against trafficking.

**[03:15:59.528] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003415)  
Meron po kaming nakalatag ngayon na National Strategic Action Plan.

**[03:16:04.350] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003416)  
It's a four-year plan.

**[03:16:06.154] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003417)  
And with the massive budget cuts,

**[03:16:09.074] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003418)  
meron pong mga priority programs na maaaring hindi ma-implement.

**[03:16:15.523] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003419)  
And alam naman po natin, pag sinasabi nating strategic action plan,

**[03:16:20.482] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003420)  
kailangan naka-step by step iyan.

**[03:16:22.560] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003421)  
Hindi ka maka-proceed to other important programs

**[03:16:26.284] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003422)  
unless you build the foundations first.

**[03:16:28.597] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003423)  
Ang maganda po dito, despite limitations in resources,

**[03:16:33.439] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003424)  
nakabuo na po tayo ng magandang mechanism for local task forces

**[03:16:38.865] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003425)  
to do responses on the ground.

**[03:16:42.924] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003426)  
Pero kulang na kulang po iyong capability by these local task forces

**[03:16:48.930] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003427)  
because,

**[03:16:50.815] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003428)  
una, kulang po iyong budget allocations.

**[03:16:55.185] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003429)  
At the same time,

**[03:16:56.924] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003430)  
fragmented pa rin po iyong response.

**[03:17:00.259] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003431)  
Kailangan itawid iyong national program

**[03:17:03.199] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003432)  
at i-localize po siya sa mga strategic local governments

**[03:17:09.040] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003433)  
and local task forces that are already in place.

**[03:17:12.395] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003434)  
However, if we don't have enough resources

**[03:17:15.377] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003435)  
to support the operations of these mechanisms,

**[03:17:18.276] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003436)  
mapapabagal po iyong rescue operations.

**[03:17:21.880] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003437)  
Important po iyong rescue operations

**[03:17:25.681] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003438)  
because this leads to victim identification.

**[03:17:29.377] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003439)  
If we identify victims,

**[03:17:31.214] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003440)  
then we know how to get to them,

**[03:17:34.445] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003441)  
where they are,

**[03:17:35.395] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003442)  
who they are.

**[03:17:36.177] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003443)  
We can rescue them.

**[03:17:37.507] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003444)  
We can take a child out of an abusive situation

**[03:17:42.280] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003445)  
the soonest possible time.

**[03:17:43.800] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003446)  
So we are really advocating po to, at the very least,

**[03:17:47.459] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003447)  
restore the budget levels of NCC-OSAEC and IACAT to the 2026 levels.

**[03:17:52.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003448)  
Mr. Chair, I am extremely alarmed

**[03:17:56.529] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003449)  
at the scenario we're looking at

**[03:17:58.935] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003450)  
to the budget levels for the special enforcement

**[03:18:02.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003451)  
and protection sub-program

**[03:18:04.971] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003452)  
remain at the current levels as proposed.

**[03:18:08.127] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003453)  
Ibig sabihin po nito, parang sinasabi natin,

**[03:18:10.869] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003454)  
okay lang na hindi ma-rescue ang mga biktima.

**[03:18:14.479] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003455)  
Okay lang na bumagal ang ating proseso

**[03:18:17.487] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003456)  
para tiyakin na maililigtas sila.

**[03:18:19.650] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003457)  
We usually say that the budget

**[03:18:21.381] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003458)  
is the most concrete,

**[03:18:22.403] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003459)  
expression of an administration's priority.

**[03:18:25.844] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003460)  
So ibig sabihin po ba nito dito sa massive budget cuts na ito,

**[03:18:29.521] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003461)  
hindi priority ng gobyerno ang pagliligtas sa mga biktima ng OSAEC.

**[03:18:35.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003462)  
Hindi priority ng gobyerno ang pag-rescue sa lalong madaling panahon

**[03:18:40.591] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003463)  
sa pinakamabilis na paraan ito pong mga biktima po.

**[03:18:45.015] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003464)  
Nito pong napakalaking, masasabi nating, sindikato

**[03:18:51.405] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003465)  
na nag-ooperate.

**[03:18:52.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003466)  
Earlier you mentioned that now we're not just talking about individuals

**[03:18:55.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003467)  
but we're also talking about how AI is being used.

**[03:18:59.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003468)  
With this massive budget cuts,

**[03:19:00.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003469)  
how will you be able to respond to this much bigger problem?

**[03:19:05.880] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003470)  
We need to catch up, sir, on our mechanisms.

**[03:19:10.004] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003471)  
Dahil nga po, constantly evolving

**[03:19:12.581] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003472)  
and nagiging mas complicated yung modus operandi

**[03:19:16.705] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003473)  
ng mga gumagawa po ng illegal recruitment, for example,

**[03:19:20.829] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003474)  
or grooming,

**[03:19:21.695] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003475)  
na-mention po kanina ni Secretary of Justice

**[03:19:25.303] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003476)  
iyong groomers behind iyong mga nihilistic violent extremism incidents po.

**[03:19:32.520] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003477)  
Now we don't even need an actual person conducting the enticement of our youth.

**[03:19:39.394] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003478)  
It could be an AI agent doing that.

**[03:19:43.813] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003479)  
And if we don't put in place the foundations of our IT infrastructure

**[03:19:50.687] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003480)  
for the proper, timely, and commensurate response to the threat,

**[03:19:57.561] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003481)  
the AI po being used to perpetrate these crimes,

**[03:20:01.980] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003482)  
that is the threat.

**[03:20:03.453] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003483)  
And we need to provide for a corresponding response to the threat.

**[03:20:11.800] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003484)  
So we need to consider yung constantly evolving mechanisms po

**[03:20:16.308] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003485)  
of how these crimes are being perpetrated.

**[03:20:18.562] DOJ Assistant Secretary Michelle Anne Lapuz** (asr-003486)  
For us to be able to be one, two, three steps ahead of the threats.

**[03:20:22.618] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003487)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:20:23.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003488)  
Thank you, Usec.

**[03:20:24.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003489)  
Asec.

**[03:20:26.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003490)  
So hindi lamang po pala yung mga masasamang elementong ito ang threat

**[03:20:31.315] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003491)  
sa kaligtasan ng ating mga kabataan.

**[03:20:33.712] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003492)  
Yung mismo pong budget na pinutol at tinanggal

**[03:20:36.589] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003493)  
is also a threat to the safety of our children.

**[03:20:38.986] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003494)  
Again, we support you in restoring your budget to the 2026 levels.

**[03:20:43.301] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003495)  
And kahit po kami ay bahagi ng minority, kasama niyo po kami.

**[03:20:47.617] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003496)  
We support your advocacy para po sa lalong madaling panahon ma-restore to 2026 levels.

**[03:20:51.932] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003497)  
Deserve po ng ating mga bata na maprotektahan.

**[03:20:55.288] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003498)  
At yung mga nasa peligro na po,

**[03:20:57.685] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003499)  
deserve na deserve nila ang immediate rescue at assistance.

**[03:21:03.439] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003500)  
Mr. Chair, let me direct my next set of questions to the Bureau of Immigration.

**[03:21:12.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003501)  
Magandang hapon po, Commissioner Viado.

**[03:21:13.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003502)  
Magandang hapon po, Congressman Cendana.

**[03:21:16.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003503)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[03:21:17.678] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003504)  
Ito po ay tungkol po sa usapin ng

**[03:21:19.595] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003505)  
Nung mga paggamit ng blacklist at entry restrictions as a political tool.

**[03:21:25.346] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003506)  
Nakita po natin yan noong nakaraang administrasyon.

**[03:21:28.221] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003507)  
Ginamit itong mga blacklist at entry restrictions laban sa mga advocates,

**[03:21:33.971] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003508)  
laban sa mga aktibista, at laban sa mismong kinatawan ng United Nations

**[03:21:38.763] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003509)  
na human rights advocate.

**[03:21:40.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003510)  
Ginamit ito in aid of violence and impunity.

**[03:21:44.737] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003511)  
Noong 2026 po, sinabi ng Amnesty International Philippines,

**[03:21:48.794] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003512)  
nananatiling mayroong entry ban si Amnesty International Secretary General Agnès Callamard

**[03:21:54.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003513)  
as a result of her previous work as, again, UN Special Rapporteur on Extrajudicial Killings.

**[03:22:00.965] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003514)  
Nagsimula po ang ban na ito noong 2018, bunga ng public attack ni former President Rodrigo Duterte.

**[03:22:07.051] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003515)  
Kaya mahalaga pong malaman natin meron po bang sapat na safeguards ang BI

**[03:22:12.122] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003516)  
upang matiyak na hindi ito nagagamit arbitrarily, hindi ito naweweaponize,

**[03:22:17.193] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003517)  
at para po tiyakin na hindi ito ginagamit laban sa human rights work and peaceful advocacy.

**[03:22:23.279] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003518)  
The first question, Mr. Chair: maaari po bang kumpirmahin ng BI ang kasalukuyang status ng entry restriction kay Ms. Agnès Callamard?

**[03:22:32.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003519)  
If I may confirm with my colleague, Your Honor.

**[03:22:50.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003520)  
Your Honors, ang lahat po ng desisyon ng Bureau of Immigration ay dumaan po sa proseso, unang-una.

**[03:22:57.667] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003521)  
Ang information po na nakalap namin ay hindi lang po galing sa vetting na ginawa ng Bureau of Immigration,

**[03:22:59.297] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003524)  
but galing po sa ibang mga ahensya,

**[03:23:00.926] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003525)  
dito po and internationally.

**[03:23:01.741] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003526)  
We refer to Interpol lists, we refer to fugitive status of individuals bago po kami umaksyon.

**[03:23:05.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003527)  
And internally, Your Honor, bago po kami mag-issue ng any blacklist, dumadaan po iyan sa proseso

**[03:23:11.165] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003528)  
kung saan ang legal division, ang amin hong mga abogado sa legal division,

**[03:23:17.331] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003529)  
and eventually iyong mga Board of Special Inquiry at Board of Commissioners.

**[03:23:20.845] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003530)  
Dinadaanan po iyan ng hindi lang maikling proseso pero mahaba-haba po, Your Honor.

**[03:23:27.319] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003531)  
So we can assure po, Your Honors, na any decision, any order concerning any blacklist

**[03:23:33.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003532)  
ay dumaan po sa tamang proseso at legal na pamamaraan po, Your Honor.

**[03:23:37.204] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003533)  
Hindi po arbitrary, hindi po

**[03:23:45.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003534)  
at the behest of any individual. Ito po ay ginagawa po sa level po ng aming bureau.

**[03:23:49.198] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003535)  
So the question again, Mr. Chair, ano po ang status ng entry restriction kay Ms. Agnès Callamard?

**[03:23:56.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003536)  
Again po, si Ms. Agnès Callamard po, sigurado ako wala po iyan sa blacklist ng Interpol.

**[03:24:05.937] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003537)  
She is former United Nations Special Rapporteur on Extrajudicial Killings.

**[03:24:16.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003538)  
We are confirming po iyong status ni Special Rapporteur Callamard.

**[03:24:20.885] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003539)  
But kung base po dito sa information po na nakalap namin ngayon, galing po dito sa honorable body na ito,

**[03:24:25.625] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003540)  
we are willing to also review the status of the Special Rapporteur.

**[03:24:29.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003541)  
Because we are in favor of human rights, we are in favor of other persons from other countries coming into our country

**[03:24:37.994] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003542)  
and to see how we have gone forward as far as human rights are concerned, Your Honor.

**[03:24:43.319] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003543)  
Mr. Chair, thank you very much, Commissioner Viado, for that response.

**[03:24:47.164] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003544)  
And we welcome your willingness to review your guidelines, no?

**[03:24:50.195] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003545)  
Lalo na po pagdating sa mga human rights defenders, international advocates, journalists,

**[03:24:55.525] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003546)  
academics, and especially UN officials.

**[03:24:58.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003547)  
Sa proseso po noong pag-review, inaasahan po natin na hindi na ito magagamit as a political weapon

**[03:25:05.146] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003548)  
against voices of dissent, against voices of human rights.

**[03:25:08.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003549)  
Nagawa na po ito noong madilim na panahon ng Rehimen Duterte, huwag na po nating hayaang maulit.

**[03:25:14.527] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-003550)  
That will be all, Mr. Chair. Marami pong salamat sa DOJ family.

**[03:25:18.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003551)  
Thank you rin po, Your Honor.

**[03:25:19.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003552)  
Thank you, Congressman Cendana.

**[03:25:21.618] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003553)  
Next in line from the majority.

**[03:25:26.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003554)  
Rep. Ann Matibag.

**[03:25:34.593] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003555)  
Ann Matibag. Thank you. Um, I just want to interject also,

**[03:25:39.880] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003556)  
no, after what my good friend Cong. Perci

**[03:25:45.080] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003557)  
has conveyed, especially to the OSAEC cases. We are really disheartened on

**[03:25:50.040] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003558)  
what happened with the deduction of your budget, and

**[03:25:55.439] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003559)  
with the OSAEC cases, we were more surprised

**[03:26:00.399] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003560)  
that much in the Department, 'yung mga law enforcement pa,

**[03:26:05.319] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003561)  
'yung mas nabawasan din, no, like the Bureau of Immigration and the National

**[03:26:10.720] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003562)  
Bureau of Investigation. So I just want to commend Mr.

**[03:26:14.920] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003563)  
Chair, Secretary Vida, and your whole team.

**[03:26:20.040] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003564)  
We were in New York, in the United Nations, last March. I am with Usec. Majken Anika, and

**[03:26:25.800] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003565)  
I want to commend their program, which is the Revised CANE+D

**[03:26:30.600] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003566)  
Protocol, which is intended to provide a more standardized and coordinated

**[03:26:36.040] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003567)  
way of handling cases such as this of violence against women and children.

**[03:26:41.279] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003568)  
So this emphasizes the clarity on the responsibilities of the agencies involved,

**[03:26:46.000] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003569)  
coordinating among them, and a more child-sensitive approach to investigation

**[03:26:51.160] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003570)  
and prosecution. So this is not just a policy in paper. I just want to

**[03:26:56.760] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003571)  
tell—and I'm just so proud of this program of the Department

**[03:27:01.680] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003572)  
of Justice—and this has already begun

**[03:27:06.720] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003573)  
bringing the Revised Protocol to the local level. I remember when

**[03:27:12.279] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003574)  
my group, my social organization, Zonta Club of Laguna,

**[03:27:16.920] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003575)  
went to the office of Secretary Vida, and he was very happy to tell us that

**[03:27:22.239] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003576)  
this is not just laws. This is not just a memorandum or any piece of paper or

**[03:27:27.399] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003577)  
a policy, but they already launched nationally in August

**[03:27:32.439] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003578)  
2025, followed by the provincial implementation in Cavite in

**[03:27:37.479] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003579)  
March 2026 and in Cebu in July 2026.

**[03:27:41.680] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003580)  
So for me, this is exactly the kind of program that deserves support in the

**[03:27:47.319] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003581)  
2027 budget, especially I'm the chairperson of the Committee on Women and Gender

**[03:27:53.080] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003582)  
Equality, and I'm with Cong. Sarah Elago and Cong. Perci,

**[03:27:58.160] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003583)  
who are members of my committee. Rest assured that I just want to manifest my

**[03:28:02.960] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003584)  
support, that we will make sure that your budget will not only be increased,

**[03:28:08.640] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003585)  
but with what you wish and hope for to have, especially to the department

**[03:28:13.800] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003586)  
of the wife of Cong. Keith—she's the director—so we

**[03:28:18.720] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003587)  
are in support of that. And maybe before the plenary session, we can

**[03:28:23.600] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003588)  
huddle and think things together, and maybe discuss on

**[03:28:29.040] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003589)  
how we can really explain

**[03:28:33.920] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003590)  
and discuss this budget that we need to really

**[03:28:39.359] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003591)  
bring it back to the DOJ's wish. And I am very happy to

**[03:28:44.319] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003592)  
see everyone here. DOJ, special po kayo dahil

**[03:28:49.040] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003593)  
ako'y pinalaki ng isang fiscal—ang tatay ko.

**[03:28:54.399] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003594)  
Especially now that NBI here is very,

**[03:28:59.560] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003595)  
very responsive to the needs of being a law enforcement for women and children.

**[03:29:04.720] Rep. Ann Matibag** (asr-003596)  
So, maraming-maraming salamat po. You have my support and respect. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:29:11.099] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003597)  
Thank you, Cong. Ann Matibag, sa suporta po sa ating pong ahensya.

**[03:29:15.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003598)  
Next in line from the minority, Representative Leila de Lima.

**[03:29:24.720] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003599)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[03:29:26.359] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003600)  
Good afternoon to my colleagues.

**[03:29:27.493] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003601)  
And good afternoon to Secretary Vida and the DOJ family.

**[03:29:31.986] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003602)  
Kumusta po kayo?

**[03:29:34.010] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003603)  
Okay.

**[03:29:35.021] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003604)  
Let me start by manifesting not only my support for the budget, the department-wide budget

**[03:29:44.048] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003605)  
of the DOJ, but also support for the push for the restoration of some of the areas of

**[03:29:49.512] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003606)  
the DOJ.

**[03:29:50.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003613)  
some of the budget that was cut, including the ones for OSAEC and also

**[03:29:55.819] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003614)  
for IACAT. And we do note very much the significant cuts, of course, in the

**[03:30:04.095] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003615)  
DOJ-wide budget, a 61.82% cut, and then the biggest among the agencies is the

**[03:30:12.553] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003616)  
Bucor by 84.18% cut. I'm talking about the NEP as against the proposed budget

**[03:30:22.168] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003617)  
and then also NBI as 65.84%. So ang gusto ko pong i-ano ngayon, lalo na dyan sa Bucor and sa NBI, if you wish to

**[03:30:35.596] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003618)  
insist about or for more in your wish list for increase in the budget, please

**[03:30:42.127] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003619)  
tell us.

**[03:30:43.486] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003621)  
But specific items in the budget for Bucor, for DOJ, for NBI, and even the

**[03:30:50.058] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003622)  
others, OSG and even PAO, so that we can help you out, at least put back the

**[03:30:58.172] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003623)  
priority, your priority programs that were actually cut in the NEP.

**[03:31:06.265] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003625)  
Now, I wish to direct my questions first to BI.

**[03:31:16.224] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003627)  
then to Bucor and the DOJ proper.

**[03:31:25.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003628)  
I would wish also to pursue further the case of Dr. Calamard.

**[03:31:29.912] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003629)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[03:31:30.937] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003630)  
It struck me what you said, that it was not at any behest when you make any blacklisting.

**[03:31:36.138] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003631)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[03:31:36.797] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003632)  
Sa case ho ba ni Dr. Callamard, ganoon? It was at no one’s behest.

**[03:31:41.833] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003633)  
As far as I know, Your Honor.

**[03:31:43.444] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003634)  
We want to understand further.

**[03:31:46.576] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003635)  
What was the legal basis for the blacklisting of Dr. Calamard?

**[03:31:49.359] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003636)  
Your Honor, based on the record, it was a national security concern, Your Honor.

**[03:31:55.119] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003637)  
A national security concern?

**[03:31:56.708] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003638)  
Yes.

**[03:31:57.701] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003639)  
Do you have any specific explanation for that?

**[03:32:01.673] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003640)  
National security concern?

**[03:32:03.659] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003641)  
Meron ho bang security threat?

**[03:32:05.645] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003642)  
There was no crime committed, correct?

**[03:32:07.631] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003643)  
There was no crime committed?

**[03:32:09.617] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003644)  
As far as I know, Your Honor, no.

**[03:32:11.604] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003645)  
And there was no immigration violation?

**[03:32:13.590] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003646)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:32:14.583] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003647)  
Immigration laws violation?

**[03:32:15.615] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003648)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:32:16.609] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003649)  
Immigration laws violation committed.

**[03:32:17.602] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003650)  
But what you are saying now is a national security threat.

**[03:32:20.581] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003651)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:32:21.574] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003652)  
But isn’t she the one who investigated the drug-war killings as the former UN Special Rapporteur on extrajudicial killings?

**[03:32:32.497] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003653)  
Your Honor, yes.

**[03:32:33.491] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003654)  
That is based on the records available, Your Honor, to us at the moment, Your Honor.

**[03:32:39.449] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003655)  
I can dig deeper into this matter, Your Honor, because this matter, I think, was...

**[03:32:45.407] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003656)  
occurred even before we came into, you know, we were appointed to the Bureau of Immigration.

**[03:32:52.359] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003657)  
But if...

**[03:32:55.338] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003658)  
So hanggang ngayon po, hindi ninyo talaga alam exactly kung anong naging basehan?

**[03:33:01.296] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003659)  
Ang basehan po is based on reports from a national security agency, Your Honor.

**[03:33:07.255] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003660)  
But I can give you the details and the other facts and issues concerning this matter.

**[03:33:15.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003661)  
If you can give us a chance, Your Honor, we can give you a full detailed report on that matter.

**[03:33:19.122] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003662)  
Hindi ho ba merong motion for reconsideration with respect to that?

**[03:33:23.044] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003663)  
As far back as August 2026, there is this motion for reconsideration on the blacklisting of Dr. Calamard.

**[03:33:29.909] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003664)  
For the lifting, Your Honor?

**[03:33:30.890] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003665)  
For the lifting, for the lifting of the blacklist.

**[03:33:34.813] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003666)  
And isn’t it that under the BI Omnibus Rules, the commissioner is supposed to resolve a lifting petition within 15 working days?

**[03:33:44.620] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003667)  
Your Honor...

**[03:33:45.600] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003668)

**[03:33:53.446] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003670)

**[03:34:09.137] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003674)

**[03:34:12.079] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003675)

**[03:34:14.040] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003676)

**[03:34:19.227] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003678)

Yes.

**[03:34:21.301] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003680)

**[03:34:24.413] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003681)

**[03:34:25.451] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003682)  
For the CAISS.

**[03:34:27.525] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003683)

**[03:34:34.787] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003684)

**[03:34:44.123] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003685)  
This is an unsolicited proposal which came from a private proponent.

**[03:34:49.309] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003686)  
Yes. So why should—I mean, I’m just wondering—why should the cost of modernizing immigration infrastructure be passed on to the traveling public, even if it’s covered by a PPP?

**[03:35:00.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003687)  
Your Honor, first and foremost, we need a more integrative program which covers

**[03:35:12.482] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003688)  
a border security platform with biometrics, advanced analytics, and passenger information system, actually, to enhance national security and immigration control, Your Honor.

**[03:35:19.343] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003689)  
At the moment, there is no particular program which can be funded by the national government at this level.

**[03:35:29.145] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003690)  
The private proponent proposes to put the capitalization in the amount of 10.7 billion for the next five years.

**[03:35:38.947] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003691)  
And with the constant upgrade of the system every four years

**[03:35:41.888] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003692)  
for the next 20-year period, Your Honor, at no cost to the government.

**[03:35:46.788] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003693)  
At no cost to the government.

**[03:35:47.769] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003694)  
At no cost to the government, Your Honor.

**[03:35:49.729] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003695)  
But you’re still insisting on the P480 fee, because that is what is being demanded.

**[03:35:56.590] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003696)  
Actually, Your Honor, it is P240 per ticket.

**[03:36:02.471] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003697)  
So if it’s a round-trip ticket, Your Honor, it’s P480.

**[03:36:07.372] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003698)  
Compared to other countries, this is way below.

**[03:36:09.333] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003699)  
This is actually the practice in other countries.

**[03:36:11.293] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003700)

**[03:36:22.075] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003701)

**[03:36:31.877] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003702)

**[03:36:34.817] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003703)

**[03:36:38.738] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003704)  
Hong Kong charges about 1,500.

**[03:36:40.698] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003705)  
Thailand charges about 1,500.

**[03:36:42.659] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003706)  
And Thailand charges about 1,800, Your Honor.

**[03:36:47.560] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003707)  
So it stays?

**[03:36:49.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003708)

**[03:36:50.565] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003709)

**[03:36:52.654] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003710)

**[03:36:57.877] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003713)

**[03:36:59.966] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003714)

**[03:37:03.100] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003715)

**[03:37:07.278] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003716)

**[03:37:09.368] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003717)

**[03:38:50.199] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003742)

**[03:40:11.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003775)

**[03:40:15.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003776)

**[03:40:24.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003780)

**[03:40:26.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003781)

**[03:40:27.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003782)  
So...

**[03:40:30.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003784)

Yes, ma'am.

**[03:40:43.319] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003790)  
May nakakalusot pong pailan-ilan

**[03:40:45.235] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003791)  
kasi talaga pong nasa private parts.

**[03:40:49.068] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003792)  
And then, of course,

**[03:40:50.026] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003793)

**[03:40:54.817] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003794)  
So I must admit.

**[03:40:55.775] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003795)  
Kaya iyan po iyong nire-report ko sa inyo,

**[03:40:58.649] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003796)

**[03:41:00.566] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003797)  
Think about it.

**[03:41:01.524] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003798)  
Isang nurse

**[03:41:02.482] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003799)

**[03:41:05.356] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003800)  
na may shabu—

**[03:41:07.273] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003801)

**[03:41:08.231] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003802)  
Shabu.

**[03:41:09.189] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003803)  
Pandeshal.

**[03:41:10.147] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003804)  
So...

**[03:41:11.105] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003805)  
So nakasuhan po iyon.

**[03:41:12.063] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003806)  
Tanggal na sa serbisyo iyon.

**[03:41:13.979] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003807)  
So...

**[03:41:14.938] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003808)  
Ang mga na-dismiss po diyan

**[03:41:15.896] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003809)  
ay 43.

**[03:41:17.812] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003810)  
Suspended, 137.

**[03:41:20.686] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003811)  
And of course,

**[03:41:21.645] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003812)

**[03:41:23.561] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003813)  
So, ma'am,

**[03:41:24.519] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003814)

**[03:41:26.435] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003815)  
Outright,

**[03:41:27.393] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003816)  
puwede ko,

**[03:41:28.352] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003817)

**[03:41:30.268] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003818)

**[03:41:32.184] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003819)  
Pero dito po,

**[03:41:34.100] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003820)  
But here,

**[03:41:35.059] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003821)  
hindi puwede.

**[03:41:36.017] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003822)

**[03:41:36.975] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003823)

**[03:41:38.891] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003824)  
Hindi po tayo makakapag-weed out

**[03:41:39.849] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003825)

that are coming from

**[03:41:44.640] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003828)

**[03:41:46.854] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003829)  
Iyong mga tina-transfer ninyo,

**[03:41:47.961] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003830)

**[03:41:49.067] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003831)

**[03:41:52.388] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003832)  
na mga PDLs.

**[03:41:53.495] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003833)

**[03:41:54.602] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003834)  
Nababantayan ho ba talaga iyon?

**[03:41:56.815] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003835)  
Ang ginawa ko, for the longest time, iyong Chinese drug lords sa NBP, nilipat ko lahat sa Mindoro, sa Supermax.

**[03:42:08.880] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003836)  
At iyong report naman namin, we will submit to you, na drastically nabawasan na natin ang pagpasok ng mga drugs.

**[03:42:17.263] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003837)  
May mga nangyayari po. Iyong bibisitang misis may dala-dalang drugs, nilalagay sa private parts.

**[03:42:30.123] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003838)

**[03:42:37.781] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003839)

**[03:42:43.306] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003840)

**[03:42:45.345] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003841)  
Opo. Totoo po.

Yes, that's true.

**[03:42:46.202] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003843)  
Paano po iyon?

**[03:42:47.250] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003844)
