**[03:42:49.479] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003845)  
Kaya talaga po, pagka ayaw nilang magpa-inspection, hindi na po namin puwedeng mag-conjugal visit kung ayaw magpa-inspect noong misis.

**[03:43:01.762] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003846)  
Tinatanong ko rin po iyan kasi iyong mga so-called witnesses doon sa kaso ko, those who gave perjurious testimonies,

**[03:43:09.467] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003847)  
iyong mga nai-transfer na po, iyon ho ba iyong mga nababantayan?

**[03:43:13.602] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003848)  
They were watched.

**[03:43:15.034] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003849)  
That's why.

**[03:43:15.881] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003850)  
You know who I’m referring to?

**[03:43:17.858] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003851)  
All those drug lords, those witnesses who testified in my cases—they were supposed to be transferred.

**[03:43:24.473] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003852)  
Nandoon po sila sa Mindoro?

**[03:43:26.954] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003853)  
Yes.

**[03:43:27.640] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003854)  
Pag bumibisita ako, nagno-noise barrage sila.

**[03:43:30.543] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003855)  
Tapos sabi ko, “Picturan mo lahat iyong nag-iingay.”

**[03:43:33.127] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003856)  
Hahanapan ko pa ng isang isla iyan. Ilagay ko sa isang isla na separate branch.

**[03:43:38.196] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003857)  
Kasi nagrereklamo sila.

**[03:43:41.059] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003858)  
Nagrereklamo po kasi nilipat sila doon sa Mindoro.

**[03:43:44.399] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003859)  
So, really...

**[03:43:45.254] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003860)  
They would want back to New Bilibid Prison.

**[03:43:48.037] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003861)  
Sa New Bilibid po, medyo mahirap po talagang ma-secure dahil—

**[03:43:52.709] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003862)  
kaya kailangan po isara na po natin iyang New Bilibid Prison dahil napaka-crowded po talaga.

**[03:43:59.548] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003863)  
At regional na lang.

**[03:44:00.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003864)  
Yes.

**[03:44:04.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003865)  
I have no time anymore.

**[03:44:05.992] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003866)  
I have questions supposedly to DOJ proper, but I have no time.

**[03:44:08.825] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003867)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[03:44:11.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003868)  
Thank you, Cong. Leila.

**[03:44:11.404] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-003869)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:44:12.914] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003870)  
Next in line...

**[03:44:13.781] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003871)  
Next in line…

**[03:44:16.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003872)  
Of course, my predecessor, previous sponsor of the DOJ budget, Cong Rufus Rodriguez.

**[03:44:26.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003873)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:44:28.236] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003874)  
Good afternoon to my distinguished colleagues and our valued resource persons this afternoon.

**[03:44:34.502] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003875)  
So I am here because I am here to support the budget of the OSEC and all the departments

**[03:44:43.740] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003876)  
and attached agencies of the Department of Justice.

**[03:44:47.411] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003877)  
Why?

**[03:44:47.711] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003878)  
Why?

**[03:44:49.267] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003879)  
I finished my undergraduate studies in De La Salle because I was able to get the salary of my father,

**[03:44:58.905] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003880)  
who was for 20 years provincial fiscal of Misamis Oriental.

**[03:45:03.235] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003881)  
And I would go to the, you know, La Salle, and then we go by jeepney to Padre Faura and get it.

**[03:45:10.917] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003882)  
So thank you again.

**[03:45:12.832] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003883)  
My life, therefore, has a history of support from the Department of Justice,

**[03:45:17.641] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003884)  
and we can never, our family can never forget that.

**[03:45:21.612] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003885)  
So first questions will be—these questions are leading questions—

**[03:45:30.142] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003886)  
To increase the budget of the OSEC.

**[03:45:32.536] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003887)  
Mr. Secretary, Frederick Vida.

**[03:45:35.210] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003888)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:45:35.689] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003889)  
The OSEC, while it looks like there is an increase,

**[03:45:39.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003890)  
however, your MOOE has been reduced from 1.9 to only 1.6,

**[03:45:47.841] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003891)  
so that 203.

**[03:45:49.717] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003892)  
million decrease. What is your wish on this, Mr. Secretary?

**[03:45:58.117] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003893)  
Your Honor, to meet the levels of 2026, to be restored to that level,

**[03:46:05.280] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003894)  
so that the department would have sufficient resources to fulfill its mandate, Your Honor.

**[03:46:12.683] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003895)  
Thank you.

**[03:46:13.142] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003896)  
So we will work on that in the BARC, in our Committee of Vice Chairpersons,

**[03:46:18.370] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003897)  
and then hopefully in the BICAM.

**[03:46:21.263] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003898)  
So thank you.

**[03:46:21.961] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003899)  
So restoration of 2.9.

**[03:46:23.358] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003900)  
2.3 for Secretary of Justice Vida.

**[03:46:26.391] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003901)  
Is that all?

**[03:46:31.200] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003902)  
Of course, Your Honor.

**[03:46:32.802] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003903)  
We made mention earlier of the request,

**[03:46:36.087] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003904)  
so particularly on the reduction of the NEP 2027

**[03:46:41.375] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003905)  
from the proposed budget for the Office of the Cybercrime,

**[03:46:45.902] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003906)  
the NCC-OSAEC and the IACAT.

**[03:46:50.249] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003907)  
Those are of particular importance,

**[03:46:52.693] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003908)  
which we do not want to lose the momentum that we have now, Your Honor.

**[03:46:57.059] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003909)  
How much for the three agencies?

**[03:47:00.164] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003910)  
Similarly, Your Honor, to be restored to the levels of 2026.

**[03:47:04.330] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003911)  
Which is?

**[03:47:05.352] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003912)  
Which is?

**[03:47:07.475] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003913)  
Take note, because we're going to work for this.

**[03:47:12.893] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003914)  
Forty-five, seventy.

**[03:47:18.862] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003915)  
Approximately 130 million, Your Honor.

**[03:47:21.787] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003916)  
Only?

**[03:47:22.528] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003917)  
For the three agencies?

**[03:47:24.351] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003918)  
Yes, for...

**[03:47:25.693] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003919)  
Please, please note down, because we’re going to make sure that it will be restored.

**[03:47:28.717] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003920)  
To meet the 2026 level.

**[03:47:29.558] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003921)  
Yes, at least 2026 level.

**[03:47:32.263] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003922)  
Anything else, Secretary?

**[03:47:35.618] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003923)  
Of course, particular interest would be on the request of the NBI

**[03:47:41.587] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003924)  
on their pursuit and in the particular challenge in addressing cybercrime

**[03:47:49.839] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003925)  
in pursuit of their investigation and their push

**[03:47:56.109] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003926)  
so that the requested budget from the DBM be restored on the ICT projects and programs.

**[03:48:03.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003927)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[03:48:04.722] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003928)  
Yes, good that you've mentioned, because I'm going to go one by one,

**[03:48:08.648] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003929)  
those who were reduced in their 2027 NEP.

**[03:48:12.934] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003930)  
And we're going now to NBI, because mentioned already.

**[03:48:16.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003931)  
Director Melvin Matibag,

**[03:48:20.950] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003932)  
is it correct that your agency,

**[03:48:25.239] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003933)  
the National Bureau of Investigation,

**[03:48:27.294] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003934)  
from 3.791 million,

**[03:48:31.024] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003935)  
it is now only 2.9 billion?

**[03:48:33.578] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003936)  
All right.

**[03:48:33.698] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003937)  
It’s a decrease of 882 million, 23.2 percent.

**[03:48:39.742] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003938)  
Is that correct?

**[03:48:41.059] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003939)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:48:42.176] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003940)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:48:43.114] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003941)  
How can they do that to you?

**[03:48:45.767] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003942)  
I have no idea.

**[03:48:47.363] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003943)  
Just to be clear, I think the reason why is the utilization at that time,

**[03:48:52.610] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003944)  
because there are some ICT projects that need to be aligned,

**[03:48:58.017] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003945)  
be completed.

**[03:48:58.475] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003946)  
But right now, we have recognized the reasons for that,

**[03:49:03.622] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003947)  
and I think the...

**[03:49:06.356] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003948)  
The catch-up plan, right?

**[03:49:07.553] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003949)  
That's correct.

**[03:49:08.311] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003950)  
So you finish everything by December 31?

**[03:49:10.525] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003951)  
By December, we will have 98 percent utilization.

**[03:49:13.677] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003952)  
The remaining 2 percent will be for the maintenance of the ICT that we will be installing,

**[03:49:19.483] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003953)  
which has already been obligated by this time, Your Honor.

**[03:49:22.734] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003954)  
Very good.

**[03:49:23.373] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003955)  
So assuming, because we will assume that you will do it.

**[03:49:26.784] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003956)  
You’re a very good director.

**[03:49:28.261] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003957)  
Thank you.

**[03:49:28.580] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003958)  
So you will be able...

**[03:49:30.874] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003959)  
Can you place here a restoration of 880?

**[03:49:33.547] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003960)  
882 million, correct?

**[03:49:36.201] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003961)  
Yes.

**[03:49:36.819] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003962)  
And you're satisfied with that?

**[03:49:38.176] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003963)  
Well, I have a specific request, Mr. Chair.

**[03:49:42.186] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003964)  
Say that now.

**[03:49:43.702] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003965)  
It will not be counted against my time.

**[03:49:45.457] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003966)  
Our agents right now is getting a hazard pay of only 4,000 pesos monthly.

**[03:49:52.739] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003967)  
So we are requesting at least we can be leveled with the other law enforcements,

**[03:49:57.108] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003968)  
which is probably the reasonable amount will be 15,000 monthly for them to operate properly,

**[03:50:03.472] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003969)  
our agents has been transferred from one province to another,

**[03:50:07.123] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003970)  
and they’ve been operating 24/7, like the concern on the issue of OSAEC.

**[03:50:13.188] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003971)  
Records will reveal that we have so many operations and apprehensions on OSAEC

**[03:50:17.776] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003972)  
and other crimes, cyber crimes also, which we've been addressing.

**[03:50:23.063] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003973)  
So I'm respectfully requesting that the hazard pay of our agents from 4,000 be increased to 15,000 at least.

**[03:50:30.784] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003974)  
15,000?

**[03:50:31.641] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003975)  
Yes.

**[03:50:32.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003976)  
Approved.

**[03:50:32.579] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003977)  
Thank you.

**[03:50:33.397] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003978)  
From me, from me.

**[03:50:35.232] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003979)  
Okay.

**[03:50:37.367] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003980)  
Work for that.

**[03:50:38.464] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003981)  
Salamat po.

**[03:50:40.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003982)  
The NBI director is my good, you know, our cherished faculty member.

**[03:50:45.585] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003983)  
I was dean of San Sebastian College of Law, and I personally requested President Rueda to teach,

**[03:50:51.869] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003984)  
and he was there for quite some time with me.

**[03:50:54.422] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003985)  
And so now to Chief Rueda-Acosta, what do you need?

**[03:50:58.072] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003986)  
I think it will be about the opening of more, you know,

**[03:51:02.939] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003987)  
PAO.

**[03:51:03.318] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003988)  
PAO lawyers because of the ratio of courts vis-a-vis the PAO attorneys.

**[03:51:12.912] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003989)  
We need 559 lawyers, Your Honor.

**[03:51:16.343] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003990)  
We are amenable to half the number.

**[03:51:18.776] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003991)  
How much is 500?

**[03:51:20.950] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003992)  
Yes, 500.

**[03:51:22.027] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003993)  
How much is that?

**[03:51:23.603] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003994)  
One billion, Your Honor.

**[03:51:25.538] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003995)  
You just know it.

**[03:51:27.393] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003996)  
Because of the high salary.

**[03:51:30.125] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003997)  
We are amenable to half.

**[03:51:31.741] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003998)  
How much is one half?

**[03:51:33.237] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-003999)  
Half, only, Your Honor.

**[03:51:35.671] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004000)  
Maybe 225 new lawyers.

**[03:51:38.463] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004001)  
But give us 400 staff.

**[03:51:40.258] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004002)  
We have no staff.

**[03:51:42.093] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004003)  
There is no creation of staff position.

**[03:51:44.148] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004004)  
How much will be the total of that?

**[03:51:46.820] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004005)  
Meaning both the PAO lawyers and the staff?

**[03:51:50.471] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004006)  
200 lawyers, maybe, and 400 staff.

**[03:51:53.842] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004007)  
That may be around 600 million.

**[03:51:57.093] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004008)  
Reasonable?

**[03:51:58.948] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004009)  
Around 700 million.

**[03:52:00.623] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004010)  
Oh, reasonable also.

**[03:52:01.561] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004011)  
So 700.

**[03:52:03.156] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004012)  
700 million.

**[03:52:04.154] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004013)  
We try to have that.

**[03:52:05.151] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004014)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:52:06.148] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004015)  
Because the Department of Justice and our PAO should support it.

**[03:52:09.140] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004016)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:52:10.138] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004017)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[03:52:11.135] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004018)  
Remember, I worked hard for this last year.

**[03:52:12.132] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004019)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:52:13.130] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004020)  
They knew.

**[03:52:14.127] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004021)  
But fortunately, there was no...

**[03:52:15.124] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004022)  
There is continuous creation of courts, branches of courts.

**[03:52:18.116] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004023)  
That's correct.

**[03:52:19.113] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004024)  
That's correct.

**[03:52:20.111] PAO Chief Persida V. Rueda-Acosta** (asr-004025)  
So we need fiscals and PAO lawyers at the same time, Your Honor.

**[03:52:23.103] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004026)  
Thank you.

**[03:52:24.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004027)  
And then, of course, my favorite, Commissioner of the Bureau of Immigration, where I was once

**[03:52:30.565] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004028)  
one of the Bureau.

**[03:52:31.556] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004029)  
That's correct.

**[03:52:32.548] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004030)  
Commissioner, I understand here in your office, the BI has been greatly reduced by

**[03:52:41.471] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004031)  
1.6 billion.

**[03:52:44.446] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004032)  
How is that, Commissioner Viado, with your very outstanding performance there?

**[03:52:49.404] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004033)  
You're doing so well.

**[03:52:50.395] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004034)  
Why are they reducing that?

**[03:52:52.378] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004035)  
I don't know the reason why, Your Honor.

**[03:52:55.353] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004036)  
But thank you very much for the endorsement.

**[03:52:57.336] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004037)  
And we will increase the additional budget.

**[03:53:01.302] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004038)  
Yeah.

**[03:53:16.175] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004042)

**[03:53:22.124] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004043)  
Honor.

**[03:53:23.115] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004044)

**[03:53:24.107] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004045)  
Yes.

**[03:53:25.098] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004046)

**[03:53:30.056] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004047)

**[03:53:33.030] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004050)

**[03:53:34.022] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004051)

**[03:53:35.013] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004052)

**[03:53:50.878] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004054)

**[03:53:56.827] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004055)

**[03:53:57.818] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004056)

**[03:53:59.801] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004058)  
527,811, Your Honor.

Thank you.

Thank you.

**[03:54:14.674] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004073)

**[03:54:47.740] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004092)

**[03:54:58.227] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004093)  
So . . .

Iyon po iyong nasa ISSP, 123 million.

**[03:55:08.113] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004096)  
Yung sa OSG po, total of 4.5 billion pesos for the whole OSG,

**[03:55:15.118] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004097)

**[03:55:18.039] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004098)

**[03:55:22.622] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004099)

**[03:55:24.043] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004100)  
So around maybe 800 million to 1 billion pesos din po yun.

**[03:55:27.966] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004101)  
And then, ang hiningi lang po namin ay dahil through the MYCA,

**[03:55:32.469] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004102)  
so 255 million will go to the first year installment to be paid to GSIS.

**[03:55:39.053] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004103)  
250 million?

**[03:55:40.994] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004104)  
First year?

**[03:55:42.035] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004105)  
First year. So iyon po iyong nakalagay sa NEP.

**[03:55:44.736] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004106)  
Has the DBM approved your MYCA proposal?

**[03:55:47.718] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004107)

**[03:55:52.441] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004108)  
Yes, ang nailagay po ng DBM dahil ang timeline po namin,

**[03:55:57.985] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004109)  
six months pa to kung makonfirm, then mafifit out po yung building.

**[03:56:03.128] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004110)  
So half year lang po.

**[03:56:05.790] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004111)  
Ang nawala po yung sa computerization, sa digital transformation program po yung 100 million.

**[03:56:12.734] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004112)

**[03:56:14.875] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004113)  
123 million po.

**[03:56:16.757] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004114)  
Computerization, we need that very much.

**[03:56:18.758] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004115)  
Yes.

**[03:56:19.298] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004116)  
Plus the building, 250 for the year, next year.

**[03:56:22.220] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004117)  
Iyong pong building, nandoon na po iyon sa NEP.

**[03:56:25.582] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004118)  
So ang medyo inihihirit po yung...

**[03:56:28.004] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004119)

**[03:56:31.686] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004120)  
Okay. Digitalization is very important.

**[03:56:34.768] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004121)  
Thank you.

**[03:56:35.448] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004122)  
So anything else?

**[03:56:44.114] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004124)

**[03:56:45.335] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004125)

**[03:56:52.019] Solicitor General Darlene Marie B. Berberabe** (asr-004126)  
Thank you, Congressman Rufus.

**[03:56:53.900] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004127)

Here, sir. Good afternoon, sir.

**[03:57:06.265] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004130)  
Here.

**[03:57:07.625] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004131)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:57:08.805] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004132)  
It was reduced by 26 million.

**[03:57:12.547] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004133)

**[03:57:14.748] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004134)

Well, you are like some other official saying, I just scolded the CDA representative in that hearing just recently

**[03:57:28.033] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004137)

He will agree to the small budget.

**[03:57:37.836] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004140)

**[03:57:42.198] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004141)  
You fight always so that you will have an increase in your budget.

**[03:57:46.739] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004142)

That's correct, Your Honor.

**[03:57:49.540] OADR Executive Director Jose Maria Palaganas** (asr-004144)  
Rufus.

**[03:57:50.021] OADR Executive Director Jose Maria Palaganas** (asr-004145)  
We would like to propose restoring it to the original 168 million for 2027.

**[03:57:58.044] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004146)  
So we restore 26 million, correct?

**[03:58:04.206] OADR Executive Director Jose Maria Palaganas** (asr-004147)  
That's right.

**[03:58:04.666] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004148)  
OADR, right?

**[03:58:05.506] OADR Executive Director Jose Maria Palaganas** (asr-004149)  
That's right.

**[03:58:06.407] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004150)  
Oh, OGCC also reduced.

**[03:58:09.748] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004151)

**[03:58:27.945] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004155)  
Anyway, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:58:50.873] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004159)  
My goodness.

**[03:58:52.194] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004160)  
Your budget is small.

**[03:58:53.854] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004161)  
Reduced by 49 million.

**[03:58:57.195] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004162)  
What can you say?

**[03:58:58.396] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004163)  
Administrator Benitez,

**[03:58:59.696] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004164)

Ah, yes.

**[03:59:07.099] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004166)  
For...

**[03:59:07.939] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004167)  
Bukor.

**[03:59:08.780] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004168)  
Ah, Bukor.

**[03:59:09.400] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004169)  
Bukor, let me see.

**[03:59:12.841] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004170)

**[03:59:17.863] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004172)

**[03:59:23.525] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004173)  
But hopefully, this budget will not anymore be touched.

**[03:59:27.807] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004174)

**[03:59:30.648] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004175)  
Yes, thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:59:32.168] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004176)  
How about the...

Administrator Benitez.

**[03:59:35.910] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004178)  
Ah, you are Administrator Benitez.

**[03:59:36.850] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-004179)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:59:37.785] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004180)  
How about the Parole and Probation Administration?

**[03:59:39.676] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004181)  
Your Honor.

**[03:59:40.671] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004182)  
Sino?

**[03:59:41.667] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004183)  
Parole Probation?

**[03:59:42.841] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004184)  
Yeah.

**[03:59:43.836] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004185)  
You are General?

Bienvenido, sir.

**[03:59:47.100] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004187)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:59:48.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004188)  
So I have a wrong addressee.

**[03:59:50.940] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004189)  
So I have some letter for you.

**[03:59:54.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004190)  
So the parole probation, again, was reduced by 49 million.

**[04:00:00.840] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004191)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:00:01.300] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004192)  
So what are the important items to be restored?

**[04:00:04.840] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004193)  
In our case, we were reduced by 49 million,

**[04:00:08.880] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004194)  
pero po kasi intact na po kami sa ISSP computerization namin.

**[04:00:14.160] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004195)  
Ang wish list po namin ay para sa probation officers’ ladderized

**[04:00:20.320] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004196)  
three-year improvement program for their major competencies po.

**[04:00:26.420] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004197)  
This is about 8,198,000 for a three-year period.

**[04:00:36.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004198)  
Eight million?

**[04:00:38.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004199)  
So not billion?

**[04:00:39.700] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004200)  
Very small?

**[04:00:41.120] Parole and Probation Administration representative** (asr-004201)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:00:41.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004202)  
Eight million.

**[04:00:42.660] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004203)  
So we'll try to work on that.

**[04:00:44.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004204)  
Any other agency not called?

**[04:00:45.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004205)  
Because I think I called everybody already.

**[04:00:51.340] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004206)  
I'll just, okay.

**[04:00:53.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004207)  
Lahat naman yata nakuha na, no?

**[04:00:55.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004208)  
PCGG, yes?

**[04:00:57.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004209)  
PCGG.

**[04:00:59.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004210)  
Yeah.

**[04:01:00.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004211)  
Well, you have an increase here.

**[04:01:02.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004212)  
But what is the wish of PCGG?

**[04:01:03.740] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004213)  
Ang wish po namin, eto talagang wish po, is more institutional rather than budgetary.

**[04:01:14.380] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004214)  
Okay.

**[04:01:14.800] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004215)  
Okay.

**[04:01:15.440] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004216)  
So our wish is that the mandate of the PCGG will be expanded, but this requires a law po kasi.

**[04:01:22.960] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004217)  
Because presently po, our mandate is limited.

**[04:01:28.080] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004218)  
It's limited to a few individuals only.

**[04:01:32.620] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004219)  
And so we would like to expand sana yung mandate ng PCGG to include all public officials.

**[04:01:43.600] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004220)  
Right now po,

**[04:01:45.440] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004221)  
Mandate namin is to file civil forfeiture cases.

**[04:01:50.360] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004222)  
So as far as corruption is concerned, that is the template that has been used by the PCGG for the past 40 years.

**[04:01:58.680] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004223)  
And we were able to recover more than 300 billion only through civil forfeiture.

**[04:02:05.700] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004224)  
Wala po kaming naipakulong, pero we were able to recover that much.

**[04:02:10.900] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004225)  
And so gusto po sana namin ma-expand yung mandate.

**[04:02:14.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004226)  
Perhaps we can now give way to a new mandate.

**[04:02:19.280] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004227)  
I understand there's already a bill for that.

**[04:02:21.660] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004228)  
Yes po.

**[04:02:22.320] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004229)  
So I will be a co-author.

**[04:02:24.420] PCGG Chairperson** (asr-004230)  
Yes po.

**[04:02:24.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004231)  
For that, so that you will be able to expand your powers.

**[04:02:30.660] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004232)  
With that, Mr. Chairman, I thank you for being lenient with me.

**[04:02:34.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004233)  
I congratulate Secretary Vida for an outstanding performance as Secretary of Justice.

**[04:02:41.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004234)  
And all of our attached agencies.

**[04:02:43.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004235)  
Again, you have our support here in Congress.

**[04:02:46.580] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004236)  
For after all, justice is an important component of a democracy and development of our nation.

**[04:02:54.420] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004237)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chairman, for your leniency in my time.

**[04:02:57.700] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-004238)  
Thank you.

**[04:02:58.319] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004239)  
Thank you, Kong Rufus.

**[04:02:59.359] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004240)  
Thank you for emphasizing the needs of the DOJ and its attached agencies

**[04:03:10.760] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004241)  
para po mas maganda pa po yung operations na magawa po nila.

**[04:03:16.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004242)  
Next in line from the minority.

**[04:03:17.920] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004243)  
Representative Egay Erice.

**[04:03:23.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004244)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:03:24.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004245)  
Good afternoon, Secretary Vida and the DOJ family.

**[04:03:29.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004246)  
We in the minority strongly supports the promises of Congressman Rufus.

**[04:03:36.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004247)  
And I think we can assure fiscal space by reducing the appropriations of the House of Representatives.

**[04:03:46.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004248)  
Anyway.

**[04:03:54.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004256)  
Ang first question ko po, Secretary, para po sa ating immigration.

**[04:03:58.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004257)  
And this is about the CAIS, the Civil Aviation Immigration Security Services Project.

**[04:04:04.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004258)  
Good afternoon, Your Honor.

**[04:04:04.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004259)  
I would like to maybe appraise of what's the status of the project na.

**[04:04:10.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004260)  
The current status, Your Honor, po, it is under contract review.

**[04:04:14.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004261)  
And the final screening will be made by the Department of Finance, the OSG, and the PPP Center, po, Your Honor.

**[04:04:22.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004262)  
But the contract was already signed?

**[04:04:24.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004263)  
No contract has been signed, Your Honor.

**[04:04:28.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004264)  
So can you just brief us on what's in particular is the contract?

**[04:04:38.720] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004265)  
This is a build, train, maintain, transfer modality?

**[04:04:45.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004266)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[04:04:47.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004267)  
Build, train, maintain, and transfer modality, it is a public-private partnership proposed

**[04:04:52.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004268)  
by SIGRIPORT and delivers an integrated aviation and border security platform.

**[04:04:58.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004269)  
Combining biometrics, merong advanced analytics.

**[04:05:02.319] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004270)  
When you say, Your Honor, advanced analytics, kahit po yung behavior ng isang tao at the

**[04:05:08.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004271)  
point wherein kinukuha yung information regarding him, any nervous stick, any facial contortion

**[04:05:17.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004272)  
can be read by the system to determine kung high risk po siya at malaking probability

**[04:05:24.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004273)  
that he will cause havoc when entering the Philippines.

**[04:05:28.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004274)  
And it also integrates passenger information systems with the ultimate objective of enhancing

**[04:05:36.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004275)  
national security and immigration control.

**[04:05:39.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004276)  
The features of this program include access to billions of facial images.

**[04:05:47.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004277)  
Yung categorization po and then risk scoring, meaning kaya po na nabanggit ko kanina, kung

**[04:05:54.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004278)  
high risk po yung pasahero na lagi intend na pumasok ng Pilipinas, then ma-analyze po

**[04:06:00.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004279)  
at mabibigyan po ng proper tool ang aming immigration officer to decide whether or not

**[04:06:05.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004280)  
to allow this person to enter.

**[04:06:08.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004281)  
Meron din pong biographic comparison against criminal and sanctions databases, meaning

**[04:06:13.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004282)  
ang interpol po, may access po tayo dyan sa kanilang...

**[04:06:17.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004283)  
system, not only sa Central, but all 194 countries sa konektado po sa Interpol.

**[04:06:24.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004284)  
Meron din pong deception detection analytics based on mga nabanggit ko po, involuntary

**[04:06:32.159] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004285)  
and physical cues.

**[04:06:34.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004286)  
And nandyan din po ang AI-assisted open source intelligence, meaning kung nagkataon po na

**[04:06:41.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004287)  
let's say 15, 20 years ago, on Facebook, meron po kayong nasabi, which would

**[04:06:47.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004288)  
be the system would read as amounting to or gearing towards terrorism.

**[04:06:54.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004289)  
Mababasa rin po yan, Your Honor.

**[04:06:56.359] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004290)  
Ang target po nito, Your Honor, is to increase, expand the borders, meaning hindi na lang po

**[04:07:04.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004291)  
border natin o physical border po ng Pilipinas ang babantayan.

**[04:07:09.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004292)  
Na-expand po natin yan pagating sa ibang bansa.

**[04:07:11.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004293)  
Ngayon po, kung ang isang pasahero nagsusubok pumunta ng Pilipinas, based po sa...

**[04:07:17.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004294)  
pagkuhan niya ng ticket, pagkuhan niya ng kanyang passenger reference number, mariread

**[04:07:23.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004295)  
po ito, and then even before boarding ng aeroplano, kung sa tingin po ng sistema na hindi po siya

**[04:07:32.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004296)  
dapat sumakay o pumasok ng Pilipinas, ibabato po yung information na po na yun in real time

**[04:07:39.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004297)  
sa immigration officers po natin dito sa Pilipinas.

**[04:07:43.119] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004298)  
So, that would also...

**[04:07:46.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004299)  
down to the benefit of the airlines, kasi hindi na po sila gagastos para pabalikin pa po ang isang tao

**[04:07:51.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004300)  
na hindi naman po pa papasukin dito sa ating bansa.

**[04:07:54.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004301)  
So, yan po ang features po.

**[04:07:56.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004302)  
And then, Your Honor, we also have to take into account that the system being resorted to,

**[04:08:03.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004303)  
or systems being resorted to, schemes, if I may call it that, resorted to by terrorists, criminals, are ever evolving.

**[04:08:13.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004304)  
So, itong sistema po na ito...

**[04:08:15.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004305)  
aims to combat, or help, or be a tool to help our immigration officers in combating human trafficking,

**[04:08:23.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004306)  
smuggling, drug and weapon smuggling, terrorism, money laundering, tax evasion, and organized crime.

**[04:08:30.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004307)  
Yan po. Yan po yung sistema, Your Honor.

**[04:08:32.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004308)  
Ano na-propose po yung Secure Reports Limited?

**[04:08:36.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004309)  
That's correct, Your Honor. Secure Reports Limited.

**[04:08:38.319] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004310)  
Sino pong mayari nito? Porin or Pilipino?

**[04:08:41.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004311)  
Ano po? Ang Secure Report po is a company incorporated in the United States.

**[04:08:45.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004312)  
It's way back in 2000. Hindi po Philippine Corporation po yan.

**[04:08:50.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004313)  
They gave an unsolicited proposal.

**[04:08:53.159] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004314)  
Two years in the making na po, Your Honor, ang pag-review po ng iba't-iba ahensya ng gobyerno dito sa proposal po nila.

**[04:09:01.279] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004315)  
It went through the PPP Center, it went to the DOJ, it went through the Bureau of Immigration.

**[04:09:10.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004316)  
So, dalawang taon na po, Your Honor, na...

**[04:09:12.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004317)  
So, but...

**[04:09:14.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004318)  
Do you mean that...

**[04:09:15.319] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004319)  
Your Honor, a sovereign company will have an access to the data of...

**[04:09:20.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004320)  
Your Honor, a sovereign function at control po dito sa datos po na ito is retained by the Philippine government.

**[04:09:27.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004321)  
But how about the servers?

**[04:09:31.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004322)  
Tayo po may control dyan, Your Honor.

**[04:09:33.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004323)  
They only provide the tools and teach our people how to operate those.

**[04:09:37.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004324)  
But ultimately, ang control po...

**[04:09:40.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004325)  
Initially, they will be accessing these datas.

**[04:09:44.159] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004326)  
Yes.

**[04:09:45.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004327)  
They will help us provide...

**[04:09:47.279] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004328)  
They will help provide the tools...

**[04:09:48.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004329)  
Initially.

**[04:09:49.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004330)  
Yes or no?

**[04:09:52.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004331)  
As far as I know, Your Honor, no.

**[04:09:55.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004332)  
They will not have control over the data.

**[04:09:58.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004333)  
They will have no control...

**[04:09:59.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004334)  
They will have no control over the data, Your Honor.

**[04:10:01.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004335)  
Okay.

**[04:10:02.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004336)  
Control is maintained by us.

**[04:10:02.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004337)  
Because I filed a resolution to investigate this particular contract.

**[04:10:08.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004338)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:10:08.720] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004339)  
Aside from the...

**[04:10:09.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004340)  
Yes.

**[04:10:10.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004341)  
Aside from the...

**[04:10:11.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004342)  
Kasi po, diba, yung tinanggal nga ng gobyerno, yung tinatanggal ngayon ng gobyerno, yung...

**[04:10:16.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004343)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:10:16.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004344)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:10:16.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004345)  
So, parang ano, parang yung policy decision ng presidente ay tanggalan ng bawasan yung gastos ng mga pasahero.

**[04:10:29.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004346)  
Pero ito dadagdagan.

**[04:10:31.119] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004347)  
Opo, Your Honor.

**[04:10:31.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004348)  
Your Honor, ito po kasi, hindi po ito form ng tax, Your Honor.

**[04:10:34.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004349)  
Ito po ay service fee.

**[04:10:36.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004350)  
So, hindi naman...

**[04:10:37.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004351)  
Yes, but that's the function of government.

**[04:10:39.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004352)  
That's why we are collecting taxes, diba, from our people to provide services.

**[04:10:44.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004353)  
And that's...

**[04:10:46.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004354)  
Immigration service yan, yung...

**[04:10:48.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004355)  
Yes, Your Honor, we...

**[04:10:50.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004356)  
Cyber security.

**[04:10:51.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004357)  
Yes, po.

**[04:10:51.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004358)  
So, ang concern ko po dito, parang magkakontra yung policy.

**[04:10:57.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004359)  
In fact, dito po sa travel tax, exempted na yung mga OFWs.

**[04:11:04.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004360)  
Pero dito, kasama.

**[04:11:07.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004361)  
So, yung mga OFWs natin na nagdadala ng dolyar dito sa ating bansa, dadagdagan pa natin ng gastos.

**[04:11:14.359] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004362)  
Samantalang, yung revenue na nare-raise natin sa kanila, eh, pwede nating gastosin for this purpose.

**[04:11:21.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004363)  
We understand, Your Honor, and we fully agree po with your position.

**[04:11:25.159] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004364)  
As I've stated, this is a secure...

**[04:11:27.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004365)  
Ano po?

**[04:11:27.479] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004366)  
Service fee po ito, Your Honor.

**[04:11:29.479] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004367)  
Meaning, hindi po namin pwede ipasa sa madlang bayan, taong bayan, sa ordinary taxpayer po, ang pag-gastos po dito.

**[04:11:37.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004368)  
Ang gagastos po dito ay ang mga taong nagka-travel.

**[04:11:40.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004369)  
Hindi po lahat ng...

**[04:11:40.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004370)  
Pag-Ws.

**[04:11:42.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004371)  
Your Honor, admittedly po, sa kanila po, ang mga...

**[04:11:44.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004372)  
Ang mga pung-ticket, isasama po ang gastosin dito.

**[04:11:46.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004373)  
Kaya nga, kaya nga.

**[04:11:47.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004374)  
Your Honor, but if I may continue, Your Honor.

**[04:11:49.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004375)  
Your Honor, ang gastosin po pagdating sa ticket, hindi po sinasagot po yan ng OFWs.

**[04:11:55.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004376)  
Sagot po yan ng mga employers nila.

**[04:11:57.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004377)  
Ang employers po, under an executive order, and under various agreements with other countries,

**[04:12:04.119] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004378)  
ang sumasagot po yan ng gastosin po sa pamasahe, at including na po, kung sakali ma-approvean po ito,

**[04:12:11.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004379)  
yung service fee po na ito, ay sasagotin ng...

**[04:12:14.319] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004380)  
So hindi OFWs po natin ang sasagot.

**[04:12:22.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004381)  
Ito po yung mga kumpanyang kukuha po sa kanila ang sasagot dito.

**[04:12:25.319] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004382)  
And if I may, Your Honor, ito pong sistema po na ito, ginagamit na po yan worldwide.

**[04:12:31.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004383)  
Hindi lang po natin siguro napapansin, pero ang ibang bansa po, nilalagay po yan, nakasama ng gastosin sa mga ticket po.

**[04:12:37.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004384)  
As I've stated earlier, Your Honor, ang Australia po, charges about 2,600...

**[04:12:42.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004385)  
Sir, you said Hong Kong is doing this.

**[04:12:44.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004386)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:12:45.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004387)  
I've been traveling to Hong Kong for quite some time, pero wala akong nakikita ng gano'n.

**[04:12:51.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004388)  
Baka po sa breakdown, makikita po natin, Your Honor.

**[04:12:54.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004389)  
Yan po, Hong Kong charges 1,800, Japan charges 1,200, Singapore charges 2,400, Australia charges 2,600.

**[04:13:05.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004390)  
We charge 240 pesos. So ganyan po yung comparison po, Your Honor.

**[04:13:10.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004391)  
The total project cost is 10,740,000.

**[04:13:15.479] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004392)  
Your Honor, for the first five years po yan, 10.7 billion, Your Honor.

**[04:13:19.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004393)  
But ang security report po promised a technology maintenance upgrade po.

**[04:13:26.279] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004394)  
Upgrade not only of the systems, but also of the training for people every four years over the 20-year period.

**[04:13:33.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004395)  
Kasama po dito sa 10 billion?

**[04:13:34.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004396)  
Hindi po, Your Honor.

**[04:13:35.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004397)  
So another increase?

**[04:13:37.159] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004398)  
Hindi po, Your Honor.

**[04:13:38.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004399)  
Ang commitment po nila is during the lifetime of the...

**[04:13:44.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004400)  
The agreement sa 20 years po na yan, ganyan lang po naka napag-usapan po namin na charges nila.

**[04:13:51.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004401)  
Ano po ang capex nila for the whole project?

**[04:13:53.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004402)  
For the whole project po, over the 20-year period, so 10.7 initially, additional, I think, 52.5 billion over the 20-year period po.

**[04:14:02.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004403)  
So 62 point...

**[04:14:03.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004404)  
It is in the proposed contract?

**[04:14:07.479] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004405)  
It is. Everything is under the proposed contract and under review by all pertinent government agencies.

**[04:14:14.239] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004406)  
The total estimated collection is about 202 billion.

**[04:14:21.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004407)  
Probably, Your Honor.

**[04:14:22.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004408)  
And the share of the private corporation is 192 billion.

**[04:14:25.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004409)  
Ang breakdown po, if I may, Your Honor, is per kada apat na dolyar po, 380 goes to the proponent, 20 cents goes to the Philippine government.

**[04:14:34.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004410)  
5%.

**[04:14:35.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004411)  
5% share.

**[04:14:38.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004412)  
Yes.

**[04:14:38.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004413)  
So this is about 10 billion.

**[04:14:40.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004414)  
Tama po, 5%.

**[04:14:41.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004415)  
95% of the collections will go to the private.

**[04:14:44.279] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004416)  
A provider.

**[04:14:45.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004417)  
Yes.

**[04:14:46.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004418)  
And only 5%.

**[04:14:49.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004419)  
To the government.

**[04:14:50.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004420)  
Sir, why are you still giving this to the government?

**[04:14:54.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004421)  
Sorry, sir.

**[04:14:54.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004422)  
Sorry, Your Honor.

**[04:14:55.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004423)  
This is your service, immigration, to our people.

**[04:14:57.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004424)  
Why are you still giving this to the government?

**[04:14:58.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004425)  
It will fund the projects of the Bureau of Immigration.

**[04:15:04.359] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004426)  
And yes, sir.

**[04:15:06.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004427)  
Immigration, projects of immigration for this?

**[04:15:10.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004428)  
Will also return to the benefit of the government, Your Honor.

**[04:15:13.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004429)  
Why would you fund another project out of this particular project?

**[04:15:19.479] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004430)  
But this will go to the National Treasury, Your Honor.

**[04:15:22.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004431)  
That's it.

**[04:15:23.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004432)  
And then, since we are the agency...

**[04:15:27.319] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004433)  
So this is still a business of the government?

**[04:15:30.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004434)  
There's a profit.

**[04:15:32.279] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004435)  
The service is what the business does.

**[04:15:35.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004436)  
No, Your Honor.

**[04:15:37.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004437)  
We are charging...

**[04:15:38.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004438)  
It's 5% profit.

**[04:15:40.319] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004439)  
It's not...

**[04:15:41.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004440)  
It's the share between...

**[04:15:43.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004441)  
Private proponent and the government, Your Honor.

**[04:15:46.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004442)  
Is this a part of the Swiss Challenge?

**[04:15:49.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004443)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:15:50.319] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004444)  
There was a comparative challenge, which is the Swiss Challenge, which...

**[04:15:53.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004445)  
Who gave the comparative challenge?

**[04:15:58.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004446)  
We sent out notices, Your Honor.

**[04:16:01.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004447)  
But no one came?

**[04:16:02.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004448)  
No one challenged, Your Honor.

**[04:16:03.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004449)  
Usually, that's the case.

**[04:16:05.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004450)  
Usually, that's the case.

**[04:16:06.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004451)  
Anyway, can we give a copy before the plenary of the proposed contract?

**[04:16:13.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004452)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:16:13.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004453)  
And the terms of reference and the proposal of the company and the resolution of your PBAC, the bidding committee.

**[04:16:31.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004454)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:16:31.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004455)  
We will provide all the necessary documents to you.

**[04:16:34.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004456)  
Before the plenary?

**[04:16:37.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004457)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:16:37.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004458)  
From the PPP, DOJ, Bureau of Immigration.

**[04:16:42.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004459)  
All of that.

**[04:16:43.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004460)  
Your Honor, we will provide it to you as requested, Your Honor.

**[04:16:47.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004461)  
Another topic.

**[04:16:50.720] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004462)  
Last question.

**[04:16:51.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004463)  
My last question.

**[04:16:52.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004464)  
Thank you.

**[04:16:53.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004465)  
Still on immigration.

**[04:16:55.399] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004466)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:16:55.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004467)  
There are a lot of issues with you.

**[04:16:57.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004468)  
Joseph C. has been in jail for a year.

**[04:16:59.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004469)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:17:01.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004470)  
What is the case of Joseph C.?

**[04:17:03.199] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004471)  
Joseph C., Your Honor, is a fake Filipino, Your Honor.

**[04:17:08.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004472)  
Yes.

**[04:17:09.359] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004473)  
Maybe around 100,000 here in the Philippines.

**[04:17:12.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004474)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:17:14.239] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004475)  
Maybe around 100,000 here in the Philippines.

**[04:17:15.119] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004476)  
100,000.

**[04:17:15.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004477)  
If you arrest all the big businessmen, our economy will collapse.

**[04:17:21.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004478)  
Your Honor, based on intelligence information that we gathered, Joseph C. is attending the communist party meetings in China.

**[04:17:31.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004479)  
Well, I asked during the budget hearing last year, I asked Secretary Año kung spy si Joseph C.

**[04:17:38.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004480)  
Sabi niya sa akin, negosyante lang 'yan. Wala kaming information.

**[04:17:43.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004481)  
Your Honor, ang pinaglalaban din po namin...

**[04:17:46.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004482)  
What is your basis for saying that?

**[04:17:48.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004483)  
Your Honor, intelligence information from government agencies.

**[04:17:52.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004484)  
From?

**[04:17:52.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004485)  
From what government agency?

**[04:17:53.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004486)  
From?

**[04:17:54.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004487)  
What government agency?

**[04:17:56.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004488)  
Because I talked to Secretary Año, tinanong ko.

**[04:17:59.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004489)  
Kilala ko kasi, matagal na si...

**[04:18:00.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004490)  
Siguro mga 15 years ko nang kilala si Joseph C.

**[04:18:03.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004491)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:18:04.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004492)  
So kilala ko naman na negosyante.

**[04:18:07.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004493)  
Nagbabayad naman ng buwis dito sa Pilipinas.

**[04:18:12.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004494)  
The information I can relate to you after this meeting, Your Honor.

**[04:18:17.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004495)  
What agency?

**[04:18:19.439] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004496)  
I can relate it to you, Your Honor.

**[04:18:21.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004497)  
Alam ko lang na agency na nagtatanong diyan, Senate, eh.

**[04:18:25.159] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004498)  
Hindi po, Your Honor. Hindi po Senate.

**[04:18:28.239] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004499)  
But ang amin naman pong, Your Honor, pinaglalaban din pagdating naman po sa fake Filipinos

**[04:18:33.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004500)  
is 'yung sistema natin ng pag-issue ng mga documents na pinanghahawakan po natin bilang Pilipino,

**[04:18:43.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004501)  
like 'yung pasaporte, 'yung mga birth certificates na nababayaran na po, Your Honor.

**[04:18:48.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004502)  
Alam niyo po, I've been talking to the Filipino-Chinese Chamber of Commerce, the officers.

**[04:18:55.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004503)  
In fact, I filed a proposal to amend the immigration law.

**[04:19:00.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004504)  
Kasi po mahigit, baka 200,000 'yung mga illegal aliens dito,

**[04:19:07.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004505)  
pero mostly Chinese.

**[04:19:09.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004506)  
Pumapasok po sila dito using fake documents,

**[04:19:12.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004507)  
marriage certificates, birth certificates.

**[04:19:14.239] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004508)  
So nagpo-provide din 'yung mga agencies natin.

**[04:19:16.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004509)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:19:17.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004510)  
They facilitate for certain fees.

**[04:19:20.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004511)  
At ang gusto nila talaga, kung at least mga seven, ten years na sila dito,

**[04:19:25.479] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004512)  
wala naman silang criminal record, wala naman silang...

**[04:19:29.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004513)  
and they're willing to pay ten million.

**[04:19:31.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004514)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:19:32.399] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004515)  
And that will amount to billions of revenue,

**[04:19:36.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004516)  
kaysa daw po tuwing Pasko, dinadaanan sila ng mga immigration agents diyan sa Binondo

**[04:19:42.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004517)  
at hinihingan sila.

**[04:19:44.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004518)  
Eh mag-one-time legitimize na nila 'yung kanilang status.

**[04:19:51.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004519)  
Alam niyo, ang masama dito, may pinag-interesan 'yung property nung isang Chinese,

**[04:19:57.040] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004520)  
o, huhulihin.

**[04:19:59.119] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004521)  
'Di ba? Ganoon ang mangyayari.

**[04:20:01.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004522)  
Ganoon ang mangyayari.

**[04:20:04.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004523)  
Bakit hindi natin i-legitimize?

**[04:20:06.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004524)  
'Yung ngang ibang mga Chinese, 'yung mga taipans dito, mga tycoons,

**[04:20:11.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004525)  
they are more Filipino than

**[04:20:12.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004526)  
many of us

**[04:20:14.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004527)  
in terms of contributing to the national economy.

**[04:20:19.199] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004528)  
Bakit hindi natin ayusin ito?

**[04:20:20.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004529)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:20:21.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004530)  
Maybe Secretary Bida can...

**[04:20:23.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004531)  
Yes.

**[04:20:26.279] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004532)  
Yes.

**[04:20:27.319] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004533)  
Actually, we have a pending for the amendment of the Immigration Act.

**[04:20:32.159] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004534)  
So that their additional powers can enter here.

**[04:20:36.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004535)  
Additional powers to address this.

**[04:20:37.840] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004536)  
It also reached us that we have one...

**[04:20:42.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004537)  
It also reached us that we have one municipality that manufactures birth certificates, right?

**[04:20:51.119] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004538)  
Yes.

**[04:20:51.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004539)  
In Mineralo.

**[04:20:52.119] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004540)  
Yes.

**[04:20:52.840] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004541)  
These are the things that, when we see it,

**[04:20:56.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004542)  
for moving forward, I completely agree with you,

**[04:20:59.279] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004543)  
so that there won't be opportunities for enterprising so-called public servants.

**[04:21:08.399] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004544)  
Yes.

**[04:21:09.760] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004545)  
Because we should send a message that...

**[04:21:12.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004546)  
that there should be a cut-clear criteria for us to look at this,

**[04:21:18.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004547)  
whether you're qualified or not.

**[04:21:19.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004548)  
Then, you enter here.

**[04:21:21.119] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004549)  
When you enter, then we're able.

**[04:21:23.760] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004550)  
Of course, a consequential benefit would be collection of the proper fees.

**[04:21:28.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004551)  
Alam niyo, sa Caloocan, motorcycle...

**[04:21:30.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004552)  
Sa district ko po, Grace Park, Motorcycle Capital of Luzon.

**[04:21:36.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004553)  
At karamihan po ng mga Chinese businessman doon, 'yung mga maliliit na businessman,

**[04:21:40.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004554)  
nagtitinda ng motor parts.

**[04:21:42.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004555)  
Matagal na po sila diyan, pero talagang illegal po 'yung entry nila.

**[04:21:48.159] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004556)  
Mga fake documents.

**[04:21:51.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004557)  
At dahil po dito sa nangyayaring ito, marami pong umaalis, eh.

**[04:21:55.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004558)  
Umaalis na.

**[04:21:55.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004559)  
May mga asawa po 'yan na Pilipina

**[04:22:00.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004560)  
na minsan naiiwan na nila.

**[04:22:03.119] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004561)  
At kung aalis po lahat 'yung mga illegals doon sa lugar namin,

**[04:22:09.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004562)  
babagsak talaga 'yung ekonomiya ng lugar namin.

**[04:22:12.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004563)  
Kaya nga po siguro, kung ayos naman, may NBI clearance,

**[04:22:19.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004564)  
mayroong national security clearance,

**[04:22:21.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004565)  
wala naman pong mga kaso,

**[04:22:23.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004566)  
at matagal na sila dito,

**[04:22:26.119] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004567)  
eh baka puwede na nating i-legitimize 'yung kanilang stay.

**[04:22:29.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004568)  
Katulad din naman 'yan ng mga Pilipino sa America,

**[04:22:33.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004569)  
mga TNT na nale-legitimize.

**[04:22:37.439] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004570)  
At hindi naman natin gawin 'yan dito sa mga kapitbahay natin,

**[04:22:42.279] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004571)  
na Filipino-Chinese.

**[04:22:45.319] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004572)  
Maybe the DOJ can work on that.

**[04:22:47.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004573)  
With your permission, Your Honor,

**[04:22:49.760] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004574)  
we will properly come up with a roadmap on this.

**[04:22:53.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004575)  
Because so that it won't go back and forth.

**[04:22:55.800] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004576)  
Because just like what I've mentioned,

**[04:22:58.760] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004577)  
we don't want to...

**[04:22:59.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004578)  
Because we have a lot of work to do.

**[04:23:01.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004579)  
I want it to be over.

**[04:23:03.359] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004580)  
So that this problem will be over.

**[04:23:05.479] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004581)  
With your permission, with permission of everyone,

**[04:23:08.080] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004582)  
we will duly consult you.

**[04:23:10.880] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004583)  
Of course, we need your...

**[04:23:11.960] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004584)  
We need your learnings on the ground.

**[04:23:14.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004585)  
We need your know-how.

**[04:23:15.600] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004586)  
You need to be more aware of the movements of those...

**[04:23:18.439] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004587)  
You said it, right?

**[04:23:20.319] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004588)  
Those who have become friends.

**[04:23:21.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004589)  
We're happy with the development of our economy.

**[04:23:24.800] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004590)  
So I'll be tasking...

**[04:23:27.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004591)  
Yes. Thank you.

**[04:23:28.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004592)  
Together with the BI,

**[04:23:30.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004593)  
once and for all, of course,

**[04:23:32.840] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004594)  
we need the former commissioner,

**[04:23:34.520] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004595)  
the wisdom of the former commissioner.

**[04:23:35.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004596)  
I consulted also...

**[04:23:36.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004597)  
So that we can see.

**[04:23:39.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004598)  
So that it won't go back and forth.

**[04:23:41.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004601)  
And we can raise a lot of revenue

**[04:23:45.640] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004602)  
from these people.

**[04:23:48.840] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004603)  
Yes. Everything, Your Honor,

**[04:23:49.920] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004604)  
within the legal framework.

**[04:23:51.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004605)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:23:51.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004606)  
Thank you.

**[04:23:52.119] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004607)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[04:23:53.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004608)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:23:56.439] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004609)  
Thank you very much. So iyon.

**[04:23:57.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-004610)  
Thank you, Cong. Egay Erice.

**[04:24:39.324] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004629)

**[04:24:42.326] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004630)  
Yung pong

**[04:24:43.327] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004631)

**[04:24:44.367] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004632)  
ng POGO, nabaklas na

**[04:24:46.569] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004633)  
po iyon, Your Honor.

**[04:24:49.791] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004634)  
As far as we know,

**[04:24:51.652] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004635)  
wala na pong malalaking grupo. Ngayon

**[04:24:54.874] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004636)  
po, pagdating sa mga

**[04:24:56.254] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004637)

**[04:24:58.135] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004638)  
there's a probability, Your Honor—there's

**[04:24:59.696] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004639)  
a possibility—but we

**[04:25:00.677] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004640)  
are always going after them once

**[04:25:02.918] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004641)  
we gather the intelligence information which

**[04:25:05.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004642)  
would warrant us to

**[04:25:07.941] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004643)  
detain them and

**[04:25:08.742] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004644)  
to deport them accordingly.

**[04:25:10.843] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004645)  
We do that, Your Honor.

**[04:25:12.344] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004646)  
Mr. Immigration Commissioner, kilala po ba ninyo si Mr. Kim Wong?

**[04:25:20.989] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004647)  
Sino po si Mr. Wong, sir?

**[04:25:23.791] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004648)  
Kasi ho pag naggo-golf ako sa Philippine Navy, pag ang kalaban ko'y mga abogadong naglilingkod din sa

**[04:25:32.576] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004649)  
bayan, sabi nila, ‘Aha, magaling ka na mag-golf dahil kaibigan mo siguro si Kim

**[04:25:38.819] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004650)  
Wong.’ Kaya nagtataka

**[04:25:39.700] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004651)  
ako. Iyan sa Navy, hindi sa ibang golf courses.

**[04:25:41.781] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004652)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:25:42.862] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004653)  
So hindi mo siya kilala, o kilala mo siya?

**[04:25:45.143] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004654)

**[04:25:53.488] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004655)  
Nagkaroon ba ng pagkakataon na magkasama kayo?

**[04:25:57.150] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004656)  
No, Your Honor, hindi po kami magkasama.

**[04:26:00.292] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004657)  
Probably in social functions, but not official.

**[04:26:04.675] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004658)  
Mabuti naman.

**[04:26:05.255] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004659)  
Are you aware na may POGO pa ring nag-e-exist sa Barangay Multinational?

**[04:26:12.379] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004660)

**[04:26:24.887] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004661)

**[04:26:31.771] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004662)

**[04:26:42.897] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004663)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:26:44.098] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004664)  
So ngayon, may decrease sa inyong budget request. Dapat matulungan iyan because we need to strengthen especially

**[04:26:56.946] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004665)

**[04:27:08.913] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004666)  
human trafficking issues, at, of

**[04:27:12.415] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004667)  
course, sa undesirable aliens na unti-unting pumapasok sa ating bansa?

**[04:27:14.916] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004668)  
Admittedly, Your Honor, yung decrease po ng budget namin would affect our

**[04:27:22.461] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004669)  
operations sa lahat. Ngayon po, pagdating sa ni-request ko po kanina, kung maaari pong sa aming border offices—border control offices—and then sa main office po, i-computerize po.

**[04:27:30.946] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004670)  
Malaking tulong po iyan sa aming efforts. Ngayon po, pagdating naman po

**[04:27:34.448] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004671)  
sa computerization, iyan din po ang aming habol, and automation iyan

**[04:27:39.531] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004672)  
din po ang habol namin. And

**[04:27:41.552] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004673)  
then nabanggit po kanina ni Congressman Erice yung tungkol po doon sa aming advance passenger information system. Malaking

**[04:27:49.497] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004674)  
magiging tulong po iyon, Your Honor, sa pag-expand po ng ating borders and for us to have a holistic approach,

**[04:27:57.221] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004675)  
intelligence-driven approach, pagdating ho sa border control, Your Honor.

**[04:28:01.624] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004676)  
Maraming salamat doon sa inyong magandang kasagutan.

**[04:28:05.326] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004677)

**[04:28:12.530] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004678)

**[04:28:17.473] Rep. Amado Carlos A. Bolilia IV** (asr-004680)
