**[04:59:57.988] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005629)  
Is this program different from what Congressman Erice mentioned a while ago?

**[05:00:04.373] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005630)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[05:00:05.063] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005631)  
The E-Gates project is different from the Enhanced Border Control System

**[05:00:09.856] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005632)  
that is being proposed under the Public-Private Partnership arrangement.

**[05:00:16.224] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005633)  
May we request everyone to please settle down?

**[05:00:21.680] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005634)  
So can you make the distinction, Commissioner?

**[05:00:23.803] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005635)  
Your Honor, with the E-Gates project,

**[05:00:26.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005636)  
we aim to integrate that with the CAIS project.

**[05:00:31.745] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005637)  
The CAIS project is a more holistic approach

**[05:00:34.398] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005638)  
because it does not only pertain to the E-Gates,

**[05:00:37.051] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005639)  
but also to our integration with the systems of Interpol,

**[05:00:41.137] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005640)  
as well as the Advanced Passenger Information System,

**[05:00:44.002] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005641)  
and biometrics.

**[05:00:46.142] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005642)  
We also can log in to a billion facial images.

**[05:00:52.634] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005645)  
And we also have, under the CAIS program,

**[05:00:55.976] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005646)  
the reactive screening based on facial contortions

**[05:01:02.910] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005647)  
or in reaction to certain forms of questioning.

**[05:01:06.430] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005648)  
And we can also access open source intelligence.

**[05:01:09.808] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005649)  
Not only can we access the 194 countries that are members of Interpol,

**[05:01:13.876] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005650)  
their databases,

**[05:01:14.584] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005651)  
we can access that.

**[05:01:16.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005652)  
But also, we can access,

**[05:01:19.165] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005653)  
we can access any information from open sources,

**[05:01:22.189] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005654)  
meaning over the internet.

**[05:01:24.206] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005655)  
Facebook, that can be accessed, Your Honor.

**[05:01:26.841] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005656)  
So this is under the PPP program?

**[05:01:28.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005657)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[05:01:29.583] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005658)  
Thank you, Commissioner Viado.

**[05:01:31.917] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-005659)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[05:02:36.141] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005676)  
Where does the 80% of the collection go?

**[05:02:38.700] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005677)  
How about the balance of 80%?

**[05:02:40.819] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005678)  
Where does it go?

**[05:02:41.518] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005679)  
Your Honor, the 80% goes directly to the National Treasury,

**[05:02:45.656] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005680)  
and we retain 20% subject to the approval of the DBM on the releases of the 20%.

**[05:02:52.392] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005681)  
So the 20% in the amount of 1.7,

**[05:02:57.830] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005682)  
can we use that amount?

**[05:03:00.308] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005683)  
To pay for titling of free patents issued by the DENR?

**[05:03:06.185] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005684)  
Your Honor, the 1.7 billion is 15% only out of the 20% it represents.

**[05:03:18.978] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005685)  
Your Honor, we believe that it can be used for it.

**[05:03:52.240] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005686)  
The coverage of the special account refers to MOOE accounts and capital outlay.

**[05:03:58.436] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005687)  
Because, Administrator, there are thousands of unclaimed free patents before the Registry of Deeds all over the Philippines.

**[05:04:06.672] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005688)  
So, probably, can you account for the free patents, unclaimed free patents, in all the Registry of Deeds all over the Philippines?

**[05:04:15.707] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005689)  
Yes, Your Honor, you are correct.

**[05:04:17.106] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005690)  
We have made that observation in our visits to the different Registers of Deeds.

**[05:04:21.724] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005691)  
We have observed several, if not thousands, of unregistered patents previously.

**[05:04:27.860] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005692)  
This was covered by an agreement with the DENR, which paid for the registration of the patents.

**[05:04:38.615] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005693)  
Subsequently, they removed that funding.

**[05:04:42.612] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005694)  
So, perhaps with the approval of the DBM, we can utilize it in registering some of these, or all the remaining patents issued.

**[05:04:54.966] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005695)  
To be taken from the special account?

**[05:04:57.844] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005696)  
Yes, Your Honor, if we can maximize the utilization to 20%.

**[05:05:02.581] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005697)  
Thank you.

**[05:05:03.661] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005698)  
So, on the other matter, Mr. Administrator, what is the legal responsibility of the

**[05:05:09.678] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005699)  
Registry of Deeds and the LRA for the safekeeping maintenance of Certificate of Titles?

**[05:05:15.714] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005700)  
Please, quiet down.

**[05:05:16.614] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005701)  
Your Honour, under PD 1529, the function of the main function of the Registers of Deeds is the main repository of all the

**[05:05:32.725] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005702)  
titles and supporting documents.

**[05:05:34.844] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005703)  
So it's a registry of deeds?

**[05:05:36.523] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005704)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[05:05:37.502] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005705)  
Is the registry of deeds required to prepare an inventory of titles which have been lost,

**[05:05:44.958] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005706)  
destroyed, or rendered unusable by deterioration?

**[05:05:48.436] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005707)  
Your Honor, under PD 1529, the RDs are obliged to conduct an inventory annually.

**[05:05:56.372] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005708)  
I would suppose that would also include all titles, whether it has been lost or damaged in their custody.

**[05:06:05.227] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005709)  
In case of lost administrator of title, who has the legal responsibility for judicial reconstitution?

**[05:06:14.322] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005710)  
Your Honor, unfortunately, when the title is lost, the RD copy is lost.

**[05:06:19.420] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005711)  
Under RA 26, as amended by RA 6732,

**[05:06:25.176] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005712)  
the responsibility is given to the owner of the title.

**[05:06:30.633] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005713)  
The responsibility was taken away from the RD.

**[05:06:34.871] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005714)  
Previously, the RD was allowed to initiate reconstitution cases,

**[05:06:39.828] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005715)  
but because of the passage of the law and because of a Supreme Court decision involving the RD of Malabon or Mandaluyong,

**[05:06:48.164] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005716)  
the Supreme Court said that the RDs cannot undertake the filing.

**[05:06:55.160] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005717)  
Under the reconstitution case.

**[05:06:57.679] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005718)  
Sir Administrator, is there no exception to the rule under RA 6732?

**[05:07:03.695] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005719)  
Under RA 26, as amended by RA 6732,

**[05:07:09.772] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005720)  
administrative reconstitution can be undertaken by the LRA

**[05:07:14.330] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005721)  
if the Administrator has evaluated that at least 10% of the titles in the RD has been lost,

**[05:07:25.144] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005722)  
But in no case should it be less than 500 titles.

**[05:07:30.601] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005723)  
As you mentioned earlier, Mr. Administrator, you said the Registry of Deeds are required or mandated to have an inventory of lost titles.

**[05:07:41.355] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005724)  
So based on the submission, did the Registry of Deeds make the submission?

**[05:07:47.392] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005725)  
They are required to conduct an inventory which should also include all lost titles.

**[05:07:51.030] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005726)  
Some of the RDs have complied with the provisions of the law, and we are closely monitoring also the other RDs, Your Honor.

**[05:08:00.245] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005727)  
Let's say in case the administrative threshold is not met, what can your office do to assist those lost titles, individual lost titles,

**[05:08:18.295] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005728)  
considering that the loss?

**[05:08:21.013] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005729)  
Loss was in the Registry of Deeds, am I not correct?

**[05:08:26.390] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005730)  
Your Honor, it is quite unfortunate that the burden is given to the registered owner in undertaking the registration or reconstitution of lost titles.

**[05:08:37.964] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005731)  
And on our part, what we can do is only provide them with the assistance of providing some of the necessary documents that they will need

**[05:08:47.179] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005732)  
in filing the appropriate constitution case with the courts, Your Honor.

**[05:08:50.997] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005733)  
Let's go to Friar Lands.

**[05:08:52.996] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005734)  
In case of loss of Friar Lands or has been lost or destroyed, and the DNR issued subsequent deed of conveyance,

**[05:09:03.990] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005735)  
what will happen to the registration of the deed of conveyance before the Registry of Deeds?

**[05:09:10.986] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005736)  
Thank you, Your Honor, for bringing it up.

**[05:09:12.985] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005737)  
We have several Friar Lands across the country.

**[05:09:16.983] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005738)  
We have the OCDC.

**[05:09:20.961] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005742)  
We have the OCDC, which is supposedly in the custody of the Registers of Deeds.

**[05:09:25.958] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005743)  
And the owner's copy is supposedly with the LMB.

**[05:09:29.956] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005744)  
It is unfortunate that some of the RDs have been destroyed or damaged, which included the titles also.

**[05:09:41.949] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005745)  
And it is also unfortunate that LMB has also been burned.

**[05:09:47.946] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005746)  
Their head office in Binondo was burned.

**[05:09:50.425] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005747)  
So they have also lost some of the owner's copies of these titles.

**[05:09:55.422] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005748)  
But I would like to inform Your Honor that we are currently talking to the LMB,

**[05:10:03.417] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005749)  
since they have lifted the moratorium on the issuance of deeds of conveyance,

**[05:10:08.415] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005750)  
to explore administrative remedies so that we can also help the awardees or claimants of these Friar Lands.

**[05:10:18.409] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005751)  
What administrative remedies?

**[05:10:19.409] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005752)  
What administrative remedies you are referring to on the part of the DNR?

**[05:10:23.407] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005753)  
We will still be discussing with the LMB, Your Honor.

**[05:10:26.405] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005754)  
In fact, we were supposed to have a meeting last week, but because of the weather, it did not permit it.

**[05:10:33.401] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005755)  
But we will be meeting with them soon.

**[05:10:35.400] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005756)  
Perhaps, Your Honor, also with your help, you can also talk to the LMB to meet with us soon on this matter.

**[05:10:46.360] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005757)  
I think the DNR is scheduled.

**[05:10:52.240] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005758)  
We will have a budget hearing on Wednesday.

**[05:10:56.160] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005759)  
Maybe, Administrator, if you can commit to us that you can be present here on Wednesday in the afternoon.

**[05:11:02.878] LRA Administrator Gerardo P. Sirios** (asr-005760)  
I will come, Your Honor.

**[05:11:04.638] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005761)  
Thank you, Mr. Administrator.

**[05:11:15.000] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005762)  
Okay, I will have my third round.

**[05:11:18.440] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005763)  
I defer to Kong Reni.

**[05:11:22.240] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-005764)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[05:11:23.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-005765)  
Thank you, Kong Alan.

**[05:11:24.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-005766)  
Next in line from the minority again, Rep. Renee Co.

**[05:11:30.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005767)  
Thank you very much, Chair.

**[05:11:31.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005768)  
And thank you again for DOJ for continuing to be here.

**[05:11:35.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005769)  
My next questions would be in relation to the supposed closure or nearing closure of the New Bilibid Prison.

**[05:11:45.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005770)  
So it's been on the news that the Bureau of Corrections transferred 15,730 inmates ahead of the closure.

**[05:11:53.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005771)  
Puwede pong matanong, since this is a huge endeavor,

**[05:11:58.558] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005772)  
Logistically, ano pong budgetary provisions?

**[05:12:03.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005773)  
Paano po napondohan ng DOJ 'yung pagpapalipat po ng mga inmates, mass transfer operation, transportation, security, etc. po?

**[05:12:17.280] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005774)  
With the permission of the Secretary, Mr. Chair, the transfer of the PDLs, ginagawa po 'yan yearly.

**[05:12:26.360] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005775)  
And it's with the support of the budget of Bucor using its MOE.

**[05:12:34.480] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005776)  
So pag nag-transfer po ng isang PDL, it costs us about 15,000 to 20,000.

**[05:12:43.240] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005777)  
And that's the way we are slowly transferring ng mga PDL.

**[05:12:52.920] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005778)  
And of course, alam niyo naman po sa New Bilibid Prison,

**[05:12:56.160] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005779)  
na 300% congested siya nung dumating ako diyan,

**[05:13:03.160] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005780)  
and now we have reduced it to almost 200% congestion na lang po.

**[05:13:09.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005781)  
Puwede po bang ma-confirm, Mr. Chair, na may mga PDLs na mata-transfer to, aside from itong penal colony in Zamboanga?

**[05:13:23.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005782)  
Meron daw pong reports na lilipat din po sa Sablayan, Mindoro;

**[05:13:25.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005783)  
Iwahig, Palawan; San Ramon, Zamboanga del Sur;

**[05:13:32.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005784)  
Abuyog, Leyte; at saka po sa Davao del Norte.

**[05:13:35.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005785)  
Tama po ba?

**[05:13:35.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005786)  
So malilipat din galing New Bilibid papunta dito sa ibang mga penal colonies?

**[05:13:40.240] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005787)  
Yes po.

**[05:13:42.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005788)  
Mr. Chair, puwede po bang makakuha ng full list?

**[05:13:45.280] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005789)  
Yes. We will submit to you the number of PDLs transferred for each operating prison and penal farm

**[05:13:52.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005790)  
outside the

**[05:13:54.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005791)  
NBP.

**[05:13:55.958] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005792)  
Okay.

**[05:13:57.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005793)  
'Yung concern ko po, Mr. Chair, ay, well, ang DOJ, ang BuCor, nagsabi that they are observant of the Mandela Rules,

**[05:14:05.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005794)  
the Nelson Mandela Rules, ahead of any legislation, any parang law that would really mandate 'yung pag-observe noon.

**[05:14:13.798] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005795)  
But Rule 59 says that prisoners should be assigned to prisons close to their homes or their places of social rehabilitation.

**[05:14:21.718] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005796)  
So ang concern ko po, 'yung mga lilipat,

**[05:14:24.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005797)  
baka po malayo doon sa kanilang mga pamilya, lalo na kung galing Luzon,

**[05:14:27.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005798)  
tapos ililipat po sa Davao del Norte or sa Zamboanga del Sur, malalayo.

**[05:14:31.638] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005799)  
Meron po bang way para i-ensure na hindi naman ganito—hindi malalayo sa pamilya,

**[05:14:37.958] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005800)  
from legal counsel or even support networks kung saan po sila matatagpuan?

**[05:14:42.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005801)  
Based on our records,

**[05:14:43.920] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005802)  
na records po, yung mga

**[05:14:45.798] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005803)  
binibisita sa Muntinlupa,

**[05:14:48.240] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005804)  
hindi po namin nililipat.

**[05:14:50.600] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005805)  
Pero yung karamihan

**[05:14:51.440] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005806)  
po noon, 20,000

**[05:14:54.000] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005807)  
pa po nandoon sa NBP,

**[05:14:55.760] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005808)  
wala pong bisita.

**[05:14:57.798] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005809)  
Kaya gusto rin po nilang malipat

**[05:14:59.958] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005810)  
dahil alam nila, mas

**[05:15:02.120] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005811)  
maaliwalas, mas secured,

**[05:15:03.200] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005812)  
mas

**[05:15:04.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005813)  
presko ang malilipatan nila

**[05:15:07.878] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005814)  
kesa dito po sila nandito sa

**[05:15:09.760] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005815)  
NBP na nagsisiksikan.

**[05:15:12.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005816)  
At nasertify naman po ba

**[05:15:13.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005817)  
Mr. Chair, na yung mga

**[05:15:15.878] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005818)  
lilipatang penal colonies nila,

**[05:15:18.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005819)  
adequate habitable infrastructure,

**[05:15:20.638] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005820)  
yung nariyan, medical services,

**[05:15:23.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005821)  
may staff, bago po

**[05:15:23.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005822)  
makareceive ng

**[05:15:25.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005823)  
influx ng thousands of inmates

**[05:15:28.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005824)  
din po kung tuloy

**[05:15:29.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005825)  
yung pagtatransfer per year.

**[05:15:32.520] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005826)  
Opo, Mr. Chair,

**[05:15:34.080] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005827)  
tinatanong po namin 'yung magiging

**[05:15:35.400] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005828)  
recipient na mga kolonya

**[05:15:38.000] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005829)  
kung kaya nilang iabsorb.

**[05:15:40.080] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005830)  
At sila naman po ang

**[05:15:41.360] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005831)  
nagsasabi sa amin na

**[05:15:42.600] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005832)  
puwede pa silang mag-absorb.

**[05:15:45.240] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005833)  
Kaya't hindi naman po

**[05:15:47.718] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005834)  
nahihirapan yung mga

**[05:15:49.080] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005835)  
tumatanggap na bagong

**[05:15:51.400] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005836)  
mga prison and penal farms

**[05:15:53.840] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005837)  
outside Metro Manila.

**[05:15:55.878] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005838)  
May kailangan po bang additional items

**[05:15:57.638] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005839)  
or additional budget

**[05:15:59.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005840)  
para po kung anong absorption

**[05:16:01.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005841)  
yung kailangan ay hindi po

**[05:16:03.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005842)  
makompromise yung pagbigay po

**[05:16:05.558] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005843)  
ng mga servisyo ng mga

**[05:16:07.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005844)

**[05:16:09.878] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005845)  
Nandito po ba yun sa proposal?

**[05:16:10.798] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005846)  
ng Bucor?

**[05:16:13.120] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005847)  
Maganda pong tanong yan kasi

**[05:16:14.760] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005848)  
kaya ang importante yung support

**[05:16:16.718] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005849)  
services. Kaya natutuwa

**[05:16:19.320] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005850)  
naman po kami pag lumipat kami

**[05:16:21.280] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005851)  
sa nilipat namin yung mga PDL

**[05:16:23.920] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-005852)
